We evaluate the learnt representations on speaker verification and keyword spotting tasks, showing improved out-of-domain speaker verification performance and evidence that speaker and content information are separated across the learned subspaces.

</details>


### [39] [Fine-tuning LLMs for Passive Depression Severity Estimation from AI Mental Health Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17973)
*Olivier Tieleman,Ziyi Zhu,Ting Su,Samuel J. Bell,Thomas D. Hull,Caitlin A. Stamatis*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用微调后的Qwen3.5大模型，通过分析用户与AI应用的对话文本，实现了对抑郁严重程度的准确预测，从而能够替代传统的自评量表进行持续的被动症状监测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 抑郁症早期检测至关重要，但传统的PHQ-9量表填答率低、存在响应偏差；旨在通过被动方式，直接从AI对话文本中准确预测抑郁严重程度。

Method: 基于Qwen3.5-27B骨干模型，添加回归头进行微调；利用Claude Opus生成伪标签，结合3,111个真实标签扩充至6,283个样本数据进行迭代训练。

Result: 在842名用户的测试集中，MAE为2.6，RMSE为4.0，Pearson相关系数为0.80；在PHQ-9 >= 10的临床阈值下，AUC达到0.91，且在整个严重程度谱系内均表现出良好的性能（AUC > 0.87）。

Conclusion: 通过大语言模型分析对话文本，能够实现无需患者主动填表即可进行的持续抑郁症状监测，这为AI心理健康平台提供了可行且高效的评估方案。

Abstract: Depression is the leading cause of disability worldwide, and early detection of symptom change is essential for timely intervention. Validated instruments such as the Patient Health Questionnaire-9 (PHQ-9) support symptom monitoring at scale, but real-world completion rates are low, introducing response bias and systematic missingness. Passive approaches that infer severity from routinely generated data could close this gap. We address this by predicting PHQ-9 total scores directly from transcripts of conversations between users and an AI mental health application, requiring only conversation text and no additional clinical data. We fine-tune a Qwen3.5-27B backbone with a regression head, augment 3,111 ground-truth labels with pseudolabels generated by a reasoning model (Claude Opus) and iteratively trained intermediate models, for a combined dataset of 6,283 users. On a held-out test set of 842 users, our best model achieves MAE = 2.6, RMSE = 4.0, Pearson r = 0.80, and AUC = 0.91 at the PHQ-9 >= 10 clinical threshold. We also find AUC > 0.87 at every severity threshold from PHQ-9 >= 3 to PHQ-9 >= 24, demonstrating that the model captures depression severity across the full clinical spectrum. This work opens the door to passive, continuous symptom monitoring in AI mental health platforms, without requiring users to complete self-report measures.

</details>


### [40] [VoidPadding: Let [VOID] Handle Padding in Masked Diffusion Language Models so that [EOS] Can Focus on Semantic Termination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17999)
*Chunyu Liu,Zhengyang Fan,Kaisen Yang,Alex Lamb*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对掩码生成语言模型（MDLMs）中 [EOS] 标记混用导致的结尾与填充冲突问题，提出 VoidPadding 方法，通过引入 [VOID] 专门用于填充，有效提升了模型推理效率与生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的掩码生成语言模型（MDLMs）在指令微调时，混用了 [EOS] 标记的语义终止和填充双重职能，这在大块解码（large-block decoding）过程中导致了 [EOS] 溢出等问题，影响了性能。

Method: 引入 [VOID] 标记作为专门的填充符，将 [EOS] 仅保留用于语义终止；在推理阶段，通过 [EOS] 实现提前停止，通过 [VOID] 指导自适应响应画布扩展。

Result: 在 Dream-7B-Instruct 模型上，VoidPadding 将数学推理和代码生成任务的平均分提升了 17.84 个点（优于原版）和 6.95 个点（优于 RainbowPadding），并将平均解码 NFE 降低了 55.7%。

Conclusion: VoidPadding通过引入专门的 [VOID] 标记成功解耦了结尾与填充逻辑，解决了 MDLMs 在大块解码中的 [EOS] 溢出问题，提升了生成质量并显著降低了推理成本。

Abstract: MDLMs generate text by denoising a preallocated masked response canvas, making response-length modeling central to instruction tuning. Existing MDLMs often inherit the autoregressive convention of using repeated \texttt{[EOS]} tokens for padding during instruction tuning, giving \texttt{[EOS]} a dual role as both a semantic terminator and a padding token. We show that this dual role is a root cause of \texttt{[EOS]} overflow under large-block decoding. To decouple these roles, we propose VoidPadding, which introduces \texttt{[VOID]} for padding and reserves \texttt{[EOS]} for termination. During inference, the learned \texttt{[EOS]} signal enables early stopping, while the learned \texttt{[VOID]} signal guides adaptive response canvas expansion. On Dream-7B-Instruct, VoidPadding improves the block-size-averaged four-task mean across mathematical reasoning and code generation benchmarks by \(+17.84\) points over the original model and \(+6.95\) points over RainbowPadding, while reducing decoding NFE by 55.7\% on average. Code is available at https://github.com/Haru-LCY/VoidPadding.

</details>


### [41] [When English Isn't the Best Teacher: Source Language Effects in Cross-Lingual In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18033)
*Fred Philippy,Siwen Guo,Jacques Klein,Tegawendé F. Bissyandé*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过大规模实验证明跨语言上下文学习（ICL）的迁移规律与传统微调不同，并揭示了语言混淆对ICL的影响，从而重构了跨语言ICL的源语言选择策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多语言NLP领域，跨语言迁移的研究多集中于监督微调；随着大模型进入少样本上下文学习（ICL）时代，学界假设微调的迁移规律在ICL中依然适用，但该假设缺乏严谨评估。因此需明确如何为跨语言ICL选择合适的源语言。

Method: 通过包含7个任务、6种模型以及具有类型学多样性语言集的广泛实证研究，并深入分析了生成任务中阻碍跨语言ICL的关键因素——“语言混淆（language confusion）”现象。

Result: 研究发现基于传统微调的预期无法完全适用于ICL范式；识别了语言混淆现象对生成任务的影响，并提出了针对跨语言ICL选择源语言的替代性启发式方法。

Conclusion: 跨语言ICL的性能表现并不完全遵循传统微调研究的规律，研究结果为选择最优源语言提供了新的启发式策略。

Abstract: Cross-lingual transfer in multilingual NLP has been widely explored in supervised fine-tuning contexts, where factors like data availability and linguistic similarity largely determine transfer quality. As the field shifts toward few-shot In-Context Learning (ICL), it is often presumed that insights from fine-tuning carry over unchanged. Yet this assumption has not been rigorously evaluated, leaving open the question of how to choose source languages for cross-lingual ICL. We conduct a broad empirical study of cross-lingual transfer in ICL spanning seven tasks, six models, and a typologically diverse set of languages. We further analyze language confusion, a key obstacle for generative tasks in cross-lingual ICL. Our results show that conventional fine-tuning-based expectations do not consistently apply in the ICL regime and point to alternative heuristics for selecting source languages effectively.

</details>


### [42] [Compositional Skill Routing for LLM Agents: Decompose, Retrieve, and Compose](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18051)
*Xueping Gao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM智能体在处理复杂组合技能任务时的不足，本文提出了SkillWeaver框架及SAD分解策略，通过引入任务感知反馈循环，显著提升了任务分解质量、检索效率及资源利用率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM智能体大多只能执行单一技能，难以处理需要组合多个技能的复杂真实世界任务，且现有的检索和规划方法在面对动态、大规模技能库时存在准确性和效率瓶颈。

Method: 1. 提出了SkillWeaver框架，包含任务分解器、基于FAISS索引的双编码器技能检索器以及依赖感知DAG规划器。
2. 引入了Iterative Skill-Aware Decomposition (SAD)策略：一种检索增强反馈循环机制，用于对齐LLM的分解过程与现有技能库。
3. 构建了CompSkillBench基准数据集，包含300个组合性查询和2209个真实MCP技能。

Result: 1. SAD策略将任务分解准确率从51.0%提升至67.7%（提升32.7%）。
2. 当任务分解粒度正确时，类别检索准确率（CatR@1）从34%提升至41%。
3. SkillWeaver大幅降低了99%以上的上下文窗口消耗，并展现出良好的泛化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究成功构建了SkillWeaver框架，证明了通过“任务感知分解（SAD）”能有效解决复杂任务的技能组合难题。研究表明，任务分解的粒度对检索准确性至关重要，SAD策略显著提升了LLM在复杂场景下的任务处理能力和效率。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external skills -- reusable tool specifications -- but real-world tasks often require composing multiple skills, not just selecting one. We formalize this as the Compositional Skill Routing problem: given a complex user query and a large skill library, decompose the query into atomic sub-tasks, retrieve the appropriate skill for each sub-task, and compose an executable plan. We present SkillWeaver, a decompose-retrieve-compose framework combining an LLM task decomposer, a bi-encoder skill retriever with FAISS indexing, and a dependency-aware DAG planner. To support evaluation, we introduce CompSkillBench, a benchmark of 300 compositional queries over 2,209 real MCP server skills spanning 24 functional categories, sourced from the public MCP ecosystem. Our experiments reveal that task decomposition quality is the primary bottleneck: standard LLM decomposition reaches only 34.2% category recall at the step level. To address this, we propose Iterative Skill-Aware Decomposition (SAD), a retrieval-augmented feedback loop that iteratively aligns decomposition with available skills. SAD improves decomposition accuracy from 51.0% to 67.7% (+32.7%, Wilcoxon p < 10^-6) in a single iteration; DA-conditioned analysis confirms that correct granularity is the prerequisite for effective retrieval (CatR@1 rises from 34% to 41% when DA=1). SkillWeaver reduces context window consumption by over 99%, and transfer experiments confirm generalization (+35.6% relative DA gain even when target categories are absent from the retrieval pool).

</details>


### [43] [ConSA: Controllable Sparsity in Hybrid Attention via Learnable Allocation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18056)
*Yao Chen,Yinqi Yang,Junyuan Shang,Xiangzhao Hao,Simeng Zhang,Yilong Chen,Tingwen Liu,Shuohuan Wang,Dianhai Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了 ConSA 框架，实现了大模型推理中全注意力（FA）与滑窗注意力（SWA）的自动化、可控稀疏度分配，通过数据驱动的学习发现了比传统人工规则更优的非均匀层级分配策略，有效提升了推理效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的混合架构（结合全注意力 FA 和滑窗注意力 SWA）主要依赖人工规则，缺乏对底层注意力行为的深入分析，且分配策略不够灵活，无法兼顾推理效率与模型性能。

Method: 提出 ConSA 框架，采用 L0 正则化学习各注意力单元的 FA/SWA 二值掩码，并利用增广拉格朗日约束实现用户指定的层级或 KV 头级稀疏度目标。

Result: 在 0.6B 和 1.7B 规模模型上的实验表明，ConSA 习得的分配策略显著优于传统启发式规则；KV 头级的分配比层级分配更有效；且学习到的模式普遍表现为底层 SWA、中层集中 FA 的分布。

Conclusion: ConSA 揭示了现有基于规则的方法的局限性，证明了通过学习得到的非均匀 FA/SWA 分配策略（在底层使用 SWA，在中间层集中使用 FA）在效率和性能上均优于传统策略。这一发现为大模型推理架构优化提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Hybrid architectures combining full attention (FA) and sliding-window attention (SWA) are a promising paradigm for efficient LLM inference. However, existing methods typically rely on hand-crafted rules or simple post-hoc heuristics for FA/SWA allocation and offer limited analysis of the attention behaviors underlying these designs. We propose Controllable Sparsity in Hybrid Attention (ConSA), a framework that learns optimal FA/SWA assignment under a user-specified sparsity target. ConSA employs L0 regularization to learn binary masks selecting between FA and SWA for each attention unit, while an augmented Lagrangian constraint enforces the target sparsity at either layer or KV-head granularity. We evaluate ConSA on two LLMs at the 0.6B and 1.7B scales. Learned allocations consistently outperform rule-based baselines, with KV-head-wise allocation yielding clear gains over layer-wise allocation. The learned patterns place SWA in the bottom layers and concentrate FA into contiguous middle-layer blocks, diverging from evenly interleaved patterns in rule-based methods. This structure persists across model scales, sparsity levels, and allocation granularities, revealing a fine-grained spectrum of intrinsic attention behaviors that underlies the learned allocation.

</details>


### [44] [Security and Privacy Prompts in the Wild: What Users Ask LLMs and How LLMs Respond](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18062)
*Hobin Kim,Xiaoyuan Wu,Omer Akgul,Lujo Bauer,Nicolas Christin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该研究分析了用户在真实场景中向LLM咨询安全与隐私问题的行为，发现商业模型虽然回复质量远高于开源模型，但普遍存在响应不一致的问题，增加了用户获取准确安全建议的风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前针对用户在真实场景（in the wild）中向大模型咨询安全与隐私问题的研究缺失，现有评估多依赖专家设定问题，缺乏对用户真实需求及模型应对能力的客观理解。

Method: 基于WildChat数据集提取14,727条S&P查询，进行主题分析；筛选270条建议寻求类（advice-seeking）提示词，通过多次（10次）测试评估不同商业与开源LLM的响应质量与一致性。

Result: 商业模型（如GPT-4）在98%的S&P提示词上能提供“足够好”的回答，而开源模型（如Llama 3/4）仅为47%；然而，即使是商业模型，在多次查询同一问题时也存在非一致性反馈。

Conclusion: 尽管商业大模型在安全性与隐私（S&P）建议方面表现出较高质量，但普遍存在响应一致性问题。模型在多次查询中产生的矛盾建议可能导致用户困惑或误导，且开源模型在相关领域的表现仍有很大提升空间。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are widely used to fulfill users' information needs; users ask LLMs about the weather, pose educational questions, and consult them for legal assistance. One particularly understudied area is digital security and privacy (S&P), where users may seek LLMs' help on how to secure their online accounts or protect their computers from cyber attacks. To the best of our knowledge, no prior study has collected or analyzed the S&P questions users ask LLMs; prior research on LLM response quality relied on expert-authored S&P misconceptions or FAQs rather than user queries. Drawing from WildChat, a dataset of 3.2M user-LLM conversations collected in the wild, our study identifies 14,727 S&P prompts and categorizes them into nine categories covering a wide range of S&P topics. From the S&P prompts, we sampled 450 and performed a thematic analysis to characterize the S&P questions users ask LLMs. Separate from the thematic analysis, we curated 270 advice-seeking S&P prompts, where users ask for recommendations, guidance, or specific S&P information. We measured LLM response quality and consistency when posing the prompt to LLMs 10 times. We found that commercial LLMs outperform open-weight models (GPT 5.5 provided "good enough" responses on 98% of prompts; Llama 4 on 47%). However, among prompts that received high-quality responses on average, commercial models sometimes produce contradictory responses across runs, risking confusing or misleading users.

</details>


### [45] [Unintended Effects of Geographic Conditioning in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18124)
*Naz Col,David M. Chan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究发现对话式AI存在“位置泄露”严重问题：即便提示词地理中性，模型也会因用户元数据及档案结构框架的诱导，产生非预期的地理位置引用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探讨现代对话式AI如何因隐藏的用户元数据而产生意想不到的区域偏见，评估模型在接收地理中性提示词时出现地理信息泄露（Location Leakage）的现象。

Method: 通过控制变量法，分别对比在创意写作和开放式问答任务中，输入不同位置元数据（如特定地区、占位符“Unknown”）与基准线（无任何元数据）下的地理引用频率，量化位置信息泄露程度。

Result: 主流LLM表现出极强的区域偏向性。当输入位置元数据时，地理泄露率最高激增至基准线的793倍；即便将位置替换为“Unknown”，泄露率仍比基准线高出72倍。

Conclusion: 模型不仅因显式地理元数据产生偏见，其预设的“用户档案”结构框架本身也会作为一种生成信号，诱导模型发生位置信息泄露。现有AI在地理敏感性方面存在严重的架构性缺陷。

Abstract: Modern conversational AI systems frequently rely on user metadata to localize responses, yet the unintended regional biases introduced by this hidden context remain poorly understood. In this work, we evaluate location leakage: the phenomenon where a model generates geographic references despite receiving a geographically neutral user prompt. Across both creative writing and open-ended Q&A prompts, even state-of-the-art LLMs systematically favor region-specific outputs when exposed to location metadata, with leakage spiking by up to 793 times above baseline (e.g., from 0.04% to 31.7% for Llama 3.1-8B, and 21.3% and 8.8% for Qwen3-8B and Claude Sonnet 4.6, respectively). Our analysis further shows a novel structural conditioning effect: replacing the injected location with the placeholder "Unknown" still elevates leakage by up to 72 times above baseline, demonstrating that the user profile frame itself, independent of any geographic content, acts as a generative conditioning signal.

</details>


### [46] [Learning from the Self-future: On-policy Self-distillation for dLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18195)
*Yifu Luo,Zeyu Chen,Haoyu Wang,Xinhao Hu,Yuxuan Zhang,Zhizhou Sha,Shiwei Liu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了d-OPSD，这是首个适配扩散大模型的在线策略自蒸馏框架，通过后缀条件化和步骤级监督解决了传统OPSD与扩散模型生成机制的冲突，显著提升了推理能力与训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）方法高度依赖自回归模型的从左到右特性，无法直接应用于具有任意顺序生成特性的扩散大模型（dLLMs）。

Method: 1. 引入“后缀条件化”（suffix-conditioning）重构自教师构建，利用模型自生成的答案取代特权前缀；2. 将监督从标记级（token-level）迁移至步骤级（step-level），使其与扩散模型的迭代去噪过程对齐。

Result: 在四个推理基准测试中，d-OPSD的表现优于RLVR和SFT基线，且训练效率极高，仅需RLVR约10%的优化步骤。

Conclusion: d-OPSD是针对扩散大模型（dLLMs）的首个在线策略自蒸馏框架，证明了通过后缀条件化和步骤级监督，可以有效优化dLLM的推理能力，且比RLVR具有更高的采样效率。

Abstract: On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) has proven effective for post-training large language models (LLMs), yet its application to diffusion LLMs (dLLMs) remains unexplored. Existing OPSD methods are inherently autoregressive-centric. They inject privileged information via left-to-right prefix conditioning with token-level divergence supervision, a design that fundamentally conflicts with the arbitraryorder generation of dLLMs. We introduce d-OPSD, the first OPSD framework tailored for dLLMs. Our approach makes two core contributions. First, we reframe self-teacher construction by using self-generated answers as suffix conditioning, enabling the student model to learn from "self future-experience" rather than privileged prefixes. Second, we shift supervision from token-level to step-level, aligning training with the iterative denoising process of dLLMs. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks show that d-OPSD consistently outperforms RLVR and SFT baselines with superior sample efficiency, requiring only around 10% of the optimization steps by RLVR and opening a promising pathway for dLLM posttraining. The code is available at https://github.com/xingzhejun/d-OPSD.

</details>


### [47] [RubricsTree: Scalable and Evolving Open-Ended Evaluation of Personal Health Agents across Health Memory and Medical Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18203)
*Weizhi Zhang,Zechen Li,Hamid Palangi,Ben Graef,A. Ali Heydari,Simon A. Lee,Salman Rahman,Ray Luo,Zeinab Esmaeilpour,Erik Schenck,Chloe Zhang,Yamin Li,Menglian Zhou,Philip S. Yu,Daniel McDuff,Lindsey Sunden,Mark Malhotra,Shwetak Patel,Ahmed A. Metwally*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: RubricsTree 是一个通过层级化、专家定义的布尔准则体系，解决了医疗 AI 评估中“评估难扩展”与“评估准确性差”的矛盾，实现了临床级的高质量、可扩展评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决大语言模型（LLM）驱动的个人医疗代理在临床部署中的评估瓶颈：即传统医师评估成本高、难扩展，而现有的“LLM 作为评判者”（LLM-as-a-judge）方法存在主观性、不一致性以及临床对齐偏差的问题。

Method: 提出了 RubricsTree 框架，建立了一个包含 100+ 个临床可验证布尔准则的专家对齐层级分类体系。通过人机协同机制（Human-in-the-loop）从 4,000 条真实用户查询中提炼，并设计了上下文感知自适应路由（Context-aware adaptive router）来优化评估效率与质量。

Result: 1. 在专家对齐能力上显著优于强基准方法；2. 能可靠地惩罚上下文退化的响应；3. 在 Gemini、GPT 和 Qwen 等模型族中，通过作为反馈或奖励，在 HealthBench 上实现了约 66% 的性能提升。

Conclusion: RubricsTree 提供了一种可扩展、可审计且可进化的评估基础设施，是实现个人医疗 AI 持续优化的关键技术，解决了临床部署中的评估瓶颈。

Abstract: The LLM-empowered personal health agents with user health (sensor) metrics have offered a promising pathway to alleviate global disparities in healthcare access. However, large-scale clinical deployment remains constrained by an open-ended evaluation bottleneck: physician annotation is reliable but costly and unscalable, while LLM-as-a-judge evaluators are scalable but subjective, inconsistent, and sometimes clinically misaligned. We introduce RubricsTree, a scalable evaluation framework with an expert-aligned hierarchical taxonomy of over 100 atomic, clinically-verifiable Boolean rubrics, evolving from the insights of 4,000 real user queries through an iterative human-in-the-loop curation protocol with an expertise panel led by an experienced physician. A context-aware adaptive router activates only the relevant auto-weighted rubric subset per query, providing the throughput needed for scalable evaluation with expert-aligned quality. Through a systematic meta-evaluation, we show that RubricsTree (i) substantially exceeds a strong large-scale evaluation baseline in expert alignment on challenging open-ended queries; (ii) reliably penalizes contextually degraded responses; and (iii) when used as structured instructions, text feedback, or training rewards for performance optimization, yields up to ~66% relative gains on HealthBench for Gemini, GPT, and Qwen model families. RubricsTree thus provides a scalable, auditable, and evolving evaluation infrastructure required for the continuous optimization of product-level personal healthcare AI.

</details>


### [48] [Analyzing and Encoding the Al-Mawrid Arabic-English Dictionary with the ISO Language Markup Framework and TEI Lex-0](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18205)
*Diaa Fayed,Laurent Romary*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一套系统化方法，将《Al-Mawrid》阿拉伯语-英语词典数字化为符合国际标准（LMF/TEI Lex-0）的计算词典，显著提升了资源在自然语言处理与语义网中的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在弥补阿拉伯语词汇基础设施的空白，将传统的纸质版《Al-Mawrid》阿拉伯语-英语词典转换为标准化的计算词典，以解决20世纪双语词典中常见的结构歧义与格式不一致问题。

Method: 采用双重标准框架，即符合ISO词汇标记框架（LMF）与TEI Lex-0指南；基于词典的词汇知识密度进行实证分析；通过抽样（字母'Ayn'）验证解析规则，并使用基于前缀的引用系统以促进链接开放数据（LLOD）的集成。

Result: 解析准确率达到91%；在同义词提取上取得了85%的查准率和98%的查全率，其他形态-语义特征的查准率为88%。该研究成功创建了一个可扩展、可集成的电子词典资源。

Conclusion: 通过构建可互操作、机器可读的电子词典，该方法为阿拉伯语NLP和数字人文领域中复杂遗留双语词典的数字化提供了一套可复用的工作流程。研究还指出了现有标准在处理特定阿拉伯语语言现象时的局限性，并为语义网的集成铺平了道路。

Abstract: This paper presents a robust methodology for the systematic digitization and encoding of the Al-Mawrid Arabic-English dictionary, transforming it from a legacy print resource into a standardized computational lexicon. Addressing a significant gap in Arabic lexical infrastructure, the study adopts a dual-standard framing that aligns the ISO Lexical Markup Framework (LMF) with the Text Encoding Initiative TEI Lex-0 guidelines. By applying an editorial view to the dictionary's macro- and microstructure, the research resolves the structural ambiguities and punctuation inconsistencies typical of 20th-century bilingual dictionaries. The methodology is grounded in an empirical analysis of the dictionary's lexical knowledge density. Drawing on a representative sample (the letter Ayn, comprising 4.6% of the total volume), the study provides scientific weight to the encoding process, demonstrating a structural parsing accuracy of 91%. Quantitative evaluation of the information extraction rules reveals high performance, with 85% precision and 98% recall for synonyms, and 88% precision for other morpho-semantic features. Beyond technical description, the paper provides a critical comparison with existing Arabic lexical resources and discusses the limitations of TEI Lex-0 when modelling specific Arabic phenomena, such as implicit "open set" semantic relations and scattered morphological cues. Furthermore, the study explores the potential for Linguistic Linked Open Data (LLOD) integration by establishing a scalable prefix-based referencing system that facilitates the resource's inclusion in the semantic web. The result is an interoperable, machine-tractable resource that provides a reproducible workflow for the retro-digitization of complex legacy bilingual lexicons within the Arabic NLP and Digital Humanities communities.

</details>


### [49] [Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization: Teacher in Prompts, Not Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18216)
*Byung-Kwan Lee,Ximing Lu,Shizhe Diao,Minki Kang,Saurav Muralidharan,Karan Sapra,Andrew Tao,Pavlo Molchanov,Yejin Choi,Yu-Chiang Frank Wang,Ryo Hachiuma*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: ZPPO提出了一种将教师指导嵌入提示词而非梯度更新的方法，通过BCQ和NCQ机制，使小模型能够高效模仿强教师并在其“最近发展区”内自主学习，显著提升了蒸馏训练的稳定性和模型性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的知识蒸馏在小模型上容易导致过拟合（陷入教师逻辑尖锐点），而在强化学习中引入强教师反馈会导致偏离策略分布从而产生“漂移”效应。需要一种既能利用强教师能力，又能保持模型鲁棒性的新训练机制。

Method: 提出“最近发展区近端策略优化”（ZPPO），设计了BCQ（二元候选问题）和NCQ（负候选问题）两种提示词重构策略，并结合提示词重放缓冲区，使小模型在当前学习能力范围（最近发展区）内针对长板问题进行定向强化。

Result: 在Qwen3.5系列（0.8B-9B）与27B教师模型的实验中，在31个基准测试上表现强于现有的离线/在线蒸馏及GRPO算法，尤其在小参数模型上增益最明显。

Conclusion: ZPPO通过将教师引导从策略梯度移除至提示词中，有效解决了小模型在蒸馏中表现脆弱的问题，在多种模型规模和基准测试上优于传统的蒸馏和RL方法。

Abstract: Knowledge distillation transfers a teacher's competence to a small student but is brittle in the small-student regime: forcing the student to imitate logits from a much larger teacher concentrates it on the teacher's sharpest modes, hurting generalization on benchmark families beyond the training corpus. Reinforcement learning (RL) avoids logit imitation by training on the student's own rollouts. However, on questions where every rollout fails-yielding zero advantage and being silently discarded-injecting a stronger teacher's response into the policy gradient breaks the on-policy assumption and induces drift. We introduce Zone of Proximal Policy Optimization (ZPPO), inspired by Vygotsky's zone of proximal development, which keeps the teacher inside the prompt rather than the policy gradient. On hard questions, ZPPO constructs two reformulated prompts: a Binary Candidate-included Question (BCQ) pairs one correct teacher response with one incorrect student response as anonymized candidates the student must discriminate, and a Negative Candidate-included Question (NCQ) aggregates the student's wrong rollouts into a single prompt to surface their shared failure modes. A prompt replay buffer recirculates each hard question until it either graduates-the student's mean rollout accuracy on it reaches half- or is FIFO-evicted under finite capacity, amplifying BCQ and NCQ inside the student's current zone of proximal development. On the Qwen3.5 family at four student scales (0.8B-9B) with a 27B teacher, post-trained as vision-language models and evaluated on a 31-benchmark suite (16 VLM, 10 LLM, 5 Video), ZPPO outperforms off/on-policy distillation and GRPO, with the largest gains at the smallest scale.

</details>


### [50] [Darshana Graph: A Parallel Commentary Corpus for Comparative Indian Philosophy, with Stylometric and Exploratory Graph Analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18222)
*Joy Bose*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Darshana Graph 是一个包含12.5万条跨印度哲学传统（印度教、佛教、耆那教）的语料库，通过对8,500条核心经文进行跨注释对齐，结合风格测量学分析和LLM关系抽取技术，揭示了不同哲学流派在论证风格和语义解释上的异同，并验证了计算方法在哲学史研究中的潜力和局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数字人文资源缺乏对印度传统哲学（印度教、佛教、耆那教）大规模、跨派系评论家的结构化对齐，难以系统性地进行跨传统的解释学比较研究。研究旨在填补这一空白，为量化哲学史研究提供基础支撑。

Method: 1. 构建了包含12.5万条记录的 Darshana Graph 语料库，其中8,500条记录实现了跨十八位评论家的对齐；2. 风格测量分析：通过引文密度、反驳率和句法复杂度进行量化对比；3. LLM管线：采用受限大模型管线，结合预定义关系词汇和确定性的事后验证（post-hoc validation）来抽取概念间的哲学关系。

Result: 1. 风格测量学揭示了不同流派在论证策略上的显著差异（如引用密度与反驳率的负相关）；2. 成功构建了包含跨流派分歧模式的哲学关系图谱；3. 实验发现现有模型在抽取复杂哲学逻辑时存在局限，且基于嵌入（embedding）的分析与基于图的抽取结果可能存在冲突。

Conclusion: Darshana Graph 提供了一个大规模、多维度的印度哲学文本分析数据集，证明了通过结构化数据及计算方法（如风格测量学和LLM信息抽取）分析复杂哲学文献的可行性。该研究强调了跨注释比对在揭示思想流派演变和论证风格差异方面的价值，并指出了当前模型在处理复杂哲学逻辑时面临的局限性。

Abstract: We introduce Darshana Graph, a corpus of over 125,000 text records spanning classical Hindu, Buddhist, and Jain philosophical traditions, drawn from public-domain and openly licensed translations of sources including the Bhagavad Gita, Brahma Sutras, principal Upanishads, the Pali Canon, and core Jain texts. Its distinctive contribution lies in a structurally unique subset of roughly 8,500 Hindu and Jain records in which the same root verse or sutra is aligned across eighteen historical commentators representing five schools of Vedanta and other darshanas, enabling direct comparison of how independent interpretive traditions read identical source material. To our knowledge, no publicly available resource provides comparable cross-commentator alignment at this scale. We present two analyses built on this corpus. First, a transparent stylometric comparison requiring no machine learning measures argumentative style through scriptural citation density, explicit refutation rate, and sentence complexity. It finds a moderate negative correlation between citation density and refutation rate, a marked increase in refutation rate across three commentators in a related doctrinal lineage, and measurable genre-level differences within the Pali Canon itself. Second, we describe a constrained large language model pipeline that extracts typed philosophical relationships between concepts using a predefined relation vocabulary and deterministic post-hoc validation. The resulting graph surfaces cross-school disagreement patterns while also revealing important extraction limitations, including cases where an independent embedding-based analysis disagrees with the graph-derived findings. We release the full corpus, extracted relationship graph, and all source code.

</details>


### [51] [ReproRepo: Scaling Reproducibility Audits with GitHub Repository Issues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18237)
*Shanda Li,Qiuhong Anna Wei,Jingwu Tang,Valerie Chen,Nihar B Shah,Tim Dettmers,Yiming Yang,Ameet Talwalkar*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对LLM Agent在复现性审计中评估难、可扩展性差的问题，本文提出了ReproRepo框架，利用GitHub真实Issue实现自动化监督，实验显示LLM在识别复现障碍方面表现优异，达到90%的匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM复现性评估基准依赖大量人工数据标注和评估，难以扩展；需要一套能利用真实场景、可规模化的复现性审计方法。

Method: 提出了ReproRepo框架，利用GitHub上真实存在的人工提交Issue作为监督数据，通过对1,149篇机器学习论文及其相关代码库进行自动化调研，对比评估了四个前沿LLM Agent配置表现。

Result: 实验表明，最强的Agent配置（Codex+GPT-5.5）能为约90%的论文找到语义相关的真实复现障碍；Agent在识别可见失败和定性定位方面效果显著，但在具体代码溯源（精确标注）上仍存在不足。

Conclusion: ReproRepo是一个可扩展的评估框架，能够有效利用GitHub真实问题进行复现性审计；当前的LLM Agent在识别复现障碍方面表现出较强能力，但在精确定位方面仍有提升空间。

Abstract: Reproducing research results from papers and released code is central to scientific progress. Existing works have introduced benchmarks to evaluate whether LLM agents can assist with reproducibility, but they are difficult to scale due to their reliance on substantial manual effort for data curation and evaluation. We introduce ReproRepo, a scalable framework for reproducibility evaluation that leverages human-raised GitHub issues as naturally occurring supervision on realistic reproduction blockers. We instantiate ReproRepo on 1,149 recent machine learning papers from major conferences and evaluate four frontier model-agent configurations. Our results show that LLM agents, even without executing code, can identify many real-world reproducibility problems from paper-repository pairs: the best agent in our study, namely Codex with GPT-5.5, surfaces at least one semantically related human-reported blocker for ~90% of papers in the study. Further analysis shows that agents are particularly effective for surfacing visible failures and identifying the right semantic region, but may still be insufficient in exact localization. ReproRepo can serve as a reusable, scalable framework for future evaluations of LLM agents on real-world reproducibility auditing. Our code is released at https://github.com/LithiumDA/ReproRepo.

</details>


### [52] [Variable-Width Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18246)
*Zhaofeng Wu,Oliver Sieberling,Shawn Tan,Rameswar Panda,Yury Polyanskiy,Yoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 为Transformer模型提出了一种非均匀宽度分配设计（> <former），通过加宽首尾层并缩窄中间层，在提升模型性能的同时显著降低了计算量和内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer架构通常在所有层中使用恒定宽度，忽略了不同深度层在计算角色上的差异，导致参数和计算预算分配不均，资源利用率有待提高。

Method: 提出了一种“> <形”架构（> <former），在保持网络深度不变的前提下，人为地缩窄中间层，加宽首尾层，并使用无需参数的残差调整机制（residual resizing mechanism）实现层间连接。

Result: 在200M到3B参数规模的多项实验中，> <former在语言建模损失上优于同参数的均匀架构，FLOPs减少了22%，KV缓存内存和I/O成本降低了15%。

Conclusion: 非均匀的宽度分配（即“> <形”架构）是一种更具资源效率的语言模型扩展策略，能在保持或提升性能的同时减少计算量和内存开销。

Abstract: Scaling model size, specifically depth and width, has driven significant progress in transformer-based language models. However, most architectures maintain a constant width across all layers, allocating a fixed parameter and computation budget evenly despite different layers potentially playing distinct computational roles. In this work, we empirically investigate nonuniform capacity allocation across network depth by proposing a $\times$-shaped > <former architecture. This design maintains wider early and late layers while narrowing the middle layers, utilizing a parameter-free residual resizing mechanism. Across decoder-only language models ranging from 200M to 2B parameters (dense) and 3B parameters (MoE), our > <former consistently outperforms parameter-matched uniform baselines on language modeling loss. By reducing the average layer width, this architecture also requires fewer overall FLOPs (22% reduction under fitted loss-matched scaling curves) and smaller KV cache memory and I/O cost (15% reduction). In analysis, we show that this bottleneck structure results in qualitatively different representations in residual streams. Overall, our results demonstrate that nonuniform width allocation can result in more resource-optimal scaling of language models.

</details>


<div id='cs.DC'></div>

# cs.DC [[Back]](#toc)

### [53] [Evaluating LLM Coding Agents on SZ-Family Lossy Compression Across Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17058)
*Changqing Li,Shouwei Gao,Kai Zhao,Sheng Di,Wenqian Dong*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该研究评估了LLM编程代理在HPC有损压缩任务中的应用，发现其在不同硬件架构（GPU与Cerebras）及不同代码复杂度下的表现存在显著差异，并强调了评估HPC代码生成需针对架构特性进行考量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前大语言模型（LLM）在代码翻译与优化领域应用广泛，但其在高性能计算（HPC）这一对性能高度敏感的场景下的实际效能仍缺乏深入研究。

Method: 以SZ系列误差有界有损压缩内核为测试基准，在NVIDIA GPU和Cerebras晶圆级加速器上，评估单代理（single-agent）迭代生成工作流，并分析其运行时行为、提示词敏感度及失败模式。

Result: 研究发现存在显著的跨架构差异：在GPU上，模型能力增强可换来性能提升，但对提示词和优化引导极度敏感；而在Cerebras上，主要挑战在于代码的可运行性及对空间执行模型的适配。此外，LLM在模块化内核（SZx）上的表现优于高度耦合的位级流水线内核（SZp）。

Conclusion: 针对HPC领域的LLM编程代理评估不能仅关注性能指标，还必须涵盖架构特定的鲁棒性。研究表明，在线程化平台（如GPU）上的成功经验无法直接推广至空间加速架构（如Cerebras）。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) coding agents are increasingly applied to code translation and optimization, yet their effectiveness in performance-critical high-performance computing (HPC) settings remains poorly characterized. This paper evaluates LLM-based coding workflows on SZ-family error-bounded lossy compression kernels, which combine numerical constraints with memory-intensive and control-flow-heavy implementations. We study two representative CUDA workloads (SZp and SZx) and target two heterogeneous execution platforms: NVIDIA GPUs and Cerebras wafer-scale accelerators. Focusing on single-agent iterative generation, we analyze not only final throughput but also agent runtime behavior, including iteration patterns, sensitivity to prompt specification, and characteristic failure modes. Our results reveal a pronounced cross-architecture divergence. On GPUs, stronger models can achieve substantially higher throughput but exhibit increased sensitivity to prompt precision and optimization guidance, whereas on Cerebras the dominant challenge lies in producing runnable programs under a PE-centric spatial execution model. We further observe that LLM agents are more effective on modular kernels (SZx) than on tightly coupled bit-level pipelines (SZp), where structural dependencies hinder optimization progress. These findings suggest that evaluating LLM coding agents for HPC requires accounting for both performance outcomes and architecture-specific robustness, and that success on thread-based platforms does not directly transfer to spatial accelerators.

</details>


### [54] [Towards Distributed Inference of LLMs on a P2P Network](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17059)
*Shabari S Nair,Krishanu Saini*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 针对大模型推理的前缀缓存分布式共享问题，本论文提出一种无需中心调度、基于弱一致性缓存元数据的P2P路由策略，实现了高效的KV缓存重用以降低推理延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存（Prefix Caching）虽能通过重用共有Prompt的KV缓存来降低延迟，但因KV缓存分散在不同节点，集群规模的缓存共享面临挑战。现有方案通常缺乏高效的分布式路由机制。

Method: 提出一种去中心化的、感知前缀缓存的P2P LLM服务路由方案。每个节点维护本地Radix树记录自身缓存，并通过周期性反熵（anti-entropy）机制异步刷新对同伴节点缓存的估计。请求基于“最长前缀匹配”原则路由至目标节点，无需中心化协调或KV缓存迁移。

Result: 在MMLU模拟工作负载下的评估显示，该机制在特定网络和分布条件下显著提升了缓存命中率和整体性能。

Conclusion: 在低通信延迟和偏态前缀分布的场景下，去中心化路由能有效降低LLM推理延迟；但在高网路延迟和因亲和性导致的负载热点场景下，其优势会受限。该方案证明了弱一致性元数据足以支持大规模、分布式KV缓存重用。

Abstract: Prefix caching can reduce LLM inference latency by reusing KV caches across requests with shared prompts, but cluster-scale reuse is challenging because caches are partitioned across nodes. We propose a decentralized, prefix-cache-aware routing scheme for peer-to-peer LLM serving. Each node maintains a local radix tree of its own cached prefixes and asynchronously refreshed estimates of peer caches using periodic anti-entropy. Requests are routed to the node with the longest estimated prefix match, without centralized coordination or KV-cache transfer. Stale metadata only causes cache misses, not incorrect outputs, making weak consistency sufficient for correctness. Evaluation on simulated MMLU workloads show that decentralized routing improves latency under low communication delay and skewed prefix distributions, while high network latency and affinity-induced hotspots limit its benefits.

</details>


### [55] [Local Fault Repair of Perfect Resource Placements in Eisenstein--Jacobi Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17288)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文建立了Eisenstein-Jacobi（EJ）网络资源局部修复的数学理论，证明了其不同于高斯网络的独特修复规律，给出了单故障与多故障场景下的最优修复策略及重叠量公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究Eisenstein-Jacobi（EJ）网络中完美资源布局在发生局部故障后的修复问题，重点探讨修复所需的最小 replacement 数量及其在多故障场景下的叠加特性。

Method: 利用三条带几何（three-strip geometry）、端点刚性（endpoint-rigidity）与对角线走廊定理（diagonal-corridor theorems）进行代数证明，结合包含-排斥恒等式分析多故障覆盖，并辅以超过19,400个实例的计算机审计。

Result: 对于单个故障，修复数恒为2，重叠量公式为$t^2$；对于两个故障，在特定条件下可实现3个replace的优化修复；对于q个故障，推导出$2q$的上限，并证明了在处理密集故障簇时具备显著的子加性。

Conclusion: EJ网络中的局部故障修复在结构上不同于高斯网络，呈现出独特的非加性行为和聚类子加性特征。研究确定了对于任意数量（q）故障的通用修复上限，并验证了大规模实例中的子加性优势。

Abstract: Perfect resource placements in dense Eisenstein--Jacobi (EJ) networks partition the network into hexagonal radius-$t$ service cells. This paper studies local repair of such placements after resource failures. For one failed resource, we prove that one replacement cannot cover the failed hexagon and two always suffice, giving $ρ_{\mathrm{EJ}}(t)=2$ for all $t\ge1$. Among minimum-size repairs, the sharp minimum-overlap formula $Ω_{\mathrm{EJ}}(t)=t^2$ follows from the three-strip geometry of EJ balls. For two failed resources, independent repair gives a four-replacement upper bound, but unlike the Gaussian case EJ repair is not always additive: two infinite neighboring displacement families admit three-replacement repairs, proved optimal by a two-ball impossibility argument. Additive behavior is established algebraically via endpoint-rigidity and diagonal-corridor theorems. For $q$ failed resources, independent canonical repair gives a universal $2q$ upper bound, exact when failed cells are pairwise more than $4t$ apart. Dense cluster subadditivity is proved for infinite four-fault and six-fault families with exact repair numbers four and five, giving savings of four and seven over independent repair. An exact inclusion--exclusion identity governs repeated coverage for arbitrary multi-fault repairs. An audit over 19,400 instances confirms widespread subadditivity. EJ local repair is structurally distinct from the Gaussian case: the one-fault overlap is quadratic, two-fault repair can be non-additive, and clustered repairs reuse replacement balls across multiple failed cells.

</details>


### [56] [Space-Efficient Lock-Free Linear-Probing Hash Table](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17315)
*Hagit Attiya,Rotem Oshman,Noa Schiller*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型无锁线性探测哈希表，通过极低的元数据开销实现了高性能、并发友好且空间高效的数据存储，支持无等待查找及高效的动态空间管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的并发线性探测哈希表设计通常通过限制并发度或使用大量元数据来换取活性保证，这牺牲了线性探测原有的空间效率优势。研究旨在克服这些局限，开发一种既具备强活性保证、又保持空间效率的并发线性探测哈希表。

Method: 设计了一种基于CAS（或LL/SC指令）的无锁线性探测哈希表算法。该算法通过极少的每条目元数据（LL/SC下为常数位，CAS下为对数位）处理并发争用，支持高效的插入、删除及无等待查找，并实现了无需重建表的内存回收机制。

Result: 实验与理论证明表明，该算法是线性一致且无锁的。在假设无重复键并发插入的情况下，其预期摊销复杂度与传统的顺序线性探测哈希表相当。此外，该方案能够安全回收已删除元素的空间。

Conclusion: 该研究成功设计并实现了一种无锁（lock-free）线性探测哈希表，在保持紧凑内存布局和空间效率的前提下，实现了强大的并发性能（包括无等待查找）和线性一致性，解决了传统并发线性探测哈希表因元数据过大或并发限制而导致的效率瓶颈。

Abstract: Linear probing is one of the simplest and most space-efficient approaches to hash table design, and is widely used in sequential settings due to its compact memory layout. However, designing a concurrent linear-probing hash table with strong liveness guarantees has proved difficult, and only a handful of such algorithms have been proposed, all of which either restrict concurrency or rely on large per-entry metadata, thereby compromising space efficiency.
  We present a lock-free linear-probing hash table with wait-free lookups that retains the core advantages of sequential linear probing while handling contention gracefully. Our design uses only a small amount of metadata per table entry: a constant number of additional bits when using LL/SC, or a logarithmic number of bits when using CAS. The algorithm is linearizable and lock-free, supports insert, delete, and wait-free lookup operations, and is able to safely reclaim space used by deleted elements without rebuilding the table.
  We analyze the amortized step complexity of our hash table assuming no concurrent insertions of the same key, and show that each operation has expected amortized step complexity matching that of sequential linear probing, up to the point contention per key.

</details>


### [57] [RISE: Relay Inference and Online Scheduling for Efficient Edge-Device Collaborative Diffusion Model Services](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17378)
*Zilan Huang,Zhiqing Tang,Hanshuai Cui,Tian Wang,Yuan Wu,Weijia Jia,Wei Zhao*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RISE 是一种针对边缘文生图任务的协作推理框架，通过“边缘大模型生成语义结构、设备端小模型细化细节”的无训练中继模式及智能情境调节，在不损失画质的前提下实现了显著的性能加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的边缘计算路径（大模型高延迟或小模型低质量）无法满足异构负载下的多样化需求，且缺乏跨边缘服务器与终端设备的协同计算机制。

Method: 1. 训练即用型中继推理（Training-free Relay Mechanism）：利用同一模型族共享潜空间的特性，边缘大模型负责早期的语义结构生成，设备端小模型负责后期的细节补全，无需额外训练即可切换。
2. 上下文感知调度（Contextual Bandit Scheduler）：基于提示词复杂度、用户偏好、网络质量及负载情况，利用情境老虎机算法实时动态调整中继配置。

Result: 在两个基准测试中，RISE 的中继机制实现了最高 2.1 倍的加速，同时保持了大模型的生成质量；其调度器能有效平衡混合负载下的质量与延迟需求。

Conclusion: RISE 通过结合“训练即用型中继推理”与“上下文感知调度”，成功解决了边缘侧文生图模型在高质量与低延迟之间的矛盾，实现了性能与质量的平衡。

Abstract: Text-to-image diffusion models are increasingly deployed at the network edge to serve heterogeneous workloads with diverse quality and latency requirements. However, existing deployment strategies choose either large edge-side models with high fidelity but high latency or lightweight device-side models that offer speed at the cost of semantic coherence. Moreover, these approaches rarely split the denoising workload between models of different sizes across edge servers and user devices. To bridge this gap, we propose RISE, a method for edge-device diffusion model services that combines relay inference with online scheduling. Driven by the finding that the latent intensity exhibits minimal deviation after a model handoff, RISE uses a training-free relay mechanism that exploits the shared latent space within a model family: the large model on the edge handles the early denoising steps that shape semantic structure, then passes the intermediate latent to a small device-side model for detail refinement. To deploy this mechanism as a practical service, a contextual bandit scheduler selects the best relay configuration based on prompt complexity, user preferences, network quality and real-time node loads. Experiments on two benchmarks show that RISE's relay mechanism achieves up to 2.1$\times$ speedup while preserving full-model quality, and its context-aware scheduler effectively balances quality and latency under mixed workloads.

</details>


### [58] [SpecGen: Accelerating Agentic Kernel Optimization with Speculative Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17518)
*Jihu Guo,Sitian Lu,Tenghui Ma,Wei Gao,Zhisheng Ye,Xingcheng Zhang,Dahua Lin*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SpecGen 是一种利用“投机生成”技术加速代理 GPU 内核优化的系统，通过在 LLM 推理期间并行生成和验证内核，有效克服了长推理延迟和低资源利用率的问题，从而提升了优化效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的代理内核优化（Agentic Kernel Optimization）方法存在三大系统级效率瓶颈：LLM 推理延迟过长、分析反馈不足以及资源的利用率低下。

Method: 1. 投机生成（Speculative Generation）：在推理过程中的不同触发点并行生成（Fork）多个内核候选，并允许在发现满足条件的候选时提前终止推理；2. 动态调度：根据请求到达率重分配验证和分析 GPU 资源池；3. 内存优化：利用验证/分析 GPU 的空闲内存作为远程 KV 缓存，避免重复计算。

Result: 在 H200 平台上进行的实验表明，SpecGen 相比现有系统减少了端到端优化时间，增加了分析反馈量，提升了资源利用率，并在固定资源预算下获得了更好的内核加速比。

Conclusion: SpecGen 引入了“投机生成”机制，通过在 LLM 推理过程中并行生成、验证和分析候选内核，显著缩短了生成延迟，提高了资源利用率，并在相同的时间和 Token 预算下实现了更高的内核性能优化。

Abstract: Agentic kernel optimization automates manual GPU kernel tuning via iterative generation, validation, and profiling with reasoning LLMs, casting the optimization task as feedback-guided search. However, our workload characterization reveals three system-level inefficiencies that limit search efficiency: (1) long generation latency due to LLM reasoning, (2) insufficient profiling feedback, and (3) underutilized validation/profiling resources. Our key insight is that the ongoing reasoning generation exposes a window for producing additional candidate kernels before it completes, allowing the system to terminate reasoning early once a satisfactory kernel appears.
  We present SpecGen, an agentic kernel optimization system with \emph{speculative generation}. First, SpecGen forks non-reasoning generations at well-chosen trigger points in the reasoning trace to yield kernels, increasing the candidate kernel count per iteration. These kernels are validated and profiled in parallel with the ongoing reasoning, increasing profiling feedback, and keeping resources busy during generation. When a kernel meets the termination criterion, SpecGen terminates the reasoning generation early to reduce the generation latency. Second, SpecGen dynamically reallocates validation and profiling GPU pools based on the arrival rate and prioritizes requests to reduce profiling feedback latency under bursty speculative generation load. Furthermore, SpecGen utilizes spare memory of the validation/profiling GPUs as remote KV cache storage to eliminate prefix recomputation of speculative generations under limited memory budget. Experiments with two reasoning LLMs on H200 show that SpecGen reduces end-to-end time over three baseline systems, while producing more profiling feedback, increasing resource utilization, and improving kernel speedup under a fixed time and token budget.

</details>


### [59] [Local Fault Repair of Perfect Resource Placements in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17527)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了密集高斯网络中资源故障后的修复机制，通过几何映射方法证明了单故障、双故障及多故障场景下的精确修复数量与最小重叠公式，并经大规模算例验证其准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究高斯网络在资源故障后的修复问题（Post-deployment repair problem），旨在解决如何高效、精确地修复因资源故障导致的覆盖失效，确保网络资源分布的稳定性。

Method: 利用旋转坐标系 ($u=x+y, v=x-y$) 将高斯 Lee 球转化为奇偶约束的正方形；通过局部修复定理、包含-排斥原理以及几何覆盖审计方法，对故障区域进行数学建模与分析，并利用 7,494 个案例进行地事实（ground-truth）验证。

Result: 1. 单资源故障：当 $t=1$ 时修复数为 3，当 $t \ge 2$ 时修复数为 2，且最小重叠公式为 $Ω_G(t)=t+1$。
2. 双资源故障：对于 $t \ge 2$，双故障总是需要且仅需 4 个局部修复。
3. 多资源故障：推导出了通用的包含-排斥重叠恒等式，并结合紧致修正公式 $Ω_{\rm extra}=P_2-A-C_3$ 描述修复重叠情况。

Conclusion: 本文确立了高斯网络中资源故障后的精确修复定理，证明了单故障、双故障及多故障场景下的修复数量与重叠公式，并通过数值验证证实了这些结论的普适性与紧致性。

Abstract: Perfect resource placement in dense Gaussian networks partitions the network into Lee balls centered at resource nodes. The fault-free placement problem is already classified; this paper studies the complementary post-deployment problem of repairing such placements after resource faults. The paper gives exact local repair theorems for the dense Gaussian placement generated by $t+(t+1)i$; by conjugation and rotation symmetry, the same results hold for the companion generator $(t+1)+ti$. For one failed resource, we prove failure-cell locality, derive the exact replacement number $ρ_G(1)=3$ and $ρ_G(t)=2$ for all $t\ge2$, and prove the sharp minimum-overlap formula $Ω_G(t)=t+1$ among minimum-size repairs. The overlap lower bound is proved from the corner structure of equal-size Lee balls in the rotated coordinates $u=x+y$ and $v=x-y$, where Gaussian Lee balls become parity-constrained squares. For two failed resources, we prove exact additivity: every pair of failed resource cells requires exactly four local replacements for $t\ge2$, and four always suffice. The two-fault lower bound reduces all relevant resource displacements to two canonical neighboring cases and exhibits four mutually incompatible failed-cell corners in each case. For multi-failure repairs, we prove a general inclusion--exclusion identity for overlap inside the failed region; hence the formula remains exact for arbitrary higher-order dense cores. When a canonical repair instance is certified to have maximum multiplicity three, the identity reduces to the compact correction $Ω_{\rm extra}=P_2-A-C_3$. A ground-truth audit over 7,494 Gaussian cases recomputes coverage from lattice geometry, verifies all exact formulas, and records reproducible multiplicity witnesses.

</details>


### [60] [Multi-Orientation Edge-Minimum Repair for Non-Redundant Fault-Tolerant Broadcasting in Dense Gaussian Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17528)
*Bader Albader*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文为密集高斯网络设计了一种名为MOEM的非冗余一对多广播故障修复算法，通过优化广播树的方向选择和最小化重连边的使用，在保证低修复深度的同时实现了高效可靠的通信恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 密集高斯网络（Dense Gaussian networks）作为一种度的为4的互连网络，需要可靠的机制来解决广播过程中的故障，以实现高效的通信拓扑维护。

Method: 提出了多方向边缘最小修复（MOEM）算法：通过评估固定大小的高斯广播树方向族，选择故障感知方向，将故障修剪后的树收缩为健康组件，并利用外部组件交叉修复边连接这些组件。

Result: 理论证明了该方法在满足特定条件下可实现非冗余且最小边缘的修复；证明了1-2个故障情况下的最大修复深度为k+2；经验证，对于k=5至200的规模，该方法能够高效处理故障，平均仅需约两条修复边。

Conclusion: MOEM算法在密集高斯网络中实现了非冗余的一对多广播故障修复，通过选定优化方向，在保证每个健康节点仅接收一次消息的前提下，仅使用最少的必要边缘完成修复，且在1-2个故障情况下能将修复深度严格控制在k+2以内。

Abstract: Dense Gaussian networks are degree-four algebraic interconnection networks with compact diameter and simple modular routing. This paper studies non-redundant one-to-all broadcast repair in the dense Gaussian network generated by $α=k+(k+1)i$. We propose multi-orientation edge-minimum repair (MOEM), which evaluates a constant-size family of Gaussian broadcast-tree orientations, selects a fault-aware orientation, contracts the fault-pruned tree into healthy components, and reconnects those components using external component-crossing repair edges. The resulting structure is a rooted spanning tree of the healthy subgraph, so each healthy node receives the message exactly once and no faulty node is used. We prove that, for a chosen orientation with $c$ fault-pruned components and a connected healthy component graph, the repair step is non-redundant and uses the minimum possible number $c-1$ of external component-repair edges. We also prove that, for every one- or two-fault placement, the MOEM orientation family contains a repair with depth at most $k+2$. The depth proof combines a certificate framework, an explicit four-case off-axis analysis, and a five-component orthogonal-axis certificate. Exhaustive validation for $k=5,\ldots,10$ and large-scale validation through $k=200$ confirm the implementation and show that random two-fault repairs use approximately two external repair edges.

</details>


### [61] [AoiZora: Topology-Aware Auto-Parallel Optimization for Inference of Diffusion Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17566)
*Kaijian Wang,Yuanyuan Xu,Fanjiang Ye,Ye Cao,Jingwei Zuo,T. S. Eugene Ng,Yarong Mu,Yuke Wang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: AoiZora 是一种针对 TPU 拓扑感知的视频扩散模型编译器规划器，通过物理布局优化减少了跨设备通信延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频扩散模型推理计算量巨大，现有的自动并行系统忽略了逻辑分片在 TPU 物理互连拓扑中的实际布局，导致通信开销大、推理延迟高，无法满足实时性要求。

Method: AoiZora 编译器优化方案：1. 基于轻量级中间表示 (IR) 筛选并排除无效的分片策略；2. 利用编译后的 HLO 代码与拓扑感知通信模型，对剩余策略进行评估与物理位置调度；3. 最终策略与现有编译器工作流集成，无需修改原始代码或底层算子。

Result: 在 TPU v5e 子切片上，AoiZora 将 Wan 2.1 单步去噪延迟降低了 1.42 倍。

Conclusion: AoiZora 通过将逻辑分片映射与 TPU 物理拓扑优化相结合，有效克服了视频扩散模型在多加速器推理中的延迟瓶颈，显著提升了生成效率。

Abstract: Video diffusion has quickly grown into a key generative serving workload, yet producing each clip demands many denoising iterations over large spatio-temporal latents, which puts low-latency inference out of reach on a single device. A denoising step is therefore typically distributed across multiple accelerators, and TPU sub-slices have become an attractive and practical fabric for doing so. Current auto-parallel systems, however, search almost exclusively over logical device meshes and disregard how a chosen sharding is actually laid out on the physical TPU interconnect -- an oversight that leaves large, topology-dependent performance on the table. We address this gap with AoiZora, a compiler-mediated topology planner built for low-latency video diffusion inference on TPU sub-slices. Its guiding principle is to reconnect logical sharding with physical placement by drawing on different points in the compilation flow: AoiZora first eliminates weak sharding candidates from inexpensive pre-compilation IRs, then compiles only the ones that survive and orders their physical placements using compiled HLO together with a topology-aware communication model. The winning plan is realized along the ordinary compiler path, leaving model code, compiler lowering, collective kernels, and network routing entirely intact. On TPU v5e sub-slices, AoiZora reduces Wan 2.1 one-step denoising latency by as much as 1.42x relative to existing solutions.

</details>


### [62] [From GPU to Microcontroller: Online Ridge Regression for Edge-Deployable Traffic Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17613)
*Suresh Purini,Archit Narwadkar,Deepak Gangadharan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 通过将复杂的深度学习预测模型简化为基于递归最小二乘法的轻量级线性模型，实现了在低功耗边缘硬件上的高性能交通流量预测，展示了模型小型化在计算效率和准确性方面的巨大潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的主流交通流量预测模型（如GCN、GLMST）计算复杂度高、依赖GPU，无法在资源受限的环境（如IoT传感器边缘端）下部署执行。作者观察到现有模型存在过度参数化问题，可通过降维简化。

Method: 提出一种基于传感器独立建模的轻量级方法，摒弃复杂的图形神经网络和MLP，采用具有周期性特征的岭回归（Ridge regression），并结合递归最小二乘法（RLS）实现在线自适应学习。

Result: 该方法在测试参数量减少80倍的情况下，在4个PEMS基准测试中占据3项最佳MAPE；在边缘设备上性能卓越，ESP32上单次预测与更新仅需2ms以内，且无需GPU算力。

Conclusion: 交通流量预测无需复杂的深度学习架构。基于递归最小二乘法（RLS）的轻量级线性模型在保持极高预测精度（MAPE）的同时，实现了极致的资源效率，能够完全在低成本边缘设备（如ESP32）上完成实时训练与预测。

Abstract: State-of-the-art traffic flow forecasting models, including Graph Convolutional Networks and graph-less MLPs, require centralized GPU training across all sensors, making them impractical for resource-constrained intelligent transportation deployments. We show that much of this complexity is unnecessary. A parametric analysis of the recent graph-less model GLMST reveals that reducing its internal embedding dimension from 64 to 4 degrades MAPE by less than one percentage point, suggesting that the model's effective capacity far exceeds what the task requires. Motivated by this finding, we replace the neural architecture entirely with per-sensor Ridge regression using horizon-aligned periodic features, combined with Recursive Least Squares (RLS) for online adaptation. With only 444 parameters per sensor (80x fewer than GLMST) and test-time online adaptation, our method achieves the best MAPE on three of four PEMS benchmarks, and remains within one percentage point on the fourth. Because each sensor's model is self-contained and involves only elementary linear algebra, the entire pipeline (training, inference, and online adaptation) runs on edge hardware without a GPU. An ESP32 microcontroller (160 MHz, 520 KB SRAM) completes cold-start training in 7.4s and each predict-and-update in under 2ms with zero heap allocation; a single Raspberry Pi 5 core completes cold-start training in 0.21s and each predict-and-update in 0.26ms.

</details>


### [63] [LUMEN: Coordinated Failure Recovery for Distributed LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17787)
*Zhang Cao,Shujie Han,Juncheng Zhang,Yuanming Ren,Yongkun Li,Patrick P. C. Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LUMEN是一种针对LLM推理集群的负载感知型故障恢复系统，通过优化检查点分布与故障处理逻辑，解决了传统方案因缺乏调度灵活性导致的恢复缓慢和资源浪费问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务系统在面对大规模GPU实例故障时存在缺陷：故障导致KV缓存丢失及计算能力下降；现有方案（如从头重算或简单的邻居检查点恢复）缺乏对集群负载的感知，导致恢复效率低下且恢复期间资源利用率不足。

Method: LUMEN系统：一种负载感知的协调系统，主要包含三项关键决策：1. 故障前的检查点放置策略；2. 故障发生时的中断请求分布式调度；3. 模型重载期间的分布式服务容量恢复。

Result: 通过原型实验和大规模仿真验证，LUMEN相较于现有方案显著缩短了服务恢复时间，并提升了整体服务性能。

Conclusion: 引入LUMEN系统作为大规模LLM服务集群的故障恢复解决方案，通过将检查点放置、请求重定向和模型重载阶段的容量恢复视为一个统一的负载感知协调问题，有效提升了系统的鲁棒性和恢复效率。

Abstract: Modern large language model (LLM) serving clusters distribute inference requests across multiple worker processes on different GPUs, but failures are prevalent at scale. When a worker fails, the cluster simultaneously loses the failed worker's GPU-resident key-value (KV) caches and serving capacity, leaving surviving workers to absorb the redirected traffic while re-running interrupted requests from scratch. Existing fault-tolerant systems either restart interrupted requests from scratch or restore KV caches from checkpoints stored on a fixed neighboring worker, but both approaches route recovery work without considering current cluster load and leave the recovering worker idle during model reload. We present LUMEN, a fault-tolerant LLM serving system that treats recovery as a load-aware coordination problem across three decision points: checkpoint placement before failures, interrupted-request distribution at failure time, and serving capacity restoration during model reload. We evaluate LUMEN using both prototype experiments and large-scale simulations and demonstrate significant improvements in serving and recovery times.

</details>


### [64] [An Epistemic Analysis of Random Coordinated Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17860)
*Sophia Knight,David Lehnherr,Sergio Rajsbaum*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种概率认识逻辑框架，用于分析有限轮次的随机分布式算法，填补了协同攻击问题在概率认识论视角的空白，并为 Varghese-Lynch 算法提供了严格的下界证明与逻辑形式化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 协同攻击问题（coordinated attack）在传统分布式计算中被证明是不可解的，但其随机版本是可解的。然而，缺乏从概率认识逻辑（probabilistic epistemic logic）视角对该随机版本的系统研究。作者旨在弥补这一理论空白，构建统一框架以分析随机分布式算法。

Method: 提出了一种用于研究有限轮次随机算法的概率认识逻辑框架，支持动态图模型（同步系统且存在 adversary 丢失消息）。该方法结合了动态网络逻辑表征、任务可解性分析以及概率动态认识逻辑，并在 Varghese-Lynch 的操作模型基础上进行了形式化。

Result: 成功建立了分析随机协同攻击、近似一致性（approximate agreement）和共识（consensus）的逻辑框架。对 Varghese-Lynch 算法进行了知识论层面的正式分析，强化并证明了其下界的紧凑性，并将原有的“信息层级”概念与其逻辑公式相对应。

Conclusion: 该研究确立了概率认识逻辑在随机分布式计算中的重要性。通过将 Varghese-Lynch 算法的“信息层级”概念形式化为认识逻辑公式，证明了在随机模型下利用不可区分性（indistinguishability）进行知识推理依然是分析算法复杂度和下界的核心手段，并给出了紧凑的下界。

Abstract: The coordinated attack problem models the challenge of coordinating a joint action within a bounded time by communicating over unreliable links. It was the first distributed computing problem proven unsolvable. Its analysis also revealed the importance of common knowledge, a central concept in epistemic logic. However, the randomized version of coordinated attack, which is solvable, has not, to the best of our knowledge, been studied through the lens of probabilistic epistemic logic, where processes generate randomness by flipping coins.
  We present an epistemic logic framework for studying randomized algorithms that execute for a bounded number of rounds. The framework applies to coordinated attack, approximate agreement, and consensus, and supports dynamic graph models: synchronous systems in which reliable processes execute a bounded number of rounds while an adversary determines which messages are lost.
  Our approach combines techniques from the logical characterization of dynamic networks and task solvability with ideas from probabilistic dynamic epistemic logic. It is inspired by the operational model of Varghese and Lynch for randomized coordinated attack. More broadly, the resulting notion of probabilistic epistemic task solvability provides a foundation for the epistemic study of randomized distributed computation.
  Using this framework, we analyze the Varghese-Lynch algorithm from a knowledge-theoretic perspective, providing a formal treatment of the algorithm and its lower bound. As a byproduct, we strengthen the lower bound and show it is tight. The proof relies on indistinguishability arguments, demonstrating that reasoning about knowledge remains essential in the probabilistic setting. We also formalize the notion of information level introduced by Varghese and Lynch, showing that it corresponds to a specific epistemic formula.

</details>


### [65] [RouteBalance: Fused Model Routing and Load Balancing for Heterogeneous LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17949)
*Wei Da,Evangelia Kalyvianaki*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: RouteBalance 融合了模型路由和负载均衡，通过联合调度优化异构 LLM 服务的质量、延迟和成本，显著优于传统的两层分离调度方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的异构 LLM 服务架构将调度分为两层且各自独立优化（路由器忽略负载，负载均衡器忽略质量），导致整体效率低下。

Method: 提出 RouteBalance，这是一种感知服务的调度层，将模型路由（基于质量/成本）与负载均衡（基于实例负载）合二为一，通过批处理内联预测器和死算实例状态降低决策代价。

Result: 在 13 实例、28 GPU 的异构集群上，RouteBalance 达到了质量-成本-吞吐量的前沿水平；在最高质量配置下，决策质量提升了 0.013（DeepEval）；在高负载情况下，吞吐量较现有基准方案提升了 2.6 至 4.1 倍。

Conclusion: RouteBalance 通过将模型路由与负载均衡合并，实现了在质量、延迟和成本之间的最优权衡。实验证明，该方法在处理异构模型请求时，显著优于现有的分层调度方法。

Abstract: Heterogeneous LLM serving stacks split scheduling into two layers that optimize in isolation: model routers pick a model from quality and cost signals while ignoring instance load, and serving load balancers optimize queues while ignoring quality. We present RouteBalance, a serving-aware scheduling layer that fuses both into a single online assignment over concrete model instances, jointly trading off quality, latency, and cost. A batched in-process predictor stack and dead-reckoned instance state keep the joint decision cheap on the request hot path ($\approx$32 ms at 12 req/s). On a 13-instance, 28-GPU heterogeneous cluster serving four model sizes, a single deployed RouteBalance stack traces the upper region of the three-way quality-cost-throughput frontier. Sweeping one weight vector reaches both the highest routing-decision quality (DeepEval $0.419$, $+0.013$ over the strongest baseline, $95\%$ CI $[{+}0.005,{+}0.022]$; the ordering holds when a second judge re-scores the actually served text) and, at its cost-priority corner, per-request cost that ties the cheapest baseline. With router engineering equalized against concurrent-scoring baseline variants we build, its balanced preset serves at $2.8$ s and $30$ req/s, leading $2.6$ to $4.1\times$ ahead of enhanced BEST-Route at high load. (Deploying those routers as published, one serial scoring call per request, makes them collapse $23\times$ under load, a deployment-architecture effect we isolate separately, not the routing result.) A four-arm isolation shows the benefit follows from pricing latency at model-selection time; the learned predictors contribute calibration and SLO headroom rather than the headline frontier. Code: https://github.com/AKafakA/route-balance

</details>


### [66] [Latency Prediction for LLM Inference on NPU Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18042)
*Juhyun Park,Seungwoo Jeong,Jingyu Lee,Kyungyong Lee*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: LENS 是一款专为 NPU 设计的 LLM 推理延迟预测器，通过简单的端到端测量技术，在无需了解硬件底层架构的情况下，有效解决了 NPU 推理中非线性延迟的预测难题，实现了 2.15% 的高精度预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大语言模型（LLM）部署中，配置空间庞大且穷举测量不可行，迫切需要高效的延迟预测。然而在NPU场景下，由于商用硬件微架构不开源、编译器优化不可预测以及“分库（bucketing）”导致的非线性延迟问题，现有方法均难以直接应用。

Method: 提出了一种基于端到端（E2E）测量的轻量级预测方法。该方法无需获知底层微架构或编译器信息，通过对每个“分桶”（bucket）进行两次E2E测量，并利用组合策略来预测任意输入-输出长度组合下的延迟，从而有效解决了延迟的非线性问题。

Result: 在多个厂商的NPU设备、多款LLM及多样化工作负载上进行了验证，LENS 的平均预测误差仅为 2.15%，且性能优于对比基准方法。

Conclusion: LENS 通过精准的建模策略，成功实现了在不依赖硬件底层细节的情况下，对多种商用NPU上大模型推理延迟的准确预测，具有较高的通用性和工业应用价值。

Abstract: Deploying Large Language Models (LLMs) requires exploring a large configuration space spanning parallelization strategies, batching techniques, and scheduling policies. Exhaustive measurement across this space is impractical, making latency prediction essential for system optimization. While NPUs have emerged as accelerators designed for LLM inference, no prediction methodology has been established for them. Specifically, applying prior work to LLM inference latency prediction on NPUs faces three challenges: undisclosed microarchitecture of commercial NPUs, unpredictable compiler optimizations, and latency non-linearity induced by bucketing.
  We present LENS, a latency estimator that predicts NPU inference latency without information on the microarchitecture or compiler, and captures the non-linear latency induced by bucketing. LENS profiles each bucket with two end-to-end (E2E) measurements and composes the results to predict latency for arbitrary input-output length combinations. We validate LENS across NPUs from multiple vendors, several LLMs, and diverse workloads, achieving a mean prediction error of 2.15\%. We further compare LENS against two methodologically related baselines, confirming the validity of its approach.

</details>

### [67] [The Right Call for Software Benchmarking: Consistent Decisions in Stateful Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17261)
*Gábor Melis*

Main category: cs.PF

TL;DR: 针对现代有状态计算系统导致的性能基准测试偏差，提出了一种基于对比测量而非绝对测量的统计方法，成功消除了系统偏差，实现了更稳健的性能优化决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代计算系统的各种有状态/自适应机制（如动态调频、缓存、资源调度等）导致了测量结果的时间依赖性，使得传统的绝对性能评估方法存在偏误，难以准确衡量软件性能。

Method: 指出性能测量的偏差源于有状态机制带来的时间依赖性；提出将基准测试重构为“识别最快程序”的决策问题，并设计了一套简单的实验方案，通过计算程序间的性能差异（Contrast）来抵消特定程序的偏差。

Result: 该方法在渐进意义下能确保做出正确的决策（即识别出最快程序），并能在存在系统偏差的现实环境中提供稳健且具有一致性的性能评估。

Conclusion: 在有状态计算环境中，通过采用基于“对比而非绝对值”的基准测试方法，可以有效抵消系统偏差，从而更准确地识别最优程序。这种方法为有限资源下的基准测试提供了一种稳健的理论方法论。

Abstract: In the perpetual pursuit of performance, modern computing systems rely ever more on stateful mechanisms to accommodate the dynamics of workloads and physical environments, bolstering efficiency but confounding benchmarking and thereby the optimization of software. Indeed, by their nature, adaptive mechanisms introduce temporal dependencies between measurements and render naive estimators of individual program performance biased. Observing that rectifying such biases necessitates speculative assumptions about system dynamics, we call for prioritizing performance differentials over absolute measures and formalize software benchmarking as the decision problem of identifying the fastest program, for which relative knowledge suffices. To this end, we propose simple experiment designs admitting consistent estimators of contrasts, whereby program-specific biases cancel under tenable assumptions. These designs asymptotically yield the correct decision and afford a robust methodology for finite-budget benchmarking in stateful environments, bearing broad implications for the development of performance-sensitive software.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [68] [Another Look at Log-PCA for Probability Measures: A Dynamical Formulation and Statistical Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17196)
*Peng Xu,Changbo Zhu,Young-Heon Kim,Xiaohui Chen*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于 Wasserstein 几何的切空间主成分分析 (WT-PCA)，利用动力学视角和最优传输的平行传输结构，实现了对 Wasserstein 空间中概率测度局部主变分的学习，并给出了相应的统计收敛率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解决 Wasserstein 空间中概率测度的主变分学习问题，克服传统 PCA 在非欧几里得几何结构下的局限性，并为 linearized PCA 提供更严谨的变分动力学解释。

Method: 通过动力学视角重新解释对数主成分分析（log-PCA），构建微分形式的 Wasserstein 切空间主成分分析（WT-PCA），利用最优传输的平行传输结构，在重心处通过协方差算子捕捉概率测度的局部主测地变分。

Conclusion: WT-PCA 能够有效地处理 Wasserstein 空间下的概率测度主变分学习，并提供了统计上的收敛性理论保证，为高维数据流形学习提供了一个微分几何视角下的分析工具。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with learning principal variations of random probability measures on $\mathbb{R}^m$ under the Wasserstein geometry. Our differentiable version, termed as the Wasserstein Tangential PCA (WT-PCA), captures the local principal modes of geodesic variations of a (weighted) probability measure on the Wasserstein space via its covariance operator at barycenter. Based on the dynamical perspective and leveraging parallel transport structure of the optimal transport problems, we derive a general statistical convergence rate of the empirical WT-PCA when estimated from data in terms of the 2-Wasserstein distance between the population and empirical barycenter reference measures.

</details>


### [69] [Tight $L_\infty$ Sample Complexity for Low-Degree and Sparse Boolean Polynomials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17319)
