Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了在带噪环境下学习布尔超立方体上多项式代理的样本复杂度。通过使用辅助范数克服了 $L_\infty$ 误差控制的困难，证明了其样本复杂度（度数 $d$ 的 $n^{d+1}$ 和稀疏度 $s$ 的 $ns^2$）在噪声存在时比无噪环境有本质上的增加，且对自适应学习者同样适用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为了优化有界二进制黑盒函数，需要学习布尔超立方体上的多项式代理。为保证代理的优化效果，必须放弃常见的 $L_2$ 误差保证，转而追求均匀的 $L_\infty$ 误差保证，并探究带噪环境下的样本复杂度。

Method: 利用辅助范数（auxiliary norms）作为 $L_\infty$ 范数的代理来克服传统傅里叶分析方法在 $L_\infty$ 设置下的局限性，并针对受限多项式类证明了极小极大样本复杂度下界。

Result: 对于度数最多为 $d$ 的 $n$ 变量多项式，样本复杂度为 $n^{d+1}$；对于 $s$-稀疏 Fourier-Walsh 多项式 ($s \leq n$)，样本复杂度为 $ns^2$。这些结果均在自适应学习者下成立。

Conclusion: 该论文通过引入辅助范数作为无穷范数的代理，确立了在带噪环境下学习优化安全多项式代理的样本复杂度下界，证明了噪声环境下学习多项式代理存在本质上比无噪情形更困难的样本复杂度代价。

Abstract: Motivated by the optimization of bounded binary black-box functions, we study the problem of learning polynomial surrogates over the Boolean hypercube. To ensure that optimizing the surrogate yields good solutions for the underlying objective, we require uniform $L_\infty$-error guarantees rather than the usual $L_2$-type guarantees. We characterize the minimax sample complexity of uniform estimation under subgaussian noise for two classes of bounded polynomials. First, for polynomials of degree at most $d$ on $n$ variables, the sample complexity scales as $n^{d+1}$. Second, for $s$-sparse Fourier-Walsh polynomials with $s \leq n$, it scales as $ns^2$. These rates differ structurally from the noiseless setting, where uniform exact recovery scales as $n^d$ and $ns$, respectively. Our lower bounds hold even for arbitrary adaptive learners, showing that the additional factors are intrinsic to the noisy cases. Standard Fourier-analysis tools for the $L_2$-norm do not naturally extend to the $L_\infty$-setting in a way that yields uniform guarantees. Our proofs overcome this difficulty by relying on suitably chosen auxiliary norms that serve as proxies for controlling the $L_\infty$-error. Together, our results provide a tight characterization of the sample complexity of learning optimization-safe polynomial surrogates.

</details>


### [70] [Bounded Difference Concentration for Infinitely Exchangeable Sequences with Applications to AI Benchmark Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17426)
*Fangyuan Lin,Spencer Frei,Victor H. de la Pena*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了无限可交换随机变量的浓度性质，提出了一种通用的分解框架，证明了在零和线性对比下潜在混合波动可完全抵消，从而获得了紧致的性能误差边界，并将其成功应用于AI模型基准测试的评估优化中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有浓度不等式通常假设独立性，无法直接处理包含潜在依赖结构（如可交换性）的复杂数据。在AI基准测试（如MMLU）中，题目通常服从可交换分布，现有方法难以准确刻画此类复杂任务的性能评价不确定性。

Method: 利用de Finetti表示定理，对无限可交换变量函数进行条件化处理；将偏差分解为条件采样波动和潜在混合波动，并通过分析两者的方差代理（Variance Proxy）推导出组合浓度不等式，进而证明在特定对比中混合项可精确抵消。

Result: 推导得出了方差代理为 $\frac{1}{4}\sum_i c_i^2 + σ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2$ 的浓度不等式；对于零和线性对比，证明了潜在混合项完全消失，得到了形式简洁且紧致的Hoeffding型界，并成功应用于MMLU基准测试的性能波动量化。

Conclusion: 研究确立了针对无限可交换随机变量函数的一般浓度不等式。通过证明零和线性对比中潜在混合项的精确抵消，为机器学习基准测试（如MMLU）的性能评估提供了紧致、无分布限制的概率边界，从而在理论上为基准测试的子集评估提供了统计保证。

Abstract: We consider the concentration properties of functions of infinitely exchangeable random variables. By conditioning on the de Finetti directing measure, we show that the deviation of any function with bounded-difference constants $c_1, \dots, c_n$ decomposes into a conditional sampling fluctuation and a latent mixture fluctuation. When this latent mixture is $σ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2$-subgaussian, we establish a concentration inequality with an effective variance proxy of $\frac{1}{4}\sum_i c_i^2 + σ_{\mathrm{mix}}^2$. Crucially, we demonstrate that for zero-sum linear contrasts, such as the difference between a subsample mean and a full population mean, the latent mixture term cancels exactly. This cancellation yields a tight, mixture-free Hoeffding-type bound that provides a direct de Finetti mechanism for the infinite-extendibility limit of recent finite-exchangeable concentration results. We apply this framework to quantify uncertainty in composite AI benchmarks, such as MMLU, where question items naturally exhibit exchangeable dependence across domains. Our results provide both a domain-stratified hierarchical model for bounding the uncertainty of accuracy scores, and a distribution-free, cost-saving statistical guarantee for accurately estimating full benchmark scores from random subsets.

</details>


### [71] [A Bayesian Boolean Matrix Factorization with Application to Copy Number Analysis in Cancer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17491)
*Adolphus Wagala,Mehmet Samur,Giovanni Parmigiani*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: BBMF通过贝叶斯框架解决了现有布尔矩阵分解方法的局限性，提供了鲁棒的、可解释的二进制数据分解方案，并成功揭示了癌症基因组中的离散潜在结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的布尔矩阵分解（BooMF）方法大多基于启发式或贪心算法，容易陷入局部最优，难以进行模型选择和不确定性量化。此外，传统的实值矩阵分解忽略了数据的离散性，缺乏可解释性，不适用于捕捉癌症基因组中常见的离散模式。

Method: 提出了一种贝叶斯布尔矩阵分解（BBMF）模型。该模型是一个全共轭生成模型，采用了稀疏诱导先验（sparsity-inducing priors），强制执行布尔约束，并利用吉布斯采样（Gibbs sampling）进行推断，具备闭式全条件概率分布。

Result: BBMF在多发性骨髓瘤的染色体臂级拷贝数变异数据上进行了应用。实验结果表明，该模型能够识别出一组可解释的“双团”（bicliques），有效地将患者亚群与反复发生的染色体臂协同变异关联起来，成功提取了肿瘤异质性的紧凑且具有生物学意义的摘要。

Conclusion: BBMF提供了一种有效、可解释且具有不确定性量化能力的二进制矩阵分解方法，在分析癌症基因组等复杂二进制数据时，能比传统的加性分解方法更自然地捕捉离散的潜在结构，具有很高的生物学应用价值。

Abstract: Binary data factorization is common, but real-valued methods ignore discreteness and yield hard-to-interpret factors. Boolean Matrix Factorization (BooMF) instead decomposes a binary matrix into two lower-rank binary matrices via logical AND and OR, expressing the data as a Boolean disjunction of interpretable patterns. In cancer genomics, BooMF can reveal coordinated feature changes that may drive tumor evolution, unlike rotational or additive decompositions. Most existing BooMF methods are heuristic, greedy, sensitive to initialization, prone to local optima, and do not support principled model selection or uncertainty quantification. We introduce Bayesian Boolean Matrix Factorization (BBMF), a fully conjugate generative model with sparsity-inducing priors. It enforces Boolean constraints, yields interpretable latent factors with coherent uncertainty quantification, and admits Gibbs sampling with closed-form full conditionals. Because cancer evolution often involves widespread, near-simultaneous chromosome-number changes (e.g., whole-genome duplication followed by instability and selection), Boolean factorizations capture these patterns more naturally than additive models. Applied to arm-level copy-number alteration data in multiple myeloma, where entries indicate presence/absence of chromosomal-arm amplifications, BBMF finds a small set of interpretable bicliques linking patient subsets to recurrently co-altered chromosomal arms, providing a compact, biologically meaningful summary of tumor heterogeneity and demonstrating BBMF's utility for uncovering discrete latent structure in complex binary data.

</details>


### [72] [Geometrical fairness in graph neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17684)
*Arturo Pérez-Peralta,Sandra Benítez-Peña,Blas Kolic,Rosa E. Lillo*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 针对图扩散模型可能放大数据偏见的问题，本文提出了一种基于修改拉普拉斯算子（结合多重变换）的公平性优化框架，实现了公平性与预测性能的有效平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的图学习模型虽然性能强劲，但在处理过程中可能放大数据中的偏见，缺乏有效的公平性保障。

Method: 通过修改拉普拉斯算子来引入公平性约束，具体包括子空间投影、谱调整和基于频率的滤波等互补变换。

Result: 在合成及真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在提升公平性指标的同时，模型性能保持在竞争水平，且产生的计算额外开销有限。

Conclusion: 提出的公平感知图扩散框架在保持竞争性性能的同时，能有效缓解图学习中的偏见问题，并具有合理的计算效率。

Abstract: Graph-based learning methods have become increasingly prominent due to their strong performance across diverse applications. Among these, recent frameworks grounded in diffusion processes provide a unifying perspective that extends traditional graph neural network formulations while addressing limitations of standard message-passing mechanisms. Despite these advances, concerns remain regarding the fairness of such models, as they may propagate or amplify biases present in the data. In this work, we introduce a fairness-aware adaptation of graph-based diffusion by modifying the underlying Laplacian operator. Our approach incorporates multiple complementary transformations, including subspace projections, spectral adjustments, and frequency-based filtering, to mitigate bias-related components. Leveraging the intrinsic smoothing properties of graph diffusion, we provide a principled analysis of the resulting behavior and establish theoretical insights into fairness properties. We evaluate the proposed framework on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating that it achieves competitive performance while improving fairness metrics with limited additional computational cost.

</details>


### [73] [Differential Privacy of Gaussian Process Posterior Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17995)
*Tomasz Maciazek*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文证明了高斯过程（GP）后验采样自带差分隐私属性，其隐私性取决于岭正则化程度，并提出通过调节正则化及增加校准噪声来平衡隐私与模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的差分隐私机制通常依赖于添加外部噪声，作者旨在研究高斯过程（GP）本身固有的后验采样随机性是否可以提供隐私保障，从而避免引入额外的外部噪声对模型效用造成的影响。

Method: 1. 理论推导：推导出高斯过程后验采样路径的 Rényi-DP 边界，并将后验均值泄漏与依赖于数据的协方差泄漏相分离；2. 攻击验证：采用成员推理攻击（MIA）验证隐私泄漏与正则化强度、后验方差及采样路径数量的关系；3. 效用分析：通过下游任务实验评估隐私保护下的决策效用。

Result: 研究表明 GP 后验采样具有内在的差分隐私保障，且该隐私水平高度依赖于岭正则化（ridge regularisation）的强度；实验证明，在适当的正则化下，可以在保持有用决策的同时实现隐私保护，且通过额外增加校准噪声可以进一步增强隐私保护。

Conclusion: 高斯过程的后验采样本身具有差分隐私（DP）特性；通过调节正则化参数或增加校准噪声，可以在隐私保护与模型效用之间实现有效的权衡。

Abstract: We study the privacy of releasing posterior sample paths from a Gaussian process (GP) when the entire training set including covariates and responses is private. Unlike standard differential-privacy (DP) mechanisms that add external noise, posterior sampling is random by construction. We show that this intrinsic randomness yields DP guarantees by deriving explicit Rényi-DP bounds for GP posterior sample-path release. The bounds separate posterior-mean leakage from data-dependent posterior-covariance leakage showing that meaningful privacy depends sharply on effective ridge regularisation. We apply membership-inference attacks to show that empirical leakage follows the predicted dependence on regularisation, posterior variance and the number of released posterior sample-paths. Utility experiments on downstream posterior-sampling tasks identify noisy-observation regimes where privacy-compatible regularisation preserves useful decisions with modest utility loss. When stronger privacy is needed, the intrinsic guarantee can be sharpened by adding calibrated GP noise, providing an explicit additional privacy knob.

</details>


### [74] [Fast Nonparametric Conditional Independence Testing via Two-Stage Regression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18011)
*Eric V. Strobl*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: BLITZ 是一种兼顾速度与精度的非参数条件独立性检验方法，通过“由宽到窄”的残差化策略解决了大规模因果发现中的计算与校准瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约束类因果发现算法依赖大量的条件独立性测试，但现有的高性能非参数测试方法在处理具有复杂非线性依赖的数据时，往往面临计算速度与结果校准度（Calibration）难以兼顾的问题。

Method: 提出 BLITZ（Broad-to-Local Independence Testing via residualiZation）算法：首先利用低阶多项式回归去除对调节集的广泛平滑依赖，随后引入轻量非线性特征映射，并使用浅层树回归对这些特征进行残差化，最后通过矩匹配卡方近似进行显著性检验。

Result: 在模拟实验中，BLITZ 表现出比内核方法、随机特征法和回归法更好的零假设校准能力，且运行速度极快；在因果发现任务中，它在图结构恢复和边方向确定方面提供了更可靠的结果。

Conclusion: BLITZ 证明了“由宽到窄”（Broad-to-Local）的残差化策略是一种实用的、可扩展且校准良好的非参数条件独立性测试方法，非常适合因果发现算法。

Abstract: Constraint-based causal discovery relies on repeated conditional independence tests, but fast nonparametric tests often sacrifice calibration, especially when variables depend on the conditioning set through nonlinear relationships. We introduce BLITZ (Broad-to-Local Independence Testing via residualiZation), a nonparametric conditional independence test designed to run well under a second while maintaining the accuracy needed for the thousands of queries performed by constraint-based causal discovery algorithms. BLITZ first removes broad smooth dependence on the conditioning set using low-order polynomial regression, then applies a small nonlinear feature map and residualizes those features with shallow tree regressions. The resulting statistic tests residual cross-covariance, with a moment-matched chi-square approximation to the null distribution. We show theoretically that the two-stage design reduces the effective complexity faced by the tree residualizers, allowing shallow trees to control residual conditional-mean bias while avoiding excessive overfitting. In simulations, BLITZ provides better null calibration than fast kernel, random-feature, and regression-based competitors while remaining among the fastest methods tested. In causal discovery experiments on synthetic graphs and flow-cytometry data, BLITZ yields more reliable endpoint orientations among retained adjacencies and competitive structural recovery. These results suggest that broad-to-local residualization is a practical route to calibrated, scalable nonparametric conditional independence testing for causal discovery.

</details>


### [75] [Tensor-based second-order causal discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18074)
*Nathan Ouyang,Kexin Wan,Anna Seigal*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: TSCD 是一种通过利用观测和干预数据的协方差矩阵（二阶统计量）进行因果发现的新算法，具有高效、鲁棒且可扩展至大规模变量的特点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种结合观测数据和干预数据的方法，以克服传统方法在统计与计算效率、对高阶矩依赖、以及对高斯分布假设限制上的不足。

Method: 提出了一种基于张量的二阶因果发现 (TSCD) 算法；使用观察数据和干预数据的协方差矩阵构建张量作为输入；在模型假设下（线性结构方程模型、非相关噪声），通过二阶统计量识别 DAG 及边函数；并扩展了非线性版本。

Result: 理论证明了在对数数量级的干预下即可实现因果顺序和参数的可识别性；实验表明该算法对噪声鲁棒，具有较强的竞争优势，且能够扩展至数百个变量。

Conclusion: TSCD 是一种高效的因果发现方法，利用二阶统计量（协方差矩阵）在保证统计和计算效率的同时，实现了对线性及部分非线性模型下因果结构和参数的可识别性，且具有良好的鲁棒性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Causal discovery seeks to uncover the causal dependencies among variables. For this purpose, we propose an algorithm called Tensor-based Second-order Causal Discovery (TSCD). Its input is a tensor obtained from the covariance matrices of observational and interventional data. Assuming the causal dependencies follow a linear structural equation model on a directed acyclic graph (DAG), TSCD outputs the DAG and the functions on its edges, requiring only that the noise variables are uncorrelated. We also implement a version of the approach for nonlinear models. Our focus on second-order statistics (via the covariance matrices) is motivated by their statistical and computational efficiency relative to higher-order moments, their identifiability relative to first-order statistics, and that they work regardless of whether the variables are Gaussian. We show that TSCD has identifiable causal order and parameters from a number of interventions that is logarithmic in the number of variables. Experiments show that TSCD is robust to noise, competitive with existing methods, and scales to hundreds of variables.

</details>


### [76] [A Diffusion Approximation for Temporal-Difference Learning with Linear Features under Markovian Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18183)
*M. Forzo,E. Monzio Compagnoni,A. Russo,A. Pacchiano*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本研究通过随机微分方程（SDE）替代传统的ODE来建模线性TD(0)，成功量化了马尔可夫采样噪声对算法常数步长误差下界的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的常微分方程（ODE）描述仅捕获渐近均值动力学，忽略了决定算法误差下界的随机波动，无法全面解释常数步长下的收敛误差问题。

Method: 引入随机微分方程（SDE）近似描述马尔可夫噪声下的线性TD(0)算法，不再仅依赖于传统的常微分方程（ODE）平均动力学。

Result: 成功构建了能够区分投影贝尔曼算子带来的收缩动力学与马尔可夫采样影响的SDE模型，并解释了误差下界的产生机理。

Conclusion: 通过随机微分方程（SDE）建模，揭示了线性TD(0)算法中由马尔可夫采样噪声与投影贝尔曼算子收缩几何特性相互作用导致的常数步长误差下界（error floor）机制。

Abstract: Temporal difference (TD) learning with linear function approximation is a core method for policy evaluation. Its classical continuous-time description is an ordinary differential equation (ODE), which captures the asymptotic mean dynamics but neglects stochastic fluctuations determining the error floor. We introduce a stochastic differential equation (SDE) approximation for linear TD(0) under Markovian noise. The resulting model distinguishes the contraction dynamics governed by the projected Bellman operator from the influence of Markovian sampling. As a consequence, the model explains the constant-stepsize error floor through the interaction between Markovian long-run covariance and the contraction geometry of the projected Bellman operator.

</details>

### [77] [Scalable K-clique Estimation with Differential Privacy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17297)
*Dung Nguyen,Ritwick Mishra,Anil Vullikanti*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种高效的差分隐私$k$-团计数算法，通过近似敏感度框架大幅降低了计算开销，克服了现有方法在处理大规模图时的性能瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的$k$-团差分隐私计数方法（如平滑敏感度和梯形函数）在$k>3$时计算代价极高，且无法在超大规模图上实际应用。

Method: 利用梯形函数作为局部敏感度的平滑上界，结合近似敏感度（approximation sensitivity）框架来校准噪声，从而避免了直接计算高复杂度敏感度带来的计算开销。

Result: 相比于传统的梯形函数方法，本算法的运行速度提升了几个数量级，且首次实现了在百万级边规模图上进行$k$-团的差分隐私计数。

Conclusion: 本文提出的算法通过近似敏感度框架成功解决了$k$-团计数在差分隐私保护下的计算效率瓶颈，实现了在百万级边规模图上的高效扩展，且精度与现有方法相当。

Abstract: Counts of $k$-cliques are commonly used metrics in subgraph mining. Since graphs often have sensitive data, there also has been a lot of work on $k$-clique counts with differential privacy. However, these metrics have very high global sensitivity, and so more sophisticated techniques have been developed for counting $k$-cliques with privacy. Smooth sensitivity and ladder functions were developed for reducing the noise magnitude for private estimates of these metrics. However, these are computationally very inefficient to estimate. No polynomial time algorithms are known for smooth sensitivity of $k$-cliques for $k>3$, while the time complexity of ladder functions is lower bounded by the time for exact counts, which does not scale very well.
  In this paper, we develop a new highly scalable algorithm for estimating $k$-clique counts with differential privacy. Our algorithm adapts the ladder function to serve as a smooth upper bound on its local sensitivity, and utilizes the approximation sensitivity framework to calibrate noise with magnitude proportional to an approximation of the bound. This gives us a significant improvement in the running time. Experiments show that our method is several orders of magnitude faster than the ladder function based estimates of $k$-clique counts, while the accuracy is similar. Our algorithm is the first to scale to graphs with millions of edges, and for larger $k$, for which the ladder function algorithm doesn't complete.

</details>


### [78] [Approximation Preserving Coresets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17316)
*Milind Prabhu,Chris Schwiegelshohn,Sudarshan Shyam*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对大数据聚类，论文提出通过“近似保持核心集”仅关注低代价的好解，从而在保证质量的前提下显著减小核心集尺寸，填补了现有理论在强与弱核心集之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大数据聚类场景中，传统的强核心集理论保证尺寸过大，而实际应用中较小尺寸的核心集往往已足够。论文旨在从理论上解释这一现象，即较小的核心集足以满足那些具有低代价的“好解”。

Method: 定义并构造了“近似保持核心集”（approximation-preserving coresets），该方法介于强核心集（针对所有解）和弱核心集（仅针对最优解）之间，并配合理论证明说明了一定程度的失真限制下核心集尺寸的必然性。

Result: 成功定义并构建了近似保持核心集，证明了其在面对“好解”时具有更优的性能特性，同时证明了即便在很小的近似失真条件下，核心集的尺寸也存在不可逾越的下界。

Conclusion: 论文引入了“近似保持核心集”（approximation-preserving coresets），证明了通过仅关注具有低代价的“好解”，可以实现比强核心集尺寸更小且比弱核心集保证更强的权衡，并给出了相应的理论下界。

Abstract: Clustering in a big data setting is an intensively studied problem, with coresets emerging as one of the important paradigms in this line of work. Given a cost function $\text{cost}(P,S)$ mapping input points $P$ and a solution $S$ to an objective value, a coreset is a typically weighted sketch $Ω\subseteq P$ such that $\text{cost}(Ω,S)\approx \text{cost}(P,S)$. In practice, coreset sizes much smaller than those suggested by theoretical guarantees are often found to be sufficient.
  In this paper, we offer an explanation for this phenomenon. Smaller coreset sizes suffice if we only wish to preserve the costs of \emph{good} solutions, i.e., solutions with low cost. We define and devise \emph{approximation-preserving coresets}, which provide a weaker guarantee than strong coresets, which apply to all solutions, while providing stronger guarantees than weak coresets, which apply only to the optimum solution. We complement this result by showing that even a very small distortion in the approximation factor cannot admit coresets of this size.

</details>


### [79] [Exact Algorithms for Edge Deletion to Cactus Graphs and Weighted Variants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17578)
*Wenhao Song*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 该论文针对“边删除至仙人掌图”问题提出了改进的指数时间算法，通过优化算法设计，将无权图的复杂度由 $O^*(3^n)$ 降低至 $O^*(2^n)$，并给出了处理加权问题的多种复杂度优化方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在改进既有算法在解决“边删除至仙人掌图（Edge Deletion to Cactus）”这一NP-hard问题时的运行时间复杂度。

Method: 利用动态规划以及针对特定图结构（仙人掌图）的指数时间算法设计，结合子集卷积和状态压缩技术来处理连通性约束。

Result: 对于无权情况，算法复杂度提升至 $O^*(2^n)$；对于具有 $q$ 个不同非负权重的情况，复杂度为 $O^*(2^n n^{O(q)})$；针对整数权重，给出了伪多项式时间算法。

Conclusion: 论文通过动态规划与子集卷积技巧，显著提升了“边删除至仙人掌图”问题的算法复杂度，将其从 $O^*(3^n)$ 提升至 $O^*(2^n)$，并针对不同权重情形给出了相应的算法。

Abstract: We study exact exponential-time algorithms for Edge Deletion to Cactus. Given a connected graph $G$, the task is to delete a minimum number of edges so that the remaining spanning graph is a connected cactus. Akhtar and Philip (IWOCA 2026) gave an $O^*(3^n)$-time algorithm for the unweighted problem, where $n$ is the number of vertices in the input graph and the $O^*(\cdot)$ notation hides polynomial factors.
  We improve this bound to $O^*(2^n)$ time and space. More generally, if the deletion costs take at most $q$ distinct nonnegative real values, then the weighted problem can be solved in $O^*(2^n n^{O(q)})$ time and space. Thus every fixed number of distinct costs, and in particular the unweighted case, admits a faster exact algorithm. For nonnegative integer costs of total weight $W$, we obtain an $O^*(2^n(W+1))$ pseudo-polynomial algorithm, while arbitrary nonnegative real costs admit an $O^*(3^n)$ exact algorithm.

</details>


### [80] [Spectral recovery of a planted triangle-dense subgraph](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17604)
*Sam van der Poel,Cheng Mao,Benjamin McKenna*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了在随机图中恢复植入三角密集子图的问题，提出了基于局部三角计数的谱算法和 SDP 方法，并证明了该问题存在类似于“植入团”问题的统计与计算差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三角密集 $k$-子图问题在最坏情况下计算复杂度高，因此作者在 Erdős-Rényi 随机图模型中进行“植入”分析，旨在寻找处理该问题的有效算法并探讨其计算难度。

Method: 提出了两种用于恢复植入子图的算法：一种是基于局部三角计数（local signed triangle counts）构建特定图矩阵的谱算法，另一种是相应的半正定规划（SDP）方法。通过对该图矩阵进行谱分析来建立理论保证。

Result: 证明了所提出的谱算法和 SDP 可以在特定条件下有效恢复植入的子图，并通过分析确立了算法在计算复杂性上的限制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了三角密集子图恢复问题中存在统计与计算之间的差距。在低度多项式算法框架下，计算阈值为 $k \geq \sqrt{n}$，而信息论极限为 $k \approx O(\log n)$。

Abstract: Given a simple graph on $n$ vertices and a parameter $k$, the triangle-densest-$k$-subgraph problem is known to be computationally hard in the worst case. To circumvent the computational hardness, we study an average-case model where a triangle-dense subgraph on $k$ vertices is planted in an Erdős-Rényi random graph on $n$ vertices. For the recovery of the planted subgraph, we propose a simple spectral algorithm and a semidefinite program, both of which use a graph matrix whose entries are local signed triangle counts. Theoretical guarantees for these algorithms are established through spectral analysis of the graph matrix. Finally, we provide evidence showing a statistical-to-computational gap analogous to that for the planted clique problem. The computational threshold in terms of the subgraph size $k$ is at least $\sqrt{n}$ in the framework of low-degree polynomial algorithms, while the information-theoretic threshold is at most logarithmic in $n$.

</details>


### [81] [Four-Cycle Counting in Low-Degeneracy Graph Streams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17732)
*Sebastian Lüderssen,Stefan Neumann,Pan Peng*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 针对图流中四环计数问题，本文提出了两种采样算法，实现了理论上的最优复杂度和实用中的高效节省空间，并有效解决了非二分图场景下的计数挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在任意顺序的边流（edge streams）模型中，现有的四环（four-cycles）计数估计算法在空间复杂度和适用性上仍有提升空间，尤其是在处理非二分图和稀疏社交网络时。

Method: 提出了两种基于诱导子图采样的新算法：1. 一个两遍算法（two-pass algorithm），利用图的退化度（degeneracy）进行空间优化；2. 一个单遍算法（one-pass algorithm），适用于四环在图中分布相对均匀的场景。

Result: 在理论上，两遍算法的空间复杂度达到了 $\widetilde{O}(κm / \sqrt{T})$，在常数退化度图上与下界匹配；实证方面，在多种真实图流数据集上，两遍算法在大幅节省空间的同时保持了高精度，单遍算法在特定稀疏网络中表现出色。

Conclusion: 本文提出的两种算法在处理图流中的四环计数问题上表现优异。两遍算法在常数退化度图上达到了理论上的最优性，而单遍算法在四环分布均匀的稀疏图中提供了极具竞争力的实用性能。这些方法成功克服了以往研究在非二分图处理上的局限性。

Abstract: We study the problem of $(1+\varepsilon)$-approximating the number of four-cycles in graphs given as arbitrary order edge streams. We propose two new algorithms based on sampling induced subgraphs. Our first contribution is a two-pass algorithm that uses $\widetilde{O}(κm / \sqrt{T})$ space, where $m$ is the number of edges, $T$ is the number of four-cycles, and $κ$ is the graph's degeneracy. This algorithm improves upon existing theoretical bounds and is provably optimal for constant-degeneracy graphs, matching the known $Ω(m/\sqrt{T})$ lower bound up to lower-order factors. Our second contribution is a one-pass algorithm that remains accurate when four-cycles are not highly concentrated around individual nodes, edges, or wedges; this structural property is common in sparse social and collaboration networks. We evaluate both algorithms on a variety of real-world graph streams. The two-pass algorithm consistently outperforms state-of-the-art methods, using substantially less space to achieve a desired accuracy. The one-pass algorithm is competitive when four-cycles are evenly distributed, matching our theoretical analysis. Unlike several recent works, our algorithms perform well even on non-bipartite graphs such as social networks.

</details>


### [82] [Reducing Prize-Collecting Stroll and Related Routing Problems to Prize-Collecting TSP](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18157)
*Hong Li*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文通过引入 Prize-Collecting-$\Phi$-TSP 这一泛化模型，证明了在预定义点集规模受限时，Prize-Collecting TSP 的算法可转化为该问题的 $(\rho+\varepsilon)$ 近似，从而将 Price-Collecting Stroll 的近似比从 1.6662 提升至 1.6 以内。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决 Prize-Collecting Stroll 及相关路径路由问题的近似算法最优性问题，旨在通过统一的泛化模型（Prize-Collecting-$\Phi$-TSP）改进现有算法的近似比。

Method: 构建了一个通用的近似算法框架，证明了若存在针对 Prize-Collecting TSP 的 $\rho$-近似算法，则对于固定的预定义定点数，可以得到 Prize-Collecting-$\Phi$-TSP 的 $(\rho+\varepsilon)$-近似算法。

Result: 对于 Prize-Collecting-$\Phi$-TSP，在预定义定点数有限的情况下，证明了多项式时间内 $(\rho+\varepsilon)$ 近似算法的存在性，并具体将 Prize-Collecting Stroll 的近似比优化至 1.6 以内。

Conclusion: 该文提出了针对 prize-collecting-$\Phi$-TSP 的强近似算法框架，当预定义定点数为常数时，实现了对 Prize-Collecting Stroll 的改进近似比（优于1.6），超过了此前 1.6662 的记录。

Abstract: The prize-collecting stroll is the path version of the prize-collecting TSP. Given a complete metric graph, two prescribed terminal vertices $s,t$, and nonnegative penalties on vertices, the prize-collecting stroll asks for an $s$-$t$ tour minimizing the length of the tour plus the total penalty of vertices that are not visited by the $s,t$ tour. We study a common generalization of the prize-collecting stroll and several related prize-collecting routing problems, which we call the prize-collecting-$Φ$-TSP. In this model, $Φ$ specifies a set of prescribed vertices alongside their parity and connectivity requirements. We show that, if a $ρ$-approximation algorithm for the prize-collecting TSP is available, then, for any $\varepsilon>0$, there is a polynomial time $(ρ+\varepsilon)$-approximation algorithm for the prize-collecting-$Φ$-TSP when the number of prescribed vertices is bounded by a fixed constant. This yields a better-than-$1.6$-approximation algorithm for the prize-collecting stroll, improving the previous best-known approximation guarantee of $1.6662$.

</details>


### [83] [Online Connectivity Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18160)
*Mohit Garg,Aditya Subramanian*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文研究了在线连通性增强问题（Online CAP），提出了一种针对动态$(k+1)$-边连通性请求的算法，证明了该算法在竞争比上达到了最优（紧凑界限），改进了该领域已有的研究成果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 连通性增强问题（CAP）是容错网络设计中的核心问题，尽管离线版本已有广泛研究，但在在线设定下，如何以最小的代价满足动态的$(k+1)$-边连通性需求仍需进一步优化以获得更优的性能保证。

Method: 本文研究了在线环境下的连通性增强问题，即在一个初始$k$-边连通的图中，响应关于$(k+1)$-边连通性的动态请求。该算法需要在请求到达时立即且不可撤销地添加链接，以最小化总成本。研究分析了算法的竞争比，并提出了优于现有界限的紧凑界限。

Result: 获得了在线CAP问题的紧凑竞争比，并给出了其他相关理论结果，改进了既往文献中的性能界限。

Conclusion: 本文针对在线连通性增强问题（Online CAP）取得了突破性进展，推导出了紧凑的竞争比（tight competitive ratio），解决了该领域的关键理论问题。

Abstract: The Connectivity Augmentation Problem (CAP) is a fundamental problem in fault-tolerant network design and has been extensively studied in the context of approximation algorithms. In this work, we consider CAP in the online setting: given a $k$-edge-connected graph $G$ and a set $L$ of additional edges over the vertices of $G$, called links, online requests arrive one by one, each specifying two vertices that need to be $(k+1)$-edge-connected. We start with the graph $G$ and progressively add links to serve these requests. More specifically, upon the arrival of a request $\{u,v\}$, we must immediately and irrevocably add zero or more links from $L$ to the graph so that $u$ and $v$ are $(k+1)$-edge-connected in the resulting augmented graph. The goal is to minimize the total number of links added, and we evaluate an algorithm's performance by its competitive ratio relative to an optimal offline solution. In this work, improving upon previous bounds, we obtain a tight competitive ratio for online CAP, along with other related results.

</details>


### [84] [Directed Reachability-Preserving Minimum Edge Cut: Approximation and Planar Hardness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18225)
*Qi Duan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 研究了有向图三端可达性保持最小割（RPMEC）问题，提供了针对一般图和无环图的近似算法，并证明了该问题在平面图上的计算复杂度为 NP-难。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究有向图中如何在保持 $s_1$ 到 $s_2$ 可达性的同时，切断 $s_1$ 到 $t$ 的可达性，并寻找最小化代价的弧集。

Method: 提出了一种基于根有向割函数（rooted directed cut function）的路径-割公式；利用多面体（polymatroid）的根线性近似方法；针对无环图提出了单点长度算法。通过规约独立集问题证明了平面图RPMEC的NP-难性。

Result: 在一般有向图中实现了 $O(\sqrt{n})$ 近似比；在无环有向图中根据相关顶点数 $r$ 和路径长度 $h$ 实现了 $O(\min\{\sqrt{r}, h\})$ 的近似比；证明了即使是无环平面图，该问题依然是 NP-难的。

Conclusion: 该研究探讨了有向三端可达性保持最小割问题（RPMEC），证明了在一般有向图中的近似性能，给出了针对无环图的优化算法，并确立了平面图情形下的NP-难性。

Abstract: We study a directed version of the three-terminal reachability-preserving minimum edge cut problem. Given a directed graph $G=(V,A)$ with arc costs and terminals $s_1,s_2,t$, the one-way directed RPMEC problem asks for a minimum-cost set of arcs whose deletion preserves the reachability $s_1\leadsto s_2$ while destroying the reachability $s_1\leadsto t$. We first give a path--cut formulation in terms of a rooted directed cut function. Using a root-linear approximation for the associated polymatroid, we obtain an $O(\sqrt r)$-approximation, where $r$ is the number of relevant vertices with positive singleton cut value. In particular this gives an $O(\sqrt n)$-approximation in general directed graphs. For acyclic directed graphs, we give an additional singleton-length algorithm and obtain an $O(\min\{\sqrt r,h\})$ guarantee, where $h$ is the maximum number of relevant vertices on an $s_1$-$s_2$ path. Finally, we prove that directed planar RPMEC is NP-hard, even on acyclic planar digraphs with nonnegative costs, by reducing from independent set on cubic planar graphs through a finite-bimodal directed node-cut construction and a planar node-to-edge split.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [85] [Correct When Paired, Wrong When Split: Decoupling and Editing Modality-Specific Neurons in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17057)
*Tingchao Fu,Wenkai Wang,Fanxiao Li,Huadong Zhang,Jinhong Zhang,Dayang Li,Yunyun Dong,Renyang Liu,Wei Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 MLLM 知识编辑中多模态与单模态表示不一致导致的“编辑解耦失效”问题，提出了 DECODE 框架，通过解耦和定位特定模态的神经元实现知识的同步更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大模型（MLLM）在执行知识编辑时存在“编辑解耦失效”问题：即更新多模态输入（图文对）下的知识后，在单模态输入下模型仍会恢复为更新前的过时知识。这是因为模型内的实体知识是分布在解耦的模态特定路径中的，而非统一存储。

Method: 提出了 DECODE 框架，该方法旨在显式地解耦并定位针对特定知识的模态专用神经元组，从而确保知识更新能够在不同模态路径间有效传播。

Result: 广泛的实验表明，DECODE 能够确保模型在不同模态触发条件下实现有效的知识更新，显著缓解了编辑解耦失效的现象。

Conclusion: 通过显式解耦并定位模态特定的神经元组，DECODE 能够有效地在多模态和单模态输入间实现知识的一致性更新，解决了知识编辑中的解耦失效问题。

Abstract: Although Knowledge Editing provides an efficient mechanism for updating the knowledge of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs), we find that current paradigms still suffer from an important yet remain underexplored issue : editing decoupling failure, where entity-related knowledge can be updated when the model is triggered by multimodal inputs (text--image query pairs), however, it often reverts to outdated pre-edit facts when the paired inputs are split into unimodal ones. Our in-depth empirical analysis reveals that the entity knowledge in MLLMs is not stored as a unified representation, but is instead distributed across disentangled modality-specific pathways. As a result, updates biased toward multimodal queries fail to propagate effectively to unimodal circuits. To bridge this gap, we propose DECODE, which explicitly disentangles and localizes modality-specific neuron groups for targeted knowledge. Extensive experiments demonstrate that DECODE consistently achieves effective knowledge updates under different modality triggers, thereby mitigating editing decoupling failures.

</details>


### [86] [Diagnosing and Repairing Shape-Prior Shortcuts in Long-Range Single-Shot Fringe Projection Profilometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17093)
*Adam Haroon,Anush Lakshman,Cody Fleming,Beiwen Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过机制可解释性和不确定性量化，诊断出远程条纹投影深度学习模型失效是由于过度依赖物体边界形状先验，进而提出了 PhiCalNet，通过架构层面的物理映射约束，实现了高精度的远程三维重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单样本条纹投影轮廓术（FPP）在远程场景（>1m）下存在信号受损、条纹级数缺失导致的解算不适定问题，且现有深度学习架构缺乏机制性理解。

Method: 1. 使用机制可解释性（MI）方法（线性探测、Grad-CAM、分布外测试）分析现有UNet基准的失败原因；2. 设计采用物理约束的 PhiCalNet 架构，直接输出包裹相位而非深度，并通过固定可微校准层映射为深度；3. 引入共形不确定性量化（UQ）定位故障像素点；4. 在大规模合成数据集上评估并对比物理信息损失函数与架构设计的影响。

Result: PhiCalNet 将物体平均绝对误差 (MAE) 从 14.54 mm 降低至 4.46 mm（提升3.3倍）；通过 UQ 剔除高不确定性像素后，RMSE 进一步降低 64%。研究证明改变架构架构比单纯使用物理损失函数更有效。

Conclusion: 通过机制可解释性（MI）和共形不确定性量化（UQ）诊断出传统架构依赖形状先验而非物理解码这一根本问题。PhiCalNet 通过架构约束（将深度回归改为相位回归结合固定解析校准）成功修复了这一缺陷，显著提升了远程条纹投影轮廓术的精度。

Abstract: Learning-based single-shot fringe projection profilometry (FPP) has been studied mostly at close range. The long-range regime (standoff beyond 1 m) remains largely unaddressed: inverse-square intensity falloff lowers fringe signal-to-noise ratio and degrades physical ground truth, the single-shot problem is ill-posed because fringe-order information is absent from one image, and these architectures have not been studied mechanistically. We present a diagnose-repair-verify study using mechanistic interpretability (MI) and conformal uncertainty quantification (UQ) as convergent diagnostics: they agree on one physical failure locus, driving and verifying an architectural repair. On a photorealistic synthetic benchmark (15,600 fringe images, 50 objects at 1.5-2.1 m), a best UNet baseline reaches 14.54 mm object mean absolute error (MAE). Three probes (linear probing, Grad-CAM, flat-plane out-of-distribution test) converge: the baseline solves the task via object-boundary shape priors rather than fringe-phase decoding. We repair this with PhiCalNet, which outputs wrapped phase rather than depth and applies a fixed differentiable calibration layer mapping phase to depth, removing the shape-prior solution from the hypothesis space architecturally rather than by a loss penalty. A physics-informed loss that enforces the same physics as a soft penalty on a depth-regressing network yields no measurable gain, isolating the architecture as the operative factor. PhiCalNet reduces object MAE 3.3x to 4.46 mm; the residual is carried by 0.103% of pixels at the +/-pi wrap discontinuity. Pixel-wise conformal UQ confirms the diagnosis: rejecting the top 5% of object pixels by snapshot disagreement cuts PhiCalNet RMSE by 64% (20.6->7.4 mm) versus 3.5% for the baseline. MI and UQ converge on the same failure locus.

</details>


### [87] [Informative Missingness to Generate Irregular Clinical Time Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17106)
*Hadi Mehdizavareh,Gabriele Santangelo,Giovanna Nicora,Simon Lebech Cichosz,Arianna Dagliati,Arijit Khan,Riccardo Bellazzi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种联合建模实验室数值与缺失模式的扩散模型，有效解决了EHR中“缺失即信息”的挑战，为构建临床基础模型提供了高质量的合成数据支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电子健康记录（EHR）中的实验室检查具有不规则采样特性，且缺失本身（是否进行检查）包含了临床决策与患者生理状态的重要信息，不应被视为单纯的预处理噪声，因此需要对数值与缺失模式进行联合建模。

Method: 基于TimeDiff框架的扩散模型（Diffusion-based generation），将连续的实验室数值与离散的观测指示器（missingness patterns）进行联合建模；数据预处理包括将记录对齐到4小时窗口并按7天入院窗口切分。

Result: 所生成的数据在单项生理指标分布以及“数值-缺失”联合嵌入空间上均与真实患者轨迹高度接近，有效模拟了非随机缺失（MNAR）场景下的临床数据特性。

Conclusion: 本研究证明了扩散模型能够捕捉临床生理状态与缺失机制（MNAR）之间的复杂依赖关系。生成的合成数据保留了关键的生理与检测模式关联，为后续训练能够利用“信息性缺失”的临床基础模型提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Laboratory tests in electronic health records are collected irregularly, and the absence of a test order can be as informative as the measurement itself. Such missingness reflects clinicians' decisions and patient physiology, making it important to model it directly rather than treat it as a preprocessing artifact. Here we present a diffusion-based approach for generating clinical time series that jointly models laboratory values and their observation patterns using the public Data Analytics Challenge on Missing Data Imputation (DACMI) benchmark derived from MIMIC-III. To preserve realistic sampling, we align chart times into 4-hour intervals and segment admissions into 7-day windows, producing trajectories that pair each lab value with a corresponding observation indicator. Standard transformations and normalization are applied to stabilize training. Our method extends the TimeDiff framework to learn continuous lab values and discrete missingness patterns through complementary diffusion objectives. Experiments show that the generated data closely match real patient trajectories across individual lab distributions and joint value-missingness embeddings, demonstrating that diffusion models can capture clinically meaningful dependencies between patient physiology and clinicians' testing behavior under MNAR-like (missing-not-at-random) missingness. These preliminary results indicate that our model can serve as an initial component toward developing clinical foundation models. By producing synthetic priors that preserve key physiology-missingness relationships, this work motivates the subsequent training of Prior-Data Fitted Networks capable of leveraging informative missingness, which we will investigate in the extended work.

</details>


### [88] [Models Take Notes at Prefill: KV Cache Can Be Editable and Composable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17107)
*Bojie Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究揭示了Transformer的KV缓存存储的是“已推导的结论”，通过对这些缓存进行可编辑的修正和模块化拼接（而非全量重计算），在显著提升推理速度的同时，赋予了模型高度灵活的上下文编辑与技能重组能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的KV缓存机制要求完全共享前缀，微小的输入变动即导致缓存失效并触发昂贵的重计算。研究发现模型决策主要依赖已生成的“结论”而非原始输入，因此旨在通过操作缓存本身，实现无需重算的上下文灵活编辑与知识迁移。

Method: 提出了对KV缓存的直接编辑与重组方法：(1) 编辑性：通过追加“勘误”项修正上下文中的错误结论；(2) 可组合性：通过RoPE位置重映射技术，将预编译的“技能”片段拼接到任意上下文中。该方法通过append-only方式实现，兼容现有的前缀缓存机制。

Result: 在12种模型架构上验证了该方法，拼接后的生成逻辑一致性（logit cosine）高达0.90-0.999；在vLLM基准测试中，该方法保持了98.5%的前缀缓存命中率，显著降低了首字延迟（TTFT）。

Conclusion: 模型上下文中的KV缓存本质上是“已推导结论的笔记簿”。通过对缓存进行直接编辑（修正）和重组（拼接），可以在无需完整重计算的情况下实现对模型决策的干预，极大地提升了推理效率（延迟降低达14.9倍，首字延迟提升高达398倍），同时保持与全量重计算高度一致的输出表现。

Abstract: Prefix caching reuses prefill only across an exactly shared prefix, so one changed field invalidates the entire downstream cache. Yet overwriting the field's own key/value vectors and reusing the rest leaves the model acting on the old value. The reason, established causally across four model families: at prefill the model has already written the field-conditioned conclusion onto downstream notes; the field's own key/value drives under 1% of the decision. Read as a notebook of memoized conclusions, two capabilities follow. (1) It is editable. A salient erratum amends the notes; and with chain-of-thought, editing the field alone recovers the decision (1.00 at 8B, ~1% compute), while without CoT it is ignored. (2) It is composable. The notes are position-portable, so a precompiled skill can be RoPE-repositioned and spliced into any context, indistinguishable from full recompute (logit cosine 0.90-0.999, twelve models) at O(L) rather than O(L^2) time-to-first-token. A unified edit+compose agent stays decision-identical to recompute at up to 14.9x lower latency. The approach applies to any per-token attention KV cache, validated across scale, quantization, Mixture-of-Experts, and multimodal caches, and extends to several attention variants through small adapters. Because the erratum is append-only, it composes with production prefix caching: in an online vLLM benchmark it keeps the prefix cache-aligned (98.5% hit-rate), cutting p90 time-to-first-token by 53-398x.

</details>


### [89] [The Critical Role of Model Selection in Causal Inference: A Comparative Analysis of Classification Models within the InferBERT Framework for Pharmacovigilance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17113)
*Csaba Kiss,Roland Molontay,Gabriele Pergola*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究评估了InferBERT框架中不同模型选择的影响，发现针对生物医学领域的预训练模型（BioBERT）优于更大的LLM和基础模型，证明了在计算药物警戒中，领域知识的深度比模型规模更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: InferBERT框架虽然结合了转换器模型与Do-calculus，但其性能高度依赖于底层的分类模型。目前尚不清楚到底哪种类型的模型最适合该框架，以及模型规模（LLM）与领域特定预训练对因果检测的具体影响。

Method: 在AILF和TRAM两个基准数据集上，对比评估了四种模型（XGBoost、ALBERT、BioBERT、Med-LLaMA）在因果ADE检测中的表现。采用了5折交叉验证（重复20次），并评估了准确率、期望校准误差（ECE，包括后验校准前后对比）以及与传统药物警戒信号（PRR, ROR, EBGM）的Jaccard一致性。

Result: BioBERT在准确率和与传统信号的一致性上均表现最优；Med-LLaMA尽管引入了在大规模参数下进行PEFT，但表现不佳；校准虽能改善ECE，但对准确率和因果发现的影响不一；领域特定的预训练对性能提升具有决定性作用。

Conclusion: 在药物警戒的因果推断任务中，针对生物医学领域的预训练模型（如BioBERT）优于传统机器学习基准模型和更大规模的通用/医疗大语言模型。研究表明，在计算药物警戒中，模型对领域知识的掌握比盲目追求模型参数规模更为关键。

Abstract: Distinguishing causal adverse drug events (ADEs) from spurious correlations remains a central challenge in pharmacovigilance. The InferBERT framework integrates transformer models with Do-calculus, but its success hinges on the underlying classification model. This study evaluates the impact of model choice in InferBERT, assessing whether simpler models suffice, if domain-specific pre-training helps, whether scaling to LLMs improves causal detection, and the effect of post-hoc calibration. We performed a comparative study on two benchmarks: Analgesics-induced Acute Liver Failure (AILF) and Tramadol-related Mortalities (TRAM). Four models were evaluated-XGBoost (baseline), ALBERT (original InferBERT), BioBERT (biomedical transformer), and Med-LLaMA (medical LLM)-using 5-fold cross-validation repeated over 20 runs. We measured accuracy, Expected Calibration Error (ECE) pre- and post-isotonic regression, and Jaccard concordance of causal terms with PRR, ROR, and EBGM; significance was tested with paired t-tests. BioBERT achieved the highest accuracy on both datasets, while Med-LLaMA underperformed despite its size and parameter-efficient fine-tuning. Domain-specific pre-training was decisive. Calibration improved ECE but had mixed effects on accuracy and causal discovery. BioBERT's superiority also yielded the strongest concordance with traditional pharmacovigilance signals. These results show that domain-specific pre-training provides a clear advantage over simpler baselines and larger LLMs. Investing in manageable, domain-aware models is more effective for computational pharmacovigilance than simply scaling model size.

</details>


### [90] [Probing, Fusion, and Trustworthiness: A Systematic Evaluation of Foundation Model Representations for Multimodal Cancer Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17115)
*Jingyu Hu,Giuseppe Tripodi,Reed Naidoo,Sarah F. McGough,Tapabrata Chakraborti*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了基础模型在病理多模态数据中的应用，发现其在分布偏移下具有强泛化性，多模态融合具有互补优势，且共形预测能有效提升临床决策的可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基础模型在医疗领域表现出强大的表示提取能力，但在面临分布偏移（distribution shift）时其泛化能力尚不明确，且多模态融合对临床任务的实际增益及决策可靠性仍需系统验证。

Method: 基于两个真实商业肿瘤队列（IH-BC和IH-NSCLC），涵盖全切片图像（WSI）和转录组数据；评估了5种基础模型在8项下游分类任务中的表现；对比了3种图像-组学多模态融合策略；使用共形预测方法评估了推理的可靠性。

Result: 实验证明基础模型表示在分布外数据上具有竞争力的性能；在特定任务中，多模态融合优于单模态；共形预测分析显示，即使点预测失败，真实结果通常包含在预测集内。

Conclusion: 基础模型在医学数据分布偏移下表现出良好的稳健性；多模态融合主要在单一模态缺乏充分预测信号时发挥显著优势；共形预测（Conformal Prediction）能够有效量化不确定性，提高临床诊断的可靠性。

Abstract: Foundation models (FMs) have emerged as powerful representation extractors for medical data, yet their generalizability to datasets under distribution shift remains underexplored. This work systematically evaluates FM-based representations on a suite of computational pathology tasks across two real-world commercial cohorts, IH-BC and IH-NSCLC, drawn from the licensed in-house (IH) oncology dataset. The analysis focuses on two modalities, whole-slide images and transcriptomic profiles, drawn from the IH multimodal data. We first benchmark unimodal probing performance across five FMs on eight downstream classification tasks, and find that image and omics representations carry complementary predictive signals. Then we investigate whether multimodal fusion can yield additional gains over unimodal baselines by comparing three image-omics fusion strategies built on paired representations. The trustworthiness of selected unimodal and multimodal pipelines is further assessed through conformal prediction. Our results show that FM representations achieve competitive performance on out-of-distribution data and that multimodal fusion helps mainly when no single modality dominates the signal. Conformal prediction reveals that in the majority of cases where a point prediction fails, the true diagnosis remains recoverable within the prediction set, reinforcing the value of uncertainty-aware inference for clinical support.

</details>


### [91] [MODE: Modality-Decomposed Expert-Level Mixed-Precision Quantization for MoE Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17118)
*Yuanteng Chen,Peisong Wang,Zhilei Liu,Nanxin Zeng,Yuantian Shao,Shiqiang Lang,Tao Liu,Chuangyi Li,Qinghao Hu,Gang Li,Jing Liu,Jian Cheng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对 MoE-MLLM 模型压缩难题，MODE 框架通过解耦视觉与文本模态的专家选择统计，消除了量化过程中的专家重要性评估偏见，实现了高效的混合精度量化，在大幅节省显存的同时保持了卓越的识别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) 多模态大模型（MoE-MLLM）虽然性能强劲但显存开销巨大。现有的 expert-level 量化方法在 MoE-MLLM 上效果不佳，原因在于专家重要性评估存在“跨模态偏见”（视觉 token 主导导致文本专家重要性被掩盖）和“视觉内部偏见”（冗余视觉 token 导致重要视觉专家被忽视）。

Method: 提出 MODE 框架，核心步骤包括：(1) 按模态分解专家选择频率，消除跨模态偏见；(2) 过滤冗余视觉 token 以获取去噪的视觉专家频率；(3) 引入模态敏感度评估作为频率统计的补充指标；(4) 使用整数线性规划 (ILP) 策略在给定内存预算下优化分配专家的量化比特位。

Result: 在 W3A16 量化设置下，MODE 将平均性能损失控制在 2.9% 以内；在更极端的 2-bit 设置下，相比现有方法获得了更大的性能提升。

Conclusion: MODE 框架通过解耦模态特定统计量，有效解决了 MoE-MLLM 在 expert-level 量化中存在的评估偏见，在极低比特（如 2-bit）下展现出优异的性能，显著降低了 GPU 内存成本。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts Multimodal Large Language Models (MoE-MLLMs) offer remarkable performance but incur prohibitive GPU memory costs, making compression essential. Among PTQ methods, expert-level mixed-precision quantization has proven effective for MoE-LLMs, yet suffers notable degradation on MoE-MLLMs due to two overlooked biases in expert importance estimation. (1) At the cross-modal level, the numerical dominance of vision tokens causes expert selection frequency to be dominated by vision tokens, masking experts that are critical to the text modality; (2) at the intra-vision level, the large proportion of redundant vision tokens further skew frequency statistics, obscuring experts critical for informative visual content. To bridge gaps, we propose MODE, a modality-decomposed expert-level mixed-precision quantization framework for MoE-MLLMs that decomposes expert selection frequency by modality, filters redundant vision tokens to obtain denoised visual frequency, and further evaluates quantization sensitivity per modality as a complementary signal to frequency-based estimation. These signals are integrated into an Integer Linear Programming formulation to assign per-expert bit-widths under a given budget. Extensive experiments show that MODE is particularly well-suited for MoE-MLLMs, limiting average performance loss to within 2.9% at W3A16, with larger gains at the extreme 2-bit setting.

</details>


### [92] [Noise-Driven Escape from Metastable Phases explains Grokking in Deep Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17120)
*Ibrahim Talha Ersoy,Karoline Wiesner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文揭示了深度学习中“Grokking”现象是由于 L2 正则化导致的一级相变滞后效应，模型通过 SGD 噪声驱动跨越能量壁垒从亚稳态逃逸，从而实现了突发的泛化提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在解释深度神经网络中“grokking”（即过拟合后延迟出现的泛化能力）的本质来源，揭示 L2 正则化强度变化对网络学习特征的影响，以及网络如何通过相变机制克服收敛障碍。

Method: 以线性深度神经网络为主要模型，结合统计物理方法分析 L2 正则化下的相变行为。通过有意利用 L2 正则化捕捉模型至亚稳态，研究其在随机梯度下降（SGD）驱动下的逃逸行为，并利用稀疏子采样重现 grokking 曲线。此外，将结论推广验证至非线性深度神经网络。

Result: 研究发现：1. DNN 在 L2 正则化之下存在一级相变；2. Grokking 是相变中滞后现象的结果；3. 亚稳态逃逸时间遵循 Arrhenius 定律；4. 亚稳态的数量与任务复杂度（数据协方差的奇异值数量）相关，且该机制在非线性网络中同样适用。

Conclusion: Grokking 现象与一级相位转变中的滞后效应一致。模型在亚稳态中的捕获是由 L2 正则化引起的，而跳出亚稳态（即 grokking 的发生）是由 SGD 噪声驱动的热激活过程（遵循 Arrhenius 定律）。这一机制在复杂任务中普遍存在，提供了理解神经网络学习动态及其优化效率的新视角。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) exhibit first order phase transitions under variations of the L2 regularization strength, with each transition marking the onset of a new learnable feature. Below a critical regularization strength, all features are in principle learnable, but coexisting metastable states, separated by energy barriers, can trap the network and impede convergence. A strength of DNNs is their ability to generalize. But many open questions remain, among them the origin of so called grokking: the abrupt, delayed onset of generalization after prolonged apparent overfitting. We show for linear DNNs that grokking is consistent with hysteresis in first-order L2 phase transitions: using L2 regularization to engineer deliberate trapping, we demonstrate that a model in a low-accuracy metastable state escapes only when SGD noise drives it across an energy barrier, with escape times following Arrhenius scaling. We reproduce grokking-like delayed convergence across two orders of magnitude in escape time by deliberately trapping models in metastable phases. Using sparse sub-sampling we also reproduce the canonical grokking curve where test error eventually approaches the final training error. Our work suggests that the number of metastable states equals the number of learnable features -- one per singular value of the data covariance -- the potential for hysteresis grows naturally with task complexity. We provide evidence that the same mechanism likely operates in general nonlinear DNNs. Our results provide routes toward more efficient learning schemes.

</details>


### [93] [Sum-of-Squares Degree Barriers for the Reweighted-Hinge Method in Robust Halfspace Learning: A Christoffel-Function Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17215)
*Xiaoyu Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过 Christoffel 函数量化了鲁棒学习中 Sum-of-Squares 证书的检测盲点，揭示了算法度数与对抗性鲁棒性之间的本质权衡，证明了现有算法的误差界限在特定度数下已达到理论极限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒学习算法（基于 SoS 的证书方法）在去除异常值时受限于低阶矩，对抗者可以利用这一盲点隐藏恶意噪声。作者旨在量化这一盲点的大小，并解释为什么现有的算法在特定边界条件下表现出特定的误差率。

Method: 利用 Christoffel 函数作为度量工具，构建了对抗模型与证书检测之间的对偶关系。通过加权 Chebyshev 多项式归约（weighted-Chebyshev reduction），分析了 Sum-of-Squares (SoS) 证书的度数与容错率、边界（margin）之间的量化关系，并构建了具体的度数障碍算例（degree-2 outlier barrier）。

Result: 1. 确立了“分辨率原则”：对于 degree-$2t$ 的证书，隐藏在中心 $c$ 处的最大污染质量精确等于 Christoffel 函数 $λ_{t+1}(c)$。2. 给出了边际-度数权衡：证明了将分布误差压缩至 $ε$ 所需的 SoS 度数为 $Ω(\log(1/ε))$。3. 发现了度数-2 障碍：证明了在度数为 2 时，算法无法突破 $η^{1/2}$ 的错误率壁垒。4. 提出了度数为 $2t$ 的算法，其错误率界限为 $η^{1-1/2t}$，并证明该界限在给定度数下是不可改进的。

Conclusion: 该论文揭示了受限度数证明（Sum-of-Squares 证书）在鲁棒学习中的本质局限性：能够隐匿的最大恶意污染量由干净边际分布的 Christoffel 函数 $λ_{t+1}(c)$ 决定。该结论确定了证书方法在处理恶意噪声时的算法边界（即“度数-容错率”权衡），并证明了现有的重加权铰链（reweighted-hinge）算法在特定度数下已达到理论最优。

Abstract: A certificate that removes outliers sees the data only through its low-degree moments, and an adversary exploits exactly this, hiding corruption where the clean data already looks typical, in the blind spot no bounded-degree test resolves. That blind spot turns out to have an exact size: the Christoffel function of the clean marginal, the very quantity modern data analysis thresholds to detect outliers, here read from the adversary's side as the corruption a bounded-degree certificate cannot remove. We turn this inversion into the organizing principle of the reweighted-hinge approach to robustly learning $γ$-margin halfspaces under malicious noise (Shen, 2025; Zeng and Shen, 2025): the governing resource is the Sum-of-Squares degree of the outlier-removal certificate, and the resolution principle states that the maximal corruption mass which can hide at a center $c$ from a degree-$2t$ certificate is exactly the Christoffel function $λ_{t+1}(c)$ of the clean marginal. Three consequences follow, all against the certificate method (not information-theoretic). A margin-degree tradeoff: certifying the dense pancake to error $ε$ costs SoS degree $Ω(\log(1/ε))$ or margin $Ω(\sqrt{\log(1/ε)}/\sqrt{d})$, explaining why the $\log(1/ε)$ margin Shen (2025) records is forced, with a weighted-Chebyshev reduction making the threshold $2t=Θ((|c|/s)^2)$ tight modulo one classical weighted-extremal estimate. A degree-$2$ outlier barrier: the resolution principle realized as an explicit instance on which degree $2$ is stuck at $η^{1/2}$ while degree $4$ escapes, locating the method's small breakdown rate in the degree, not the analysis. And a degree-$2t$ algorithm tracing the frontier $η^{1-1/2t}$ (recovering Shen (2025) at $t=1$), whose gain is an explicit constant, capped by the pancake density and shown unimprovable by the degree-$2$ barrier.

</details>


### [94] [Towards Fast GNN Surrogates for CO2 Migration in Complex Geological Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17180)
*Rodrigo S. Luna,Thiago H. N. Coelho,Luiz S. L. Neto,Roberto M. Velho,Adriano M. A. Cortes,Renato N. Elias,Alexandre G. Evsukoff,Fernando A. Rochinha,Mauricio Araya-Polo,Herve Gross,Alvaro L. G. A. Coutinho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种图神经网络代理模型，通过各向异性消息传递机制建模复杂地质中的CO2羽流迁移，有效模拟了强对流和指进现象，在SPE11A基准测试中表现出良好的预测精度和稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决CO2地质封存过程中多相流（具有尖锐气水界面、强对流和复杂指进现象）的复杂物理演化预测难题，探索数据驱动机器学习方法对高精度物理过程仿真的复现能力。

Method: 提出了一种针对CO2羽流迁移预测的端到端图神经网络代理模型：将SPE11A基准测试重构为图结构，利用各向异性消息传递机制（anisotropic message-passing）结合几何条件边嵌入，以捕捉网格几何、渗透率对比和非均质性引起的定向传输；采用基于多步监督的自回归残差公式在潜在空间建模时间演化。

Result: 该模型在SPE11A基准测试中实现了对气体饱和度和液相密度的良好预测，达到了行业竞争力水平，且在长期的预测时间跨度内保持了适中的累积误差。

Conclusion: 该端到端图神经网络代理模型证明了数据驱动机器学习方法在模拟复杂地质中多相流（如CO2羽流迁移）关键物理行为的有效性，能够在扩展预测范围内保持较低且稳定的累积误差。

Abstract: This chapter discusses how a data-driven machine learning approach can reproduce key aspects of the physical behavior of multiphase flows in complex geological formations. We propose an end-to-end graph neural surrogate tailored to CO$_2$ plume migration forecasting in geological storage. The method is evaluated on the SPE11A benchmark, a well-known industry test case designed to assess CO$_2$ storage scenarios and characterized by sharp gas-water interfaces, strong advective transport, and rapid convective mixing with fingering development. The benchmark is reformulated as a graph in which nodes represent computational cells and edges encode transmissibility-based interactions enriched with geometric attributes. Directional transport arising from grid geometry, permeability contrasts, and geological heterogeneity is captured through an anisotropic message-passing mechanism, where interaction weights are computed via geometry-conditioned edge embeddings, biasing message aggregation toward physically relevant transport directions. Temporal evolution is modeled in latent space using an autoregressive residual formulation trained with multi-step supervision. The proposed model produces competitive forecasts of gas saturation and liquid-phase density, which are key indicators for CO$_2$ storage monitoring, with cumulative errors that remain moderate over extended forecasting horizons.

</details>


### [95] [Finsler Geometry, Graph Neural Networks, and You](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17185)
*T. Mitchell Roddenberry,Richard G. Baraniuk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种Finslerian图神经网络架构，通过引入Finsler拉普拉斯算子克服了传统图神经网络在处理非各向同性几何时的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于图拉普拉斯算子的图神经网络主要近似Laplace-Beltrami算子，仅限于处理各向同性算子，无法处理复杂的非各向同性几何特征。

Method: 提出了一种基于点云上Finsler拉普拉斯算子估计的图神经网络层；证明了离散算子在采样点增加时向流形上真实算子的收敛性；构建了约束为体现Finsler几何的图神经网络架构。

Result: 理论上验证了估计值的收敛性；实验表明FGNNs能成功恢复非线性扩散方程背后的潜在几何结构。

Conclusion: 研究提出了一类Finslerian图神经网络（FGNNs），能够有效捕获流形上的非各向同性几何特征，并在非线性扩散方程的模拟中表现出优异的几何恢复能力。

Abstract: Graph neural network architectures based on the graph Laplacian approximate the Laplace-Beltrami operator, thus limiting their application to isotropic operators. As a nonlinear alternative to the Laplace-Beltrami operator, we consider estimates of the Finsler Laplacian on point clouds sampled from a manifold. We prove that these discrete estimates converge to the true operator on the manifold as the number of point samples grows. Moreover, we show that this operator can be expressed as a graph neural network layer, which we use to define a family of Finslerian graph neural networks constrained to express Finsler geometry. We show that Finslerian graph neural networks recover the geometry underlying nonlinear diffusion equations in practice.

</details>


### [96] [Verified Detection and Prevention of Concurrency Anomalies in Multi-Agent Large Language Model Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17182)
*Sajjad Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多智能体LLM系统中共享状态导致的并发异常，本文提出了四种形式化定义，建立了首个机器核查的一致性层级（L0-L4），并通过Verus验证在实际运行环境中有效规避了这些异常，解决了复杂生产系统中的并发缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统通过共享内存存储、向量索引和工具注册表来协同工作，但在这一过程中存在潜在的并发一致性问题。现有的多智能体系统缺乏针对这些并发异常的严格形式化定义与验证机制，导致运行时容易出现系统级的逻辑错误。

Method: 1. 使用TLA+形式化模型定义多智能体LLM系统中的四种并发异常（陈旧生成、幻觉工具、因果级联、工具效应乱序）；2. 构建一致性层级（L0-L4）格；3. 使用Verus进行形式化验证（274个义务，零假设零承认）；4. 开发并部署三个Rust运行时，分别实现不同层级的并发控制（如悲观锁、可串行化快照隔离等）。

Result: 1. 提出了首个机器核查的多智能体系统一致性层级；2. 验证了检测器对相关异常的声称表现；3. 在三个Rust运行环境中实现了从L0到L4的保护，并成功修复了真实生产环境（如ByteDance deer-flow和LangGraph）中的并发缺陷。

Conclusion: 研究建立了多智能体LLM系统的首个机器核查一致性层级（L0-L4），并通过形式化验证证明了检测器的完备性与正确性。通过Rust运行时实现与验证，成功规避了多种并发异常，并修复了包括ByteDance和LangGraph在内的实际系统问题，为多智能体系统的并发安全性提供了坚实的理论与工程基础。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems share state through memory stores, vector indices, and tool registries. We model such sharing as long-running read-generate-write operations under deterministic-generation semantics -- the regime durable-execution engines enforce by deterministic replay -- and formalize four concurrency anomalies in TLA+: stale-generation, phantom-tool, causal-cascade, and tool-effect reordering, structural analogues of classical isolation anomalies, each with a TLC counter-example. The exclusion lattice over these anomalies is trivial; the contribution is the mechanically verified realizability and strict separation of one maximal chain within it, $L_0 \subsetneq \cdots \subsetneq L_4$, to our knowledge the first machine-checked consistency hierarchy for such runtimes. A development of 274 Verus obligations (zero assume, zero admit; trust base: two structural axioms and a mutex correspondence) proves the detectors sound and complete against the specifications and each runtime its avoidance set. Three deployed Rust runtimes realize L0-L1 (pessimistic locking, serializable snapshot isolation, default-SI), each verified against stale-generation and refined to its state machine; L2-L4 are exec-mode-verified with dependency-free prevention twins (A3, A6, A2: 0/1000 versus 1000/1000), and L2 is run live across three model families (A3 prevented in all 120 retracted sessions). We reproduce a silent lost update in ByteDance's deer-flow, formalizing its fix as a verified $L_0 \to L_1$ refinement, and exhibit tool-effect reordering in LangGraph's ToolNode on unmodified output, removed by an L3 commit-order sequencer. The verified detector, refinements, and realizability artifacts are the contribution; the phenomena and lattice are classical.

</details>


### [97] [Reconfigurable Computing Challenge: Transformer for Jet Tagging on Versal AI Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17500)
*Gram Koski,Sean Lipps,Zhenghua Ma,G. Abarajithan,Ryan Kastner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个开源框架，将Transformer架构自动化映射至AMD Versal AI Engine，解决了其在高能物理低延迟触发系统中部署的资源与性能挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer虽然在粒子物理学（LHC射流标记）中表现优异，但其计算复杂性使其难以在低延迟、资源极其受限的硬件触发系统中实时部署。

Method: 开发了一个通用的软件框架，用于将Transformer层（包括稠密层和多头注意力层）映射为AIE（AMD Versal AI Engine）的构建块，并能够根据高层Python模型描述自动生成Vitis图形代码，实现了全整数算术运算的量化推理。

Result: 成功在AMD Versal AI Engine上实现了量化、全整数的射流标记Transformer模型，证明了将复杂模型映射到片上AI引擎的高效性。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于AIE的量化Transformer实现框架，为在资源受限的环境（如LHC触发系统）中部署高性能Transformer模型提供了可行的技术路径，并为通过自动化流程扩展至更复杂架构奠定了基础。

Abstract: Transformer-based models achieve strong performance for jet tagging at the CERN LHC, but deploying them in low-latency, resource-constrained trigger systems is challenging. We present an initial implementation of a quantized, integer-only transformer for jet tagging on the AMD Versal AI Engine (AIE), mapping dense and multi-head attention (MHA) layers to AIE tiles. The main contribution is a reusable software framework that represents transformer layers as composable AIE building blocks and automatically generates the corresponding Vitis graph code from a high-level Python model description. This framework provides a foundation for future research and is released as open-source software at https://github.com/KastnerRG/particle_transformer_aie.

</details>


### [98] [Uncertainty Quantification of Engineering Structures by Polynomial Chaos Expansion and Multivariate Active Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17233)
*Qitian Lu,Jafar Jafari-Asl,Panagiotis Spyridis,Lukas Novak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对多输出工程模型，提出了一种自适应顺序采样方法，通过权衡输入空间探索与多输出方差贡献，高效构建高精度代理模型，克服了传统单一采样策略在精度与计算成本上的局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂工程应用中，高保真模型往往会产生多个相关的感兴趣量（QoIs）。现有的单一实验设计（ED）难以同时满足所有输出的精度要求，而为每个输出分别采样不仅成本高昂，且忽略了输出间的相关性。需要一种高效、能处理多输出相关性并具有良好数据一致性的采样策略以降低计算成本。

Method: 一种针对向量值感兴趣量（QoIs）的自适应顺序采样方法。该方法在构建多项式混沌展开（PCE）代理模型时，通过迭代方式从候选池中选择样本，计算逻辑基于样本对输出方差的局部贡献，并结合了基于距离的探测与跨多输出的聚合方差信息利用。

Result: 通过多个工程算例对比验证，该方法在代理模型的准确性、稳定性以及二阶统计量的估计能力上均优于非顺序的拉丁超立方抽样（LHS）。

Conclusion: 所提出的自适应顺序采样方法在处理向量值输出时，能有效平衡输入空间的探索与多输出方差信息的利用，相较于传统的拉丁超立方抽样，提供了更高精度的代理模型和更可靠的二阶统计量估计。

Abstract: In many engineering applications, a single high-fidelity model produces multiple quantities of interest (QoIs) under the same input parameters, e.g. finite element models of complex physical systems. To alleviate the high computational cost of direct model evaluations, surrogate models are widely used to construct efficient approximations of model responses. Naturally, the accuracy of surrogates strongly depends on the quality of the experimental design (ED). However, a single ED may not provide an adequate representation for all outputs simultaneously, especially when different outputs exhibit varying sensitivities to the input variables. A straightforward solution is to perform separate sampling for each output, but this results in increased sampling complexity and computational cost. From a statistical perspective, such an approach also ignores potential correlations among all outputs and may compromise data consistency. To address this issue, an adaptive sequential sampling method for constructing polynomial chaos expansion surrogate models is generalized for vector valued QoIs. The method sequentially selects new samples from a candidate pool based on their local contribution to the output variance, while balancing distance-based exploration of the input space and exploitation of aggregated variance information across all outputs. Its performance is compared with non-sequential Latin Hypercube Sampling through several numerical examples from engineering problems. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed strategy improves both surrogate accuracy and stability, and provides a more reliable estimation of second-order statistics.

</details>


### [99] [Constrained Diffusion Models with Primal-Dual Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17192)
*Samar Hadou,Yigit Berkay Uslu,Alejandro Ribeiro*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了原始-对偶推断（PDI）扩散模型，通过在扩散生成过程中联合更新对偶变量和分布，实现了高效满足均值约束的条件采样，解决了传统静态对偶估计在约束采样中的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统约束采样方法通常需要预先计算优化问题的对偶变量并将其冻结，这在复杂分布或不确定环境下难以保证采样偏离最小化。因此，通过在生成采样过程中动态实时调整对偶参数，实现一种更灵活、准确的约束满足采样机制。

Method: 提出“原始-对偶推断（PDI）”框架：1. 形式化为拉格朗日对偶域中的约束采样问题；2. 在生成过程中联合迭代：通过带有当前乘子的得分场进行去噪（反向扩散步），并基于去噪样本的约束违反情况通过对偶上升更新乘子；3. 训练单一的“对偶条件得分网络”以适配多种Gibbs分布。

Result: 在混合高斯采样、无线资源分配和投资组合管理三个任务上验证了PDI的有效性。证明了推理路径上对偶变量的时间平均值能够收敛至对偶最优值的邻域，并给出了残余对偶失配对终端分布影响的界限。

Conclusion: PDI方法成功地将扩散模型与拉格朗日对偶上升法相结合，实现了对带有均值约束的熵正则化优化问题的有效采样。该方法通过联合推断原始分布和对偶变量，能够动态适应约束条件，无需预先精确估计对偶最优值，且具有收敛性理论保证。

Abstract: This paper develops constrained diffusion models with primal-dual inference (PDI) to sample from optimal distributions of entropy-regularized optimization problems with \emph{average} constraints. We formalize constrained sampling in the Lagrangian dual domain, where the optimal distribution takes the form of a Gibbs distribution indexed by the optimal dual variable. Rather than estimating this dual multiplier before sampling and freezing it throughout generation, PDI jointly infers the optimal primal distribution and its parametrizing dual variable. Each reverse diffusion step denoises using the score field associated with the current multiplier and then updates the multiplier through dual ascent using the estimated constraint violation of the denoised samples. To enable this conditional score field, we train a single dual-conditioned score network over the family of Gibbs distributions induced by the dual variables encountered during inference. We prove that the time average of the dual variables generated along the inference trajectory converges to a neighborhood of the dual optimum and bound the effect of residual dual mismatch on the terminal distribution through schedule-dependent stability factors. We evaluate PDI on constrained sampling from a mixture of Gaussians, wireless resource allocation, and portfolio management.

</details>


### [100] [Embedded Machine Learning for Microcontroller-Class Edge Devices: Data, Feature, Evaluation, and Deployment Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18122)
*Mostafa Darvishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文构建了一套针对微控制器的嵌入式机器学习工作流，重点解析了特征提取、模型协同设计及流式部署等关键工程决策，并总结了实现鲁棒端侧推理的设计准则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在资源受限（内存、功耗、延迟限制）的微控制器上部署机器学习模型时，如何处理从数据采集、预处理到模型推理完整工作流的工程难题。

Method: 采用以系统为导向的方法，通过惯性运动识别和关键词检测（关键字识别）两个典型信号处理案例，解析了采样、缓冲、特征提取、处理类别不平衡、模型/运行时协同设计以及流式部署等工程化关键环节。

Result: 通过两个实例（惯性传感器数据分类及一维卷积神经网络音频识别），展示了如何将机器学习工作流有效地映射至嵌入式环境，验证了硬件与模型协同设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 提出了一套实用的嵌入式机器学习设计准则（包括数据策划、量化、阈值设定、调度及现场监控），为在资源受限的微控制器平台实现鲁棒的端侧推理提供了指导。

Abstract: Embedded machine learning moves inference from cloud services to resource-constrained devices that must acquire data, preprocess signals, run a model, and act within tight limits on memory, energy, and latency. This paper presents a systems-oriented synthesis of an embedded machine-learning workflow for microcontroller-class platforms. The emphasis is placed on engineering decisions that are often hidden in generic machine-learning introductions: sampling and buffering, feature extraction as dimensionality reduction, validation under class imbalance, model/runtime co-design, and streaming deployment. Two representative signal families are used throughout the paper. The first is inertial motion recognition, where a two-second, three-axis accelerometer window is transformed from raw samples into root-mean-square and spectral features before classification. The second is keyword spotting, where audio is sampled, anti-aliased, transformed into mel-frequency cepstral coefficients, and processed by a compact one-dimensional convolutional network. The paper concludes with practical design rules for robust on-device inference, including data curation, quantization, thresholding, scheduling, and field monitoring.

</details>


### [101] [FoundCause: Causal Discovery with Latent Confounders from Observational Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17516)
*Patrick Blöbaum,Krishnakumar Balasubramanian,Shiva Prasad Kasiviswanathan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FoundCause是一个在大规模合成数据上训练的摊销式因果发现Transformer模型，它能通过单次前向传播从观测数据中直接恢复因果图，且首次在同类模型中显式处理了潜在混杂因素，性能显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现有因果发现方法在从观测数据中恢复有向结构及处理潜在混杂因素时的计算复杂性与准确性痛点，避免依赖干预数据，并打破传统非摊销方法在每一数据集上均需重新优化的低效模式。

Method: 1. 采用基于Transformer的端到端架构，通过大量合成的结构因果模型（SCMs）进行摊销式训练；2. 设计了排列不变的Transformer编码器（交替注意力机制），整合变量间依赖与变量分布；3. 注入类统计特征引导模型学习因果不对称性；4. 使用因式分解解码器处理边存在与方向，并集成三角细化模块；5. 引入专门的潜变量Token模块以显式建模隐藏的共同原因。

Result: 在15个真实世界数据集上，FoundCause的表现优于11种经典非摊销方法和4种摊销方法；相比最强的非摊销方法，其F1分数提升9.6%，AUROC提升1.2%，结构海明距离（SHD）降低18.9%，且推理速度仅需单次前向传播。

Conclusion: FoundCause 证明了通过在大量合成数据上进行训练，可以构建出能够泛化到真实世界数据的摊销式因果发现模型，且该模型首次在摊销框架下实现了对潜在混杂因素的显式建模，为因果发现提供了一个高效、准确的新范式。

Abstract: Causal discovery from observational data remains challenging due to the need to recover directed structure and latent confounding without interventions. We propose FoundCause, an amortized causal discovery model trained entirely on synthetic data that maps datasets directly to causal graphs in a single forward pass. By learning from large collections of simulated structural causal models, FoundCause captures transferable statistical patterns that generalize beyond individual datasets. The architecture incorporates several key inductive biases for causal discovery. It uses a permutation-invariant transformer encoder with alternating attention over samples and variables to jointly model cross-variable dependence and per-variable distributions. Pairwise statistical features derived from classical asymmetry measures are injected through statistics-conditioned attention, guiding the model toward known causal signals. A factorized decoder separates edge existence from direction, while a triangular refinement module enables reasoning over higher-order causal motifs such as chains and colliders. In addition, a dedicated confounder module based on learnable latent tokens explicitly models hidden common causes, and the model explicitly handles missing data via its masked input representation. To our knowledge, FoundCause is the first amortized causal discovery approach to explicitly model latent confounding. FoundCause outperforms 11 classical non-amortized methods (e.g., PC, GES, NOTEARS-style optimization) and 4 amortized causal discovery methods on 15 real-world datasets, achieving +9.6% improvement in $F_1$, +1.2% in AUROC, and an 18.9% reduction in structural Hamming distance relative to the strongest non-amortized methods, while performing inference in a single forward pass.

</details>


### [102] [PowerOPD: Stabilizing On-Policy Distillation with Bounded Power Transformation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17199)
*Anhao Zhao,Junlong Tong,Yingqi Fan,Ping Nie,Wenjie Li,Xiaoyu Shen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PowerOPD 通过引入有界的 Box-Cox 变换代替原有的无界对数比奖赏，解决了大型语言模型在线策略蒸馏中的梯度不稳定问题，实现了训练效率与推理性能的同步提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中的对数比奖赏函数无界，导致高方差梯度、训练不稳定及采样效率低下，且传统后置缩放方案无法根治。

Method: 提出 PowerOPD：一种利用 Box-Cox 幂变换（参数化为 alpha）的奖赏函数，通过约束奖赏范围保持符号一致性来平滑梯度。

Result: 在数学推理任务中，PowerOPD 相比 vanilla OPD 准确率提升显著（Avg@8 最高提升 6.37），计算时间减少 59.2%，显存降低 23.1%，并将梯度范数降低了 3000 倍以上。

Conclusion: PowerOPD 提出了一种基于 Box-Cox 变换的奖赏机制，有效解决了标准在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中对数比奖赏导致的梯度爆炸与训练不稳定问题，实现了比完全词表蒸馏更高的性能且大幅降低了计算开销。

Abstract: Standard on-policy distillation (OPD) for large language models estimates the reverse-KL objective using student-sampled tokens, yielding an unbiased single-sample Monte Carlo estimator that avoids vocabulary-wide computation. However, we show that this estimator suffers from severe training pathologies in practice: sample inefficiency, unstable generation dynamics, and a substantial performance gap compared to exact full-vocabulary OPD. Reward-level diagnosis traces these pathologies to the log-ratio reward, which is unbounded by construction, producing extremely high-variance gradients concentrated at early positions and persisting throughout training; standard post-hoc scaling fail as they operate only after this distortion occurs. To solve this problem, we propose PowerOPD: a family of natively bounded, sign-consistent rewards from the Box-Cox power transformation, parameterized by alpha > 0, of which the log-ratio is the degenerate alpha -> 0 limit. Across six mathematical reasoning benchmarks and four Qwen3 teacher-student pairs, PowerOPD achieves benchmark-averaged Avg@8/Pass@8 gains of up to +6.37/+5.71 over vanilla OPD, +3.01/+3.54 over post-hoc stabilization, and +2.59/+8.90 over full-vocabulary OPD, while reducing wall-clock time by 59.2% and peak GPU memory by 23.1%. Larger alpha generally improves accuracy, consistently shortens responses, and keeps gradient norms more than 3,000x smaller than vanilla OPD.

</details>


### [103] [When the Next Step Is Not One Step: Distribution-Aware Execution Modeling for Concurrent Go Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17508)
*Kaviru Hapuarachchi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对并发程序执行的不确定性，本文提出通过对多次运行样本的分布进行KL散度目标微调的方法，有效提升了并发代码下一步行为的预测准确性与置信度校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发程序中相同的前缀可能导致多种合法的下一步行为（非确定性），传统的单标签监督学习（即强迫模型猜测单一结果）难以有效拟合这种随机过程。

Method: 1. 利用并发执行的非确定性，通过多次运行程序收集下一步事件的经验分布；2. 使用KL散度（KL Divergence）作为目标函数，对7B规模的预训练模型进行模型微调（Fine-tuning）；3. 引入形式化方法，定义特定goroutine阻塞场景的死锁特征（goroutine-leak signature）。

Result: 1. 在包含CockroachDB、Kubernetes等真实生产环境Go语言Bug的测试集上，微调后的模型准确率（36.2%）优于Gemini 1.5 Flash（34.8%）及未经微调的模型（28.6%）；2. 分布训练相比传统的交叉熵损失，显著降低了预期校准误差（ECE）从0.205降至0.169，模型预测更符合实际概率分布。

Conclusion: 通过将并发程序的非确定性转化为训练信号，利用KL散度目标函数对模型进行分布训练，不仅能提升并发程序下一步预测的准确率，还能显著改善模型的置信度（降低ECE）。此外，结合形式化方法可进一步提升对特定死锁（如goroutine泄漏）的检测能力。

Abstract: Training a model to predict the next step in a concurrent program is harder than it looks: two runs of the same program from the same trace prefix can produce different next events, both valid, because the scheduler is nondeterministic. A model trained against a single label is learning to guess one outcome of a random process. We turn this around and use the nondeterminism as a training signal. We run each program many times, aggregate the observed next events into an empirical distribution, and fine-tune a 7B model to match that distribution with a KL objective. On 798 held-out predictions drawn from real production Go bugs (CockroachDB, Kubernetes, gRPC, etcd), fine-tuning on fewer than a thousand traces reaches 36.2% accuracy, ahead of Gemini 3.5 Flash used zero-shot (34.8%) and the same model without fine-tuning (28.6%). Distribution training matches cross-entropy on accuracy (35.8% vs. 36.2%) while reducing Expected Calibration Error from 0.205 to 0.169. We also derive a formal goroutine-leak signature for a class of select-blocked goroutines where P(GoUnblock)=0 holds by scheduler semantics, not by learning. We release the dataset, trained adapters, and all tooling.

</details>


### [104] [Rift: A Conflict Signature for Deception in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17229)
*Petr Nyoma*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究发现，AI模型在故意说谎时会产生独特的“内部冲突签名”（表现为更高的残差秩），这一特征无需标签即可被高精度检测，且在不同模型架构和语言之间具有极强的通用性，为探测欺骗性AI提供了重要依据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于行为（behavioral evaluation）的评估方法无法有效处理“明知真相却故意撒谎”的欺骗模型（ELK问题的核心难点）。研究旨在探究这种欺骗行为是否会在模型的内部表示中留下可检测的特征签名，从而解决黑箱欺骗识别问题。

Method: 研究引入了“错误控制”（control for wrongness）机制，通过对比“潜伏代理”（知道真相但受触发说谎）与“天真骗子”（仅经过错误输出微调、缺乏相关事实知识）的内在状态，利用残差秩（residual rank）分析、线性探针（probe）以及跨模型、跨语言的零样本测试，评估模型在说谎时的 internal state（内部状态）差异。

Result: 1. 欺骗性前向传递的残差秩比“天真骗子”高出2.1-2.3倍；
2. 在GPT-2、Qwen2.5和Phi-3等多个模型上，检测准确率达到100%（AUC 1.0）；
3. 该特征具有极强的泛化能力（跨架构、跨语言零样本识别AUC均在0.8以上），且能够抵御主动隐藏和策略性欺骗。

Conclusion: 欺骗行为在大型语言模型的内部激活中留下了显著的“冲突标记”（conflict signature，表现为更高的残差秩），这种标记独立于输出内容、模型架构及语言，且具有高度的鲁棒性和泛化性，为检测隐蔽的欺骗行为提供了有效的技术路径。

Abstract: A model that lies while knowing the truth is the central case ELK cannot handle with behavioral evaluation alone. We ask whether such deception leaves an internal signature distinguishing it from honest error. Our key move is a control for wrongness: we contrast a sleeper agent (knows the truth, lies on trigger) against a naive liar (fine-tuned to emit the same wrong answers with no honest training). Both produce identical wrong outputs; any difference is about knowledge conflict, not incorrectness. We find deceptive forward passes carry a conflict signature - 2.1-2.3x higher residual rank than naive-liar passes on the same wrong answer - strong enough to identify which of two responses is the lie with 100% accuracy and no labels, across GPT-2 small/medium (three seeds) and three instruct models. Across Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B and Phi-3-mini, instructed deception raises residual rank on every tested fact (18/18, 40/40, 34/34); on Phi-3, lies separate perfectly from both honest answers and hallucinations (AUC 1.0, Wilcoxon p~6e-11). The signature survives strategic self-constructed deception (model invents its own lie, AUC 1.0), active concealment attempts (AUC 1.0), and length-controlled replication (20/20, AUC 1.0, p~1e-6). Using basis-free relative representations, a probe trained on one model family detects deception in two other families zero-shot (mean AUC 0.933), surviving simultaneous architecture and format change (AUC 0.821), and transfers across five languages (AUC 1.000, length-controlled). The signature is read-only: detectable but not injectable (0/8 both directions). Honest limitations and six negative experiments are documented in full.

</details>


### [105] [S4oP: Operator-level Pruning of Structured State Space Models for Resource-Constrained Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18096)
*Marco Deano,Filippo Ziche,Nicola Bombieri*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了针对S4及S4D架构的算子级结构化剪枝方法，通过渐进式剪枝与微调平衡效率与精度，在减少最多70%计算开销的同时保持了模型性能，解决了SSM在资源受限环境下的部署难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然S4/S4D等结构化状态空间模型在长序列依赖建模方面表现出色，但其高昂的算力和内存需求限制了在资源受限场景下的部署。现有研究缺乏针对SSM结构化算子剪枝的系统性探讨。

Method: 提出了一种增量式的、算子级别的结构化剪枝方法。通过交替进行结构化掩码（Structured Masking）和微调（Fine-tuning），并在训练和评估过程中联合监控模型精度与推理延迟，在保证性能的前提下逐步剔除冗余算子。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，在剪除多达70%的模型算子后，模型仍能保持原始性能，同时大幅降低了推理延迟。这是首个针对SSM系统的结构化算子剪枝研究。

Conclusion: 结构化算子剪枝是提升状态空间模型（SSM）推理效率的有效策略，能够实现显著的延迟降低而几乎不损失预测能力，为资源受限场景下的部署提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Structured State Space Models (SSMs), including the S4 and S4D architectures, have recently emerged as powerful alternatives to attention-based models for capturing long-range dependencies in sequential data. Despite their strong empirical performance, deploying these models in time- and resource-constrained settings remains challenging due to their computational and memory demands. In this paper, we propose a novel incremental, operator-level pruning approach for S4- and S4D-based models that significantly reduces inference cost while preserving predictive performance. To the best of our knowledge, this is the first work to systematically investigate structured operator pruning for SSMs. Our method progressively prunes model operators by interleaving structured masking with fine-tuning, while jointly monitoring accuracy and inference latency. We implement this approach within a unified training and evaluation framework that enables systematic exploration of efficiency-accuracy trade-offs. Experiments across multiple benchmark datasets show that pruning up to 70% of the model operators preserves the performance of the original models in most cases, while substantially reducing inference latency. These results demonstrate that structured operator pruning is an effective and previously unexplored strategy for improving the efficiency of SSMs and facilitate their deployment in practical, resource-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [106] [Rethinking Groups in Critic-Free RLVR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17250)
*Yihong Wu,Liheng Ma,Lingfeng Xiao,Muzhi Li,Xinyu Wang,Yingxue Zhang,Jian-Yun Nie*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种负向标记过滤策略，剔除了大模型强化学习对多轨迹采样的依赖，实现了高效、稳定的单轨迹训练，并在多种任务中展现了优于传统组基准RL方法的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无需评价模型（Critic-free）的强化学习方法严重依赖“同问题多轨迹采样”来估算优势基线，导致数据效率低下、同步障碍及结构化轨迹支持不足等问题。作者旨在通过解决这些负面惩罚问题来优化强化学习训练范式。

Method: 提出“负向标记过滤”（Negative Token Filtering）策略，分析并取代了传统的基于同问题多轨迹采样（Group-based rollouts）来估计基线的方法，实现了单轨迹（Single-rollout）训练。
