In this paper, we propose HyGRAG, a hierarchical graph RAG framework that transcends source documents by addressing three core challenges: constructing summaries that genuinely integrate contextual and relational information, leveraging these synthesized representations to access emergent knowledge during retrieval, and efficiently updating hierarchical structures for dynamic corpora. Specifically, we design hierarchical index structures over hybrid graphs with both chunk and entity nodes, then iteratively cluster them and generate LLM-based summaries. Then, we design context and relation-aware retrieval that searches across all abstraction levels while expanding through community membership. Moreover, we enable dynamic knowledge update through attachment-based algorithms with only local re-summarization. Experimental results show that HyGRAG improves the average accuracy of multi-hop reasoning tasks by 9.7%, while maintaining reasonable efficiency.

</details>


### [284] [IsabeLLM: Automated Theorem Proving Applied to Formally Verifying Consensus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18098)
*Elliot Jones,William Knottenbelt*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过引入RAG、错误追踪及反例生成等技术优化了IsabeLLM工具，有效提升了其对区块链共识协议（以比特币PoW为例）进行形式化验证的自动化能力与效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助定理证明能缓解形式化验证的高门槛与工作量；区块链系统中共识协议的安全性至关重要，但手动验证困难大，亟需自动化工具来降低验证成本并提升安全性。

Method: 在IsabeLLM原有的基础上：1) 实现了检索增强生成（RAG）框架以优化提示信息；2) 加入了错误追踪与反例生成机制以提升上下文理解；3) 实现了与最新版本Isabelle和Sledgehammer的兼容以提升运行效率。

Result: 通过对改进后的IsabeLLM与原版进行对比实验，验证了其在完成比特币工作量证明（Proof of Work）共识协议形式化验证任务中的性能提升。

Conclusion: 通过改进IsabeLLM（引入RAG、错误追踪及反例生成、兼容性优化），能显著提升AI辅助定理证明在处理复杂区块链共识协议（如比特币PoW）形式化验证中的效率与可行性。

Abstract: Advances in Artificial Intelligence (AI) have led AI for Theorem Proving to become a promising means of formally verifying computer systems. Whilst formal verification is traditionally reserved for safety-critical systems due to the required amount of expertise and effort, AI can help to automate a large amount of this workload and make it far more accessible. Blockchain-based systems are becoming increasingly popular and are frequently targeted by malicious actors, often resulting in huge financial losses, highlighting the need to better verify these systems and mitigate vulnerabilities. Arguably the most important component of these systems is the consensus protocol, which allows nodes to agree on decisions in a potentially adversarial environment. In this paper, we improve upon IsabeLLM, the automated theorem proving tool in Isabelle. Namely, we implement a Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework, Error tracing and counterexample generation for improved context supplied to the Large Language Model. Compatibility with the latest version of Isabelle and Sledgehammer is also implemented for improved efficiency. We compare the performance of the two versions of IsabeLLM in their ability to complete the verification of Bitcoin's Proof of Work consensus.

</details>


### [285] [Trust the Right Teacher: Quality-Aware Self-Distillation for GUI Grounding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18101)
*Jingyuan Huang,Zuming Huang,Yucheng Shi,Tianze Yang,Xiaoming Zhai,Wei Chu,Ninghao Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对GUI坐标定位任务中自蒸馏信号不可靠的问题，本论文提出了一种质量感知自蒸馏（QASD）方法，通过正确性门控和概率缩放机制精准筛选并校准监督信号，显著提升了模型的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的在线策略自蒸馏（OPSD）在GUI定位任务中存在缺陷：当学生生成的预测路径偏差时，教师提供的坐标令牌信号质量会下降，导致监督信号不可靠。

Method: 提出了一种质量感知自蒸馏（Quality-aware Self-distillation, QASD）框架，包含两个核心机制：1. 软正确性感知门控（Soft correctness-aware gating），用于抑制当学生模型前缀偏差导致教师信号不可靠时的权重；2. 教师概率缩放（Teacher-probability scaling），利用教师置信度进一步校准监督信号的强度。两者结合使用以实现优势互补。

Result: 实验表明，QASD中的两个组件具有高度互补性；在六个GUI定位基准测试中，该方法均能有效提升基础模型性能，并优于现有的强基线模型。

Conclusion: 提出了一种质量感知的自蒸馏方法（QASD），通过结合正确性感知门控机制和教师概率缩放，有效提升了GUI定位任务中教师信号的质量，在六个基准测试中均超越了强基线模型。

Abstract: Graphical user interface (GUI) grounding requires vision-language models (VLMs) to identify small target elements in high-resolution screenshots and predict precise screen coordinates. On-policy self-distillation (OPSD) is a promising post-training approach for this coordinate-sensitive task, since it provides dense token-level teacher signals beyond hard coordinate labels. However, naive OPSD is not well suited to GUI grounding: OPSD evaluates the teacher on student-generated prefixes, the quality of coordinate-token teacher signals can degrade when the prefix has already deviated from the target coordinate, leading to unreliable teacher signal. To mitigate this, We propose quality-aware self-distillation for VLM-based GUI grounding, which improves coordinate-token teacher-signal quality through soft correctness-aware gating and teacher-probability scaling. The soft correctness-aware gate checks whether the teacher's current coordinate-token prediction can still be completed into the ground-truth box under the student-generated prefix. If not, the corresponding teacher signal is down-weighted. Teacher-probability scaling then uses the teacher's confidence as a lightweight factor to further calibrate the strength of the gated supervision. A key empirical finding is that neither component alone improves overall performance, whereas combining them consistently improves performance. This suggests that the two mechanisms play complementary roles: correctness-aware gating suppresses unreliable coordinate-token supervision, while teacher-probability scaling calibrates the strength of the remaining signals. Experiments across six GUI grounding benchmarks show that our method consistently improves the base model and outperforms strong baselines.

</details>


### [286] [First Proof Second Batch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18119)
*Mohammed Abouzaid,Nikhil Srivastava,Rachel Ward,Lauren Williams*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本研究通过测试AI系统在10个真实研究级数学问题上的表现，指出当前AI尚无法独立完成专业数学研究工作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI技术的发展，学术界需要评估当前AI系统在解决高难度、研究级数学问题方面的实际能力限度。

Method: 选取了由多位数学家提供的、在各自研究过程中自然产生的10个研究级数学问题，对多个现有的主流AI系统进行测试，并邀请专家对AI生成的解决方案进行评审（提供完整日志和人机对比）。

Result: AI系统未能成功解决测试中的大多数研究级问题，表现出在复杂逻辑推理和深度数学理解上的局限性。

Conclusion: 目前的AI系统在处理研究级数学问题时表现严重不足，尚无法胜任专业数学研究任务。

Abstract: To assess the ability of current AI systems to correctly solve research-level mathematics problems, we tested several AI systems on a set of ten problems in a broad range of mathematical fields; these problems arose naturally in the research process of the contributors. This document includes the problems, our methodology, and the results of our testing. We provide links to supplementary documents including the human solutions, the AI-generated solutions, and the referee reports and logs for the AI-generated solutions.
  The ten problems were contributed by the following mathematicians: (1) Dariusz Kalociński and Theodore A. Slaman, (2) Richard Schwartz, (3) Aleksa Milojevic and Benny Sudakov, (4) Larry Guth, (5) Oleg Butkovsky, Jonathan Mattingly, and Lorenzo Zambotti, (6) Joshua Evan Greene and Duncan McCoy, (7) Sucharit Sarkar, (8) Sam Payne and Jidong (Jayden) Wang, (9) Sylvie Corteel and John Lentfer, (10) Srivatsav Kunnawalkam Elayavalli.

</details>


### [287] [Knowledge Reutilization in Meta-Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18132)
*Yuan Meng,Bo Wang,Juan de los Rios Ruiz,Xiangtong Yao,Zhenshan Bing,Fuchun Sun,Alois Knoll*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种将任务知识与智能体形态解耦的元强化学习框架，通过在简单环境下学习通用任务知识并迁移至异构智能体，显著提升了样本效率与任务执行精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端元强化学习方法将任务推理与具体化控制紧密耦合，导致任务语义抽象不足、样本利用率低以及跨智能体复用困难。

Method: 提出了一种“元知识重利用”框架：1. 在动态简化后的智能体上学习任务级知识；2. 利用贝叶斯非参数先验对潜在任务模式进行组织；3. 引入“语义-幅度接口（semantic-magnitude interface）”和轻量级时间适配器，将冻结的元知识转化为针对特定智能体的低层控制器子目标。

Result: 在多个运动智能体任务中，与现有SOTA基准相比，跟踪误差降低了94.75%至99.79%，且仅需约23.8%的交互数据即可达到同等性能。

Conclusion: 通过解耦任务推理与具体化控制，该框架成功实现了跨异构智能体的元知识迁移，在显著降低交互数据需求的同时，大幅提升了任务执行的精度。

Abstract: Meta-reinforcement learning enables fast adaptation by extracting shared structure from related tasks, but existing end-to-end methods often couple task inference with embodiment-specific control. This coupling can obscure non-parametric task semantics, reduce sample efficiency, and limit cross-agent reuse. We propose a meta-knowledge reutilization framework that learns task-level knowledge on a dynamics-simplified agent and transfers it to heterogeneous agents. The framework uses a Bayesian non-parametric prior to organize latent task modes and a high-level policy to generate task-level magnitude guidance. To bridge reusable task knowledge with different embodiments, we introduce a semantic-magnitude interface and a lightweight temporal adaptor, which convert frozen meta-knowledge into temporally aligned subgoals for embodiment-specific low-level controllers. Experiments on multiple locomotion agents show that our framework reduces final-step tracking error by 94.75% -- 99.79% compared with recent state-of-the-art baselines and achieves comparable deployment performance with about 23.8% of their interaction data.

</details>


### [288] [Your AI Travel Agent Would Book You a Bullfight: An Agentic Benchmark for Implicit Animal Welfare in Frontier AI Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18142)
*Jasmine Brazilek,Oliver Tulio,Joel Christoph,Miles Tidmarsh,Carol Kline,Arturs Kanepajs*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该研究提出了首个代理评估基准TAC，发现当前前沿AI代理在进行旅行规划操作时，往往会选择涉及动物剥削的服务，表现远低于随机水平，揭示了AI代理在实际决策中缺乏福祉评估能力的重大隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前的AI基准测试多关注文本响应，而非代理（Agentic）在实际操作中的行为。随着AI代理从“建议者”转向“执行者”，评估AI在现实操作（如预订旅行）中是否会为了执行任务而忽视动物福祉显得尤为迫切。

Method: 提出TAC（Travel Agent Compassion）基准，通过12个手动编写的旅行预订场景（扩展至48个样本以控制干扰因素）测试AI代理在涉及动物剥削时的操作选择。通过对比七款前沿模型在默认状态与加入福祉诱导提示词后的表现，并利用Inspect Scout辅助审计评估模型意识，验证其决策机制。

Result: 所有被测模型表现均低于机会水平（64%），最佳模型仅为53%。添加福祉感知提示词后，Claude和GPT系列模型表现大幅提升（提升达47-63个百分点），而DeepSeek和Gemini提升较弱。审计显示模型表现低迷并非源于识别出测试环境，而是缺乏内生的评估意识。

Conclusion: 现有的AI模型在代理部署环境下缺乏对动物福祉的考量，表现远低于预期。尽管通过系统提示词（System Prompt）可以显著改善其行为，但模型本身并不具备内生的福祉意识。研究强调了开发更具针对性的代理评测基准的必要性，并为制定AI行为准则提供了实证依据。

Abstract: AI agents are moving from advisors to actors, booking travel, planning menus, and running procurement on behalf of users. Existing benchmarks for AI and animal welfare evaluate model text responses to question-answer prompts, leaving open whether the welfare reasoning surfaced in those responses transfers to agentic deployment where the model must take actions with tools. We introduce TAC (Travel Agent Compassion), the first agentic benchmark measuring whether AI agents avoid options involving animal exploitation when acting on behalf of users. TAC presents an AI agent with twelve hand-authored travel booking scenarios across six categories of animal exploitation, augmented to forty-eight samples to control for price, rating, and position confounds. We evaluate seven frontier models from four labs. Every model scores below the chance level of sixty-four percent, with the best performer (Claude Opus 4.7) at fifty-three percent. A single welfare-aware sentence in the system prompt yields gains of forty-seven to sixty-three percentage points in Claude and GPT-5.5, twenty-six points in GPT-5.2, and under twelve points in DeepSeek and Gemini. An auxiliary Inspect Scout audit of 288 base-condition transcripts from the top two performers, using Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite as judge, flags zero transcripts for evaluation awareness, suggesting the below-chance rates do not stem from the models recognising the evaluation. We discuss implications for category-level variation across cultural domains, the limits of text-response welfare benchmarks, and the EU General-Purpose AI Code of Practice systemic risk framework.

</details>


### [289] [WEQA: Wearable hEalth Question Answering with Query-Adaptive Agentic Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18147)
*Yuwei Zhang,Tong Xia,Bianca Emmerich,Yu Yvonne Wu,Dimitris Spathis,Xin Liu,Daniel McDuff,Cecilia Mascolo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对大模型难以处理复杂可穿戴健康数据的问题，WEQA 提出了一种动态调用专业分析工具的智体框架，显著提升了健康数据问答的准确性与临床价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在通用医学问答中表现优异，但在处理可穿戴设备产生的连续、高维、纵向数据时存在困难，且单一的预训练模型难以应对多样化的传感器模态和用户需求。

Method: 提出了一种名为 WEQA 的查询自适应代理框架。该框架由一个大语言模型（LLM）控制器驱动，负责合成执行计划，根据用户查询动态调用适当的传感器分析工具或预训练模型，并利用外部知识对生成的回复进行落地审计（grounded auditing）。此外，研究者还构建了一个涵盖四个数据集、三个健康领域的基准测试。

Result: 实验结果表明，WEQA 框架在准确性上比现有的 LLM 及智能体基线提高了 24%；在与 12 名医学专家和 8 名用户进行的盲测中，该框架在实用性和临床合理性方面展示了显著优势。

Conclusion: WEQA 框架通过将大语言模型与专业可穿戴数据分析工具相结合，有效提升了可穿戴健康数据问答的准确性和临床可靠性，为处理复杂、多模态的连续健康数据提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: Language models are remarkably capable at medical question answering, in some cases surpassing the accuracy of general physicians. However, answering questions about wearable health data remains challenging and understudied, as these ubiquitous sensors produce continuous, high-dimensional, and longitudinal data, which is non-trivial to align with text-centric distributions in LLM pretraining. The diversity of sensor modalities and user intents cannot be effectively handled by a fixed reasoning workflow or a single pretrained foundation model. To address these challenges, we propose WEQA, a query-adaptive agent framework that unifies LLM reasoning with specialized wearable analytical and modeling tools. An LLM controller is employed to synthesize execution plans and dynamically route each query to the appropriate combination of sensor analysis and pretrained models, and perform grounded response auditing with external knowledge. We also curate a benchmark spanning four open wearable datasets comprising analytic and predictive tasks in three different health domains. Experiments show that our framework is 24% more accurate than LLM and agentic baselines, and a blinded study with 12 medical experts and 8 users shows substantial gains in usefulness and clinical soundness.

</details>


### [290] [Learning Cardiac Electrophysiology Digital Twins Through Agentic Discovery of Hybrid Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18154)
*Ziqi Zhou,Yubo Ye,Sumeet Atul Vadhavka,Linwei Wang,Zhiqiang Tao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: LEADS是一种利用LLM智能体自动发现结构化和物理稳定心脏电生理混合模型的框架，解决了人工建模的局限性与通用LLM缺乏物理先验的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统构建个性化心脏电生理数字孪生的方法依赖专家手动设计混合模型，耗时且难以跨患者通用；现有的通用LLM方法缺乏物理仿真所需的结构化先验，导致模拟不稳定。

Method: 提出LEADS框架，将心脏电生理领域知识转化为结构化动作空间，利用LLM代理通过“推理-行动”循环（reasoning-and-action loop）自动化选择、组合和细化混合模型架构，并结合梯度下降进行参数优化。

Result: 在合成数据（三种真值模型）和真实心脏电生理数据上的验证表明，LEADS生成的模型在物理接地性、可解释性和数值稳定性方面表现优异，性能指标超过了现有的人工设计模型及其他LLM集成方法。

Conclusion: LEADS框架证明了结合物理先验与LLM代理的架构发现能力，能够优于人工设计的混合模型和其他LLM方法，在心脏电生理数字孪生建模中展现出卓越的泛化性、可解释性与稳定性。

Abstract: Building personalized cardiac electrophysiology (EP) digital twins requires identifying the appropriate model structure for each patient, not merely fitting parameters. Traditional methods rely on experts to manually prescribe hybrid physics-neural architectures, which requires deep domain expertise and does not transfer across patients. Recent works have applied large language models (LLMs) to generate or act as hybrid models. However, despite their promising generalization capacity, these LLM-based methods lack the structural priors needed for stable cardiac simulations. Hence, we propose LEADS, a framework that formulates cardiac EP domain knowledge as a structured action space and utilizes an LLM agent to discover hybrid models. The agent follows an iterative reasoning-and-action loop to select, combine, and refine hybrid models, whilst gradient descent handles parameter fitting. The proposed LEADS designs every candidate model towards physically grounded, interpretable, and numerically stable, while allowing open-ended architectural discovery. We validate LEADS on synthetic data with three ground-truth reaction models and on real cardiac EP data, demonstrating that it outperforms both human-designed hybrid models and other LLM-based hybrid modeling.

</details>


### [291] [DRFLOW: A Deep Research Benchmark for Personalized Workflow Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18191)
*Md Tawkat Islam Khondaker,Raymond Li,Muhammad Abdul-Mageed,Laks V. S. Lakshmanan,Issam H. Laradji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对现有深度研究系统在企业复杂工作流任务执行上的不足，本文提出了 DRFLOW 基准测试及相应的参考代理（DRFA），旨在通过多源证据评估模型预测个性化行动步骤序列的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度研究（DR）系统多关注总结与报告生成，未能满足企业级任务中对“识别具体行动步骤工作流”的需求。

Method: 构建了 DRFLOW 基准测试（100 个任务、5 个领域、1246 个参考步骤、3900+ 源文档）；定义了 7 个诊断指标（事实根据、步骤覆盖、结构排序、条件解析和个性化）；提出了 DRFLOW-Agent (DRFA) 作为参考模型。

Result: DRFA 在平均 F1 分数上比强基线代理提升了高达 10.02%，但整体评估指标显示，在完整且准确地预测个性化工作流方面仍面临巨大挑战。

Conclusion: DRFLOW 框架揭示了预测个性化工作流任务的复杂性，现有模型（如 DRFA）虽然优于基准，但在处理这类任务时仍存在显著的提升空间，是一个具有挑战性的研究前沿。

Abstract: Deep research (DR) systems are increasingly used for complex information-seeking tasks, but existing works mainly focus on generating reports and summaries. In contrast, many enterprise tasks instead require an agent to identify concrete workflows which is a sequence of action-steps. For example, rather than summarizing budgeting policies, an agent should be able to determine the steps needed to answer a question such as: "How do I request new headcount given a fixed budget?". Therefore, we introduce DRFLOW, a benchmark for evaluating personalized workflows predicted by agents from heterogeneous sources. Each task requires the agent to identify relevant evidence from scattered sources, then use that evidence to predict the correct action-step sequence for the user's task. DRFLOW contains 100 tasks across five domains, with 1,246 reference workflow steps grounded in more than 3,900 sources. We define seven diagnostic metrics covering factual grounding, step recovery, structural ordering, condition resolution, and personalization. We further present DRFLOW-Agent (DRFA), a workflow-oriented reference agent to predict personalized workflow. We show that although DRFA improves over strong baseline agents (upto 10.02% average F1 score), there is substantial room for improvement remains across these workflow metrics, indicating that predicting complete and correct personalized workflows remains a challenging frontier for deep research.

</details>


### [292] [The Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset: Reconstructing U.S. Corporate and Financial Disclosures into Layout-Faithful and Token-Efficient Pretraining Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18192)
*Nick Bettencourt,Xiaowei Ding,Kay Giesecke*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对长上下文 LLM 训练数据匮乏的问题，本文构建了基于 SEC 文件的 Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD)，提供了大规模的高质量金融文本语料及两项配套的金融推理与文档理解评估任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着高质量公共网络数据趋于枯竭，适合长上下文训练的干净数据变得稀缺。现有金融语料多为私有或领域狭窄（如代码），缺乏大规模、通用的高质量金融文本。

Method: 研究人员利用 SEC 文件（EDGAR 数据库）进行重构，转化为版面忠实的 MultiMarkdown 格式；通过 152B token 的初步数据集（SEFD-v1）进行验证，并构建了两个特定任务基准：EDGAR-Forecast（数值预测）和 EDGAR-OCR（复杂表格识别）。

Result: 发布了 152B token 的公开数据集 SEFD-v1，其与 Common Crawl 重叠度极低（<0.1%），并提供了针对金融预测与 OCR 能力的评估基准，填补了金融领域长上下文数据和评估体系的空白。

Conclusion: SEFD 提供了一个高质量、大规模且与常用数据集低重叠的金融领域长文本语料库，显著扩展了长上下文 LLM 的训练数据储备，并为金融领域的多任务能力评估提供了标准 benchmark。

Abstract: As high-quality public web corpora become increasingly exhausted, clean long-context documents have become a scarce and expensive source of training data for large language models (LLMs). Existing long-context corpora are often proprietary and costly to acquire, synthetically generated, or concentrated in narrow domains such as programming. We introduce the Stanford EDGAR Filings Dataset (SEFD), an open reconstruction of SEC filings into layout-faithful MultiMarkdown for financial language modeling and evaluation. SEFD makes audited financial statements, risk disclosures, ownership reports, accounting notes, and market-moving event filings usable as long-context pretraining data and as a basis for financial reasoning, forecasting, compliance, and document understanding. The resulting corpus is token-efficient, model-ready, and has less than 0.1% overlap with Common Crawl-derived corpora. We release SEFD-v1, a 152B-token initial public snapshot, and provide corpus-level analyses of a larger 18.5M-filing archive estimated at 550B tokens. We further introduce two SEFD-derived benchmarks: EDGAR-Forecast, which evaluates filing-grounded numerical forecasting after model knowledge cutoffs, and EDGAR-OCR, which evaluates transcription of complex financial tables.

</details>


### [293] [Fixed-Point Reasoners: Stable and Adaptive Deep Looped Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18206)
*Sajad Movahedi,Vera Milovanović,Shlomo Libo Feigin,Alexander Theus,Thomas Hofmann,Valentina Boeva,T. Konstantin Rusch,Antonio Orvieto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为 FPRM 的循环 Transformer 模型，通过预归一化和残差缩放优化信号传播，并采用不动点收敛作为推理过程的自适应终止策略，有效提升了模型在复杂推理任务中的处理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环架构（Looped architectures）具有学习分步推理的归纳偏置，但随着循环次数增加，会面临类似深层架构的信号传播失效问题，且需要更有效的终止机制来动态平衡计算量与任务难度。

Method: 1. 针对循环架构引入预归一化（pre-norm）和残差缩放（residual scaling）以改善深度带来的信号传播问题；2. 提出 FPRM（Transformer-based Fixed-Point Reasoning Model），使用不动点收敛作为端到端的终止机制。

Result: FPRM 能够根据任务难度自适应调整计算步骤，在 Sudoku（数独）、Maze（走迷宫）、状态跟踪和 ARC-AGI 等复合推理基准测试中表现出色。

Conclusion: FPRM 通过引入预归一化（pre-norm）和残差缩放（residual scaling）解决了循环架构中的信号传播问题，并利用不动点收敛实现了计算量的按需分配，在推理基准测试中展现了有效性。

Abstract: Looped architectures provide an inductive bias toward learning step-by-step procedures for tasks that require compositional reasoning. The number of effective layers reached by looping determines the quality of the solution these models find. Like deep architectures, looped architectures are prone to a signal propagation problem induced by depth as the halting decision is postponed. In this paper, we address this signal propagation issue using pre-norm layers and residual scaling. Building on these architectural modifications, we propose FPRM, a Transformer-based Fixed-Point Reasoning Model that uses fixed-point convergence as an end-to-end halting mechanism in a looped architecture. We show that fixed-point halting allows FPRM to adapt its compute to task difficulty. FPRM is effective on common reasoning benchmarks, namely Sudoku, Maze, state-tracking, and ARC-AGI.

</details>


### [294] [EvolveNav: Proactive Preflection and Self-Evolving Memory for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18235)
*Qi Chai,Wenhao Shen,Nanjie Yao,Yue Xia,Kaiyong Zhao,Jie Ma,Guosheng Lin,Hao Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 为解决零样本导航中静态先验导致的低效与重复错误，本文提出了一种能够自主演进的导航框架，通过行动规则记忆与动作前预判机制，实现了测试时的持续提升与更高效率的导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的零样本物体目标导航（ZS-OGN）方法多依赖于静态先验知识，缺乏测试时的自我调整能力，导致智能体容易陷入重复错误并产生高昂的探索成本。

Method: 提出了一套自进化零样本导航框架：1. 构建基于行动规则的记忆库（Agentic rule memory），从过往轨迹中提取知识；2. 采用基于置信上限（UCB）的检索策略，在语义关联与历史成功率间平衡以选择最优规则；3. 引入记忆引导的“预判”（Preflection）模块，在执行动作前预测潜在后果，减少盲目探索。

Result: 实验表明，该方法在零样本基准测试中表现优于现有方法，成功率提高了10.1%，并显著减少了不必要的导航步数。

Conclusion: 该论文提出的自进化框架（Self-evolving framework）证明了通过从过去经验中提取行动规则并结合预判机制，能够显著提升智能体在零样本物体导航任务中的效率与成功率，为具备连续测试时自适应能力的导航系统提供了新范式。

Abstract: Zero-Shot Object-Goal Navigation (ZS-OGN) requires embodied agents to explore and locate target objects without any prior training. To this end, recent methods leverage foundation models. But they typically rely on static priors and lack adaptation, which leads to repeated errors and costly trial and error. In this paper, we propose a self-evolving ZS-OGN framework that enables continuous test-time improvement. Specifically, we build an agentic rule memory by extracting actionable knowledge from past trajectories. Then, we propose a retrieval strategy based on upper confidence bound, selecting effective rules by balancing semantic relevance and historical success. In addition, we introduce a memory-guided preflection module that forecasts potential outcomes before action, reducing inefficient exploration. Extensive experiments show that our method outperforms existing zero-shot baselines, achieving a 10.1\% improvement in success rate with fewer unnecessary steps.

</details>


<div id='cs.OS'></div>

# cs.OS [[Back]](#toc)

### [295] [Cordon: Semantic Transactions for Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17573)
*Zheng Chen,Hanqing Liu,Duling Xu,Dong Dong,Jialin Li,Bangzheng Pu,Jidong Zhai*

Main category: cs.OS

TL;DR: Cordon 是一个为 LLM 智能体设计的事务性运行时系统，通过引入任务级语义事务和“影子执行”机制，对多步骤工作流中的不可逆操作进行逻辑校验与受控提交，从而提升了智能体决策的安全性与可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 LLM 智能体运行环境将工具视为孤立的 RPC 调用，缺乏对多步骤任务工作流的整体（跨步骤）执行边界支持（如提交、回滚、恢复和审计），导致难以安全地处理具有不可逆后果的操作。

Method: Cordon 系统采用了“事务性运行时”架构，核心包括：1）语义事务（Semantic Transactions）：一种任务执行边界，绑定工具意图、结果世系和本地状态；2）影子状态（Shadow State）：用于执行可逆的中间突变；3）效应发件箱（Effect Outbox）：暂存外部效应；4）事务管理器：负责追踪衍生对象并进行最终的校验与提交。

Result: 在对抗性及良性工作流的评估中，Cordon 不仅能识别并拦截现有的单一调用防护机制所遗漏的跨步骤攻击，还能在保持任务完成率的同时，将因不可逆操作导致的失败率降低，且仅带来了可接受的延迟和人工确认成本。

Conclusion: Cordon 通过引入任务级的事务抽象，成功在 LLM 智能体运行环节中实现了对不可逆操作的有效控制。它能够捕捉传统单调用（RPC）防护机制无法察觉的跨步骤违规行为，显著降低了不可逆影响的失败率，为智能体任务执行提供了安全保障。

Abstract: Tool-using LLM agents are shifting the unit of computation from explicit human-issued commands to model-driven tasks with stateful consequences. Yet today's agent runtimes still expose tools as isolated RPCs. This interface gives runtimes a convenient integration point, but it lacks a task-scoped execution boundary for commit, rollback, recovery, and audit across multi-step agent workflows. We argue that this mismatch calls for a runtime containment boundary rather than another per-call guardrail. This paper introduces Cordon, a transactional runtime system for staging and validating irreversible agent effects before commit. A semantic transaction is a task-level execution boundary that binds tool intents and runtime-tracked result lineage to reversible local state, staged external effects, delegated authority, and audit metadata. Cordon implements this abstraction with a transaction manager that tracks derived result objects, executes reversible mutations in shadow state, stages outward-facing actions in an effect outbox, and records recovery metadata. The runtime then validates the composed execution flow before it commits state or releases external effects. Our evaluation across adversarial and benign workflows shows that Cordon exposes cross-step violations missed by existing defenses. It also reduces irreversible-effect failures while preserving benign task completion with modest approval and latency overhead.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [296] [Collaborative Large and Small Language Models for Accurate and Scalable Data Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17582)
*Qian Chen,Jianwei Wang,Wenjie Zhang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 针对现有数据修复方法在处理噪声上下文和模型不确定性时的不足，本文提出了 LasRepair++ 框架，通过 LLM 指导、EM 迭代优化上下文以及置信度加权机制，实现了更准确、高效的数据修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于 LLM 的数据修复方法存在两个主要问题：一是直接在低质量原始上下文中修复导致学习信号退化；二是盲目采纳模型输出可能引入错误。旨在结合 SLM 的高效性与 LLM 的能力，提升数据修复的准确性和鲁棒性。

Method: 提出 LasRepair 框架，利用 LLM 作为指令器选择全局上下文，SLM 作为修复器执行修复；在此基础上扩展出 LasRepair+（采用 EM 算法交替优化修复器参数与修复上下文）和 LasRepair++（引入列级校准的模型置信度进行加权，以减少不可靠修正的影响）。

Result: 理论上证明了 EM 流程与置信度加权机制的有效性；在真实数据集上的实验表明，LasRepair++ 的平均 F1 分数较最强基线提升了 18.1%。

Conclusion: LasRepair++ 通过融合大模型引导、EM算法迭代优化上下文以及置信度加权机制，有效提升了数据修复的质量与可靠性，实验证明其在实际数据集中显著优于现有基线方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of data repair, a key task in data cleaning that corrects erroneous entries in raw datasets to improve overall data quality. Although recent data-driven methods, especially those based on large language models (LLMs), achieve remarkable performance, we observe that: (i) they directly repair data in the raw and low-quality context, which may compromise learning signals, and (ii) they directly use uncertain model outputs as repairs, potentially introducing unreliable corrections and compromising repair quality. Motivated by the efficiency of small language models (SLMs) and the capabilities of LLMs, and aiming to address the above limitations, we propose LasRepair, a framework that collaborates Large and small language models for data repair. LasRepair employs an LLM as an instructor, which selects a global repair context to guide the SLM. The SLM acts as a corrector, using the selected context to repair erroneous data more efficiently. Moreover, to further improve context quality, we extend LasRepair to LasRepair+, which formulates data repair as an Expectation-Maximisation (EM) procedure that alternates between an E-step for updating the corrector parameters and an M-step for refining the repair context. Furthermore, to mitigate model uncertainty, we propose LasRepair++, which uses column-calibrated model confidence to down-weight unreliable repaired rows when updating the corrector, thereby enhancing repair quality. Theoretical analysis and empirical evaluation demonstrate the superiority of our methods. We theoretically prove the effectiveness of the EM-style procedure and the confidence-based weighting. Experiments on real-world datasets show that LasRepair++~ achieves an average F1-score improvement of 18.1% over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [297] [The Value of Adaptivity in LSM Bloom-Filter Tuning: A Log-Law and a Two-Clock Frontier](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18138)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 这是一项关于LSM树中布隆过滤器内存分配的研究。论文通过数学推导和实证分析指出，昂贵的动态实时内存自适应机制多属冗余，仅在合并（compaction）时进行调整即可满足绝大多数高性能需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的系统研究侧重于使LSM树中布隆过滤器的内存分配更加动态、自适应，以应对不断变化的工作负载热度。然而，这种复杂机制所带来的性能提升是否值得其系统复杂性消耗，一直缺乏深入的定量分析。作者旨在探究“何时需要自适应调节”这一核心问题。

Method: 1. 建立数学模型：推导了基于访问频率的最优比特数（log-law）、偏差的代价函数（robustness law）及适应度价值边界（adaptivity-value frontier）。
2. 实证验证：利用合成负载、Twitter生产缓存跟踪数据以及RocksDB引擎进行实验验证。
3. 理论分析：通过理论公式量化了适应性调整带来的潜在性能增益，并与静态或周期性调整策略进行了对比。

Result: 1. 证明了在编译阶段重新分配内存足以捕捉96-99%的动态追踪收益。
2. 发现工作负载对读取代价的影响呈对数关系，导致对热度估算的微小偏差对整体性能影响较小。
3. 给出了“由于偏态增加，精细化追踪不再必要”的预测，并在RocksDB实验中验证了布隆过滤器的性能稳定性。

Conclusion: 对于LSM树中的布隆过滤器内存分配策略，频繁进行动态调整的回报往往有限。在大多数工作负载下，仅在合并（compaction）阶段进行调整即可捕获几乎全部（96-99%）的性能收益，无需引入复杂的持续性监测机制。此外，论文提出了一个三阶段策略，并在极端偏态（skew）负载下揭示了过度精细化调整的效用会下降。

Abstract: Log-structured merge (LSM) trees attach an approximate-membership filter to every run and must split a fixed memory budget across them. The static optimum is known (Monkey); a large systems literature then makes the allocation adaptive, tracking shifting hotness online. We ask a prior question: when is that adaptivity worth its machinery? We give three analytical answers and validate them on synthetic sweeps, real Twitter production cache traces, and a real RocksDB engine. First, a log-law: optimal bits-per-key is affine in the logarithm of access frequency, at a fixed slope. Second, a robustness law: because the workload enters only logarithmically, the excess read cost from a hotness misestimate is half the size-weighted variance of the log error, and a common-factor misestimate is absorbed by the budget multiplier, so coarse estimates lose little. Third, an adaptivity-value frontier: since compaction rebuilds filters for free on its own clock, the value of continuous tracking over an allocation recomputed only at compaction grows quadratically in the within-epoch drift, with a closed-form scale. This yields a three-regime policy (coarse-at-compaction suffices, then track, then at extreme drift fall back to uniform) and predicts that more skew makes fine tracking matter less. On a real cluster, reallocating only at compaction captures 96-99% of tracking's benefit; on RocksDB the false-positive primitive holds within four percent to eight bits per key. The contribution is a characterization of when adaptive tuning pays; we add no new filter and no engine fork. Code and pre-registration are public.

</details>


### [298] [Group Commit Self-Clocks: Why Tuning Is Unnecessary Above a Device-Set Load Threshold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18187)
*Madhulatha Mandarapu,Sandeep Kunkunuru*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 通过闭环队列理论证明，对于OLTP数据库的日志刷新，复杂的定时器调优往往是冗余的；贪婪流水线策略不仅是参数无关的，而且能在绝大多数负载下实现最优性能，无需额外调优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的数据库日志刷新理论（基于开环系统）过度依赖于定时器或批处理大小的调优。该研究旨在质疑这种“手动调优”在实际闭环OLTP环境中的必要性，并从理论和实验上解释为何常用的“贪婪刷新策略”往往就是最优方案。

Method: 将事务日志提交建模为闭环排队网络。通过分析吞吐量与延迟的关系，推导出发射率阈值 $λ^\star$。在该阈值以上，理论上的“平方根法则”会自动退化为贪婪策略，证明了手动调优的无效性。研究者在AWS存储设备（EBS gp3 vs NVMe）上进行了实验，并在PostgreSQL中验证了无论调优参数如何，`commit_delay=0`（贪婪模式）均具有高度竞争力。

Result: 理论推导表明：在超过特定负载阈值 $λ^\star$（由设备性能决定）后，无论如何调优定时器，其效果都等同于贪婪流水线策略。实验验证显示，在不同性能的存储介质上，PostgreSQL的默认贪婪模式（commit_delay=0）在性能上均与任何经过精密调优的定时策略无明显差异（差距在0.1%以内）。

Conclusion: 在实际的闭环OLTP（在线事务处理）系统中，无需对日志刷新策略进行复杂的参数调优。贪婪流水线策略（即设备空闲即刷新）表现出完美的自适应性，其性能极其接近甚至等同于最优调优策略。因此，对于高性能日志记录，免调优的“贪婪”策略在实践中是最佳选择。

Abstract: Group commit amortizes the fixed cost of a durable log flush across many committing transactions; the release rule - a timer, a batch size, or an adaptive policy - is a classic tuning knob. The textbook theory is open-loop: for Poisson arrivals the optimal timer is the EOQ square-root rule, and the wait-or-flush decision is ski-rental 2-competitive. We ask when that tuning is worth its machinery, and show that in closed-loop OLTP it usually is not. Real commit arrivals are closed-loop: a client issues its next transaction only after its last commits, so the arrival rate is induced by the policy's own latency. Modeling this as a closed queueing network, the parameter-free greedy-pipelined policy (flush the instant the device is free) self-clocks to a computable fixed point and is within about 0.1% of the best oracle-tuned timer at every load. The square-root rule prescribes waiting $T^\star=\sqrt{2F_0/λ}$, but $T^\star<F_0$ exactly when $λ>λ^\star=2/F_0$; above this device-set load threshold the timer collapses onto greedy and tuning is vacuous. The clean theory only bites below $λ^\star$ and in the open-loop world, where a parameter-free ski policy still beats a fixed tuned timer under rate shifts. We instantiate $λ^\star$ with measured fsync distributions on two AWS storage classes (EBS gp3 versus instance NVMe, a $25\times$ range), and confirm on PostgreSQL that commit_delay=0 is competitive with any tuned value. The contribution is a characterization that explains deployed practice; we add no new logger.

</details>


### [299] [A benchmark suite of intracellular Boolean model variants and multiscale simulations for computational biology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18215)
*Marco Masera,Riccardo Smeriglio,Roberta Bardini,Alessandro Savino,Stefano Di Carlo*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: PhysiBench 是一个系统生物学基准资源，包含 612 个布尔调节网络变体与 12 万条多尺度随机仿真数据，旨在支持计算建模、数据推理与算法评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统生物学领域缺乏大规模、标准化的复杂多尺度模型基准资源，制约了计算方法（如仿真优化、数据驱动推理等）的表现评估与发展。

Method: 1. 基于7个已发表的布尔网络模型，运用变异构建、在线行为过滤及离线敏感性评估生成了612个可执行的变体模型；2. 使用 PhysiBoSS/PhysiCell 多尺度仿真框架，对60个代表性模型进行系统采样的动力学模拟，生成包含12万条时间分辨的多尺度随机仿真数据集；3. 实施文件完整性、执行力检查及拓扑结构与行为异质性分析进行技术验证。

Result: 构建了包含612个布尔网络变体及其对应的大规模（12万条）多尺度仿真数据集，支持多种计算任务，并验证了模型的结构多样性和行为异质性。

Conclusion: PhysiBench 为系统生物学领域提供了一个标准化的、大规模的基准测试资源，有助于推动多尺度建模、替代模型构建及仿真优化等计算方法的发展与评估。

Abstract: We present PhysiBench, an open resource for developing and evaluating computational methods in systems biology including a benchmark suite of 612 executable intracellular Boolean regulatory network variants and a dataset of 120,000 time-resolved multiscale stochastic simulations. The benchmark models are derived from seven published Boolean networks spanning cell-cycle control, developmental patterning, cancer signaling, immune response, and cell-fate decisions, and are executable in the PhysiBoSS/PhysiCell multiscale simulation framework. Model variants are generated through mutation-based model construction, online behavioral filtering, and offline sensitivity evaluation. The simulation dataset is produced from 60 selected models under systematically sampled stimulation protocols and fixed model-level initial configurations. Each trajectory is linked to its model identifier, input-parameter file, stochastic seed, and cell-level output file. PhysiBench supports direct simulation, surrogate modeling, data-driven inference, simulation-based optimization, and comparative benchmarking. Technical validation includes file-integrity and executability checks, graph-based structural diversity analyses, and behavioral heterogeneity assessment from multiscale simulation outputs.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [300] [On the Memorization Behavior of LLMs in Generative Recommendation: Observations, Implications, and Training Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17276)
*Sunwoo Kim,Sunkyung Lee,Clark Mingxuan Ju,Donald Loveland,Bhuvesh Kumar,Kijung Shin,Neil Shah,Liam Collins*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了生成式推荐中 LLM 存在过度依赖“一跳记忆”的问题，并提出了 IIRG 训练策略，通过强化多跳协同和语义关联的学习，显著提高了 LLM 的推荐泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于 LLM 的生成式推荐常表现出“一跳记忆”倾向（直接推荐训练数据中的后继项），这限制了其利用预训练知识进行复杂推理的能力。研究发现 LLM 的性能提升很大程度上依赖于这种记忆行为，而非复杂的推理。

Method: 提出了 IIRG（Item-Item Relation-aware Generation）训练策略：1. 捕捉跨多跳的协同关系（通过用户序列中共现的物品）；2. 捕捉物品间的语义关系（基于主题相似度）。通过这些额外的训练信号引导模型超越简单的“一跳记忆”。

Result: 实验表明，IIRG 显著优于仅使用标准“下一项预测”训练的 LLM，特别是在测试物品未出现在训练一跳路径中的用户案例上，性能提升更为显著。

Conclusion: 提出了一种名为 IIRG 的训练策略，通过在训练中显式学习协同关系和语义关系，有效缓解了 LLM 在生成式推荐中的过拟合（一跳记忆）问题，显著提升了模型在非直接关联测试案例上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Generative recommendation (GR) has emerged as a promising direction for recommender systems. Recently, large language models (LLMs) have been increasingly adopted for GR, as their rich pretrained knowledge is expected to help them generalize beyond common user behavior patterns that traditional memorization-oriented baselines can capture. However, existing LLM-based GR works largely ignore LLMs' well-known tendency to memorize, which, if present in LLMs fine-tuned for GR, would restrict their utilization of pretrained knowledge. In this work, we investigate this concern by examining one-hop memorization, where a model recommends items that are direct successors of items in the training data. We show that LLMs do this more than non-LLM-based GR models-in fact, the vast majority of their gains over GR baselines are actually on users whose target items can be predicted through one-hop memorization. We intuit that improving performance on the remaining users requires LLMs to learn richer item-item relations beyond one-hop transitions. To achieve this, we propose IIRG, a novel training strategy that teaches LLMs to capture: (1) collaborative relations derived from item co-occurrences across multiple hops in user sequences, and (2) semantic relations among items with similar themes, both of which can serve as useful recommendation signals. We show that IIRG significantly improves over LLMs trained solely with standard next-item prediction, with especially large gains for users whose test items are not covered by train-time one-hop transitions.

</details>


### [301] [RSRank: Learning Relevance from Representational Shifts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17468)
*Archit Gupta,Sai Sundaresan,Debabrata Mahapatra*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文旨在解决RAG重排中依赖启发式阈值及评分指标不准确的问题，通过引入“代表性偏移（RS）”作为衡量文档相关性的新信号，并设计相应的学习框架，实现了更精准的重排序和自动化文档过滤。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG系统中的重排序组件存在两类局限：一是依赖启发式阈值来过滤文档，缺乏普适性；二是现有的基于模型Logit（通常用于预测下一个token）的相关性得分方法并非专为评估文档相关性设计，准确度有限。

Method: 提出了一种利用“代表性偏移（Representational Shift, RS）”作为相关性度量指标的方法。具体而言，通过计算候选文档引发的查询内部状态变化与预定义的Oracle文档集引发的状态变化之间的对齐程度，学习将该RS映射为校准后的相关性评分，并训练模型实现零阈值过滤。

Result: 在多个检索数据集上的实验结果表明，该方法在性能上优于当前最先进（SOTA）的重排序器，能够在无需人工干预阈值的情况下有效过滤无关文档。

Conclusion: 本文提出的基于代表性偏移（RS）的重排序方法通过利用模型内部状态变化的对齐度，有效提升了相关性评估的准确性。该方法不仅实现了更鲁棒的相关性打分，还通过零阈值过滤机制消除了对启发式参数调节的依赖，为RAG系统提供了一种高效且可扩展的重排序方案。

Abstract: As enterprises deploy RAG-based systems to provide grounded responses to user queries, reranking has become a critical component for the final filtering step that separates relevant from distracting or irrelevant documents. Existing rerankers often rely on heuristic thresholds to achieve optimal filtering. Moreover, for relevance scoring, state-of-the-art methods use a language model's logit signals, which are designed for next-token prediction, not for assessing relevance. To address these limitations, we identify a principled signal for relevance: the representational shift (RS) induced in a query's internal state when conditioned on a document. We observe that the alignment between (a) RS induced by a candidate document and (b) RS induced by an oracle document-set provides a robust indicator of relevance. Building on this insight, we introduce a lightweight training framework that learns projections mapping RS to calibrated relevance scores. Our training objectives naturally filter irrelevant content at a zero threshold, reducing dependence on heuristic tuning. Across diverse retrieval datasets, our method delivers gains over SOTA rerankers.

</details>


### [302] [Temporal Preference Optimization for Unsupervised Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17664)
*HyunJin Kim,Jaejun Shim,Young Jin Kim,JinYeong Bak*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: TPOUR 是一种新型无监督时间感知密集检索模型，通过创新的时间偏好优化（TRPO）和时间嵌入插值，解决了无监督检索中缺乏时间相关性的问题，在极小参数下显著提升了时间信息检索的准确度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督密集检索器（Dense Retrievers）虽具备扩展性，但无法处理时间相关性，容易检索到语义相关但时间维度不匹配的过期文档。且现有监督方法依赖大量显式时间标注，限制了其应用场景。

Method: 提出 TPOUR (Temporal Preference Optimization for Unsupervised Retriever) 框架，核心是 Temporal Retrieval Preference Optimization (TRPO) 方法。TRPO 在时间维度上重新解释偏好学习，引导检索器倾向于时间对齐的文档；同时结合时间嵌入插值技术，使模型在未见的时间段内也能保持检索的连续性和准确性。

Result: 在时间信息检索（T-IR）任务中，TPOUR 表现优于现有的无监督和监督基准。与 Qwen-Embedding-8B 相比，尽管参数量减少了约 72.7 倍，但在 nDCG@5 指标上，显式查询提升了 +4.04% (+12.15%)，隐式查询提升了 +4.98% (+15.21%)。

Conclusion: TPOUR 成功地将偏好优化引入无监督时间信息检索，通过时间嵌入插值实现了泛化性，在显式和隐式时间查询上均超越了包括大型监督模型在内的基准，证明了该方法在轻量化模型上的高效性。

Abstract: Unsupervised dense retrievers offer scalability by learning semantic similarity from unlabeled documents via contrastive learning, but they struggle to capture the temporal relevance, retrieving semantically related but temporally misaligned documents-an important aspect when a document collection spans multiple time periods (e.g., retrieving documents from 2018-2025 for "Who is the president in 2019?" introduces temporal ambiguity). Existing methods rely on supervised training with explicit timestamps, which are not always feasible. We propose TPOUR (Temporal Preference Optimization for Unsupervised Retriever), which uses our novel training method Temporal Retrieval Preference Optimization (TRPO). TRPO reinterprets preference learning in the temporal dimension, guiding the retriever to favor temporally aligned documents. TPOUR further generalizes to unseen time periods via interpolation in a learned time embedding, enabling continuous temporal alignment. Experiments on temporal information retrieval (T-IR), TPOUR outperforms both unsupervised and supervised baselines. Compared to Qwen-Embedding-8B, despite being about 72.7x smaller, TPOUR Contriever improves average nDCG@5 by +4.04 (+12.15%) on explicit and +4.98 (+15.21%) on implicit queries. We provide our code at https://github.com/agwaBom/TPOUR.

</details>


### [303] [Do Generative Recommenders Deepen the Information Cocoon? A Closed-Loop Simulation with LLM-powered User Simulators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17707)
*Jiyuan Yang,Gengxin Sun,Mengqi Zhang,Lingjie Wang,Yuanzi Li,Hongxi Cui,Xin Xin,Pengjie Ren*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该研究通过模拟实验发现生成式推荐系统比传统推荐更能打破信息茧房，但通过SID生成的内部空间仍存在茧房隐患，其严重程度取决于Tokenization策略和模型规模。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多关注传统序列推荐导致的信息茧房问题，不清楚基于语义ID（SID）的生成式推荐系统是否会强化这一现象，机制尚不明确。

Method: 提出了RecLoop框架，利用LLM驱动的用户代理进行闭环仿真评估，并引入了“Code-Space Structural Cocoon”指标度量生成SID空间的浓度，对比了生成式推荐与传统序列推荐在多轮反馈下的表现。

Result: 生成式推荐系统在曝光层面更不容易形成茧房；但在模型内部空间（Code-Space）仍会随反馈产生浓度积累；协作信号驱动的Tokenization比语义Tokenization更容易产生茧房效应，而更大的模型规模有助于维持多样性。

Conclusion: 生成式推荐系统在缓解信息茧房方面优于传统序列模型，能保持更好的曝光多样性并减缓用户同质化；但其在大模型内部（Code-Space）仍存在茧房风险，且受Tokenization策略（协作信号vs语义）和模型规模影响显著。

Abstract: Recommender systems alleviate information overload, yet repeated feedback between recommendations and user interactions can reinforce existing preferences and narrow users' exposure, forming information cocoons. While this phenomenon has been widely studied in traditional sequential recommendation, its impact on generative recommendation remains unclear. By replacing atomic item IDs with Semantic ID (SID) sequences, generative recommenders introduce a different recommendation mechanism whose role in information cocoon formation is not yet understood. To investigate whether generative recommenders deepen information cocoons, we propose \textsc{RecLoop}, a closed-loop simulation framework with LLM-driven user agents. We compare two generative recommenders and two traditional sequential baselines on two Amazon datasets across multiple feedback cycles. In addition to standard exposure-level metrics, we introduce \emph{Code-Space Structural Cocoon}, a model-level metric that measures concentration in the generated SID space. Experimental results show that generative recommenders are generally less prone to exposure-level cocoon formation than traditional baselines, preserving broader exposure diversity and slowing cross-user homogenization. However, feedback loops can still induce concentration within the generated SID space. We further find that cocoon severity depends strongly on tokenization strategy and model scale: collaborative-signal tokenization produces stronger cocoon effects than semantic tokenization, whereas larger models maintain greater code-space diversity and better retain access to niche content. These findings suggest that information cocoons in generative recommendation are shaped not only by recommendation behavior, but also by item tokenization and model capacity. Our code is available at https://github.com/Dregen-Yor/RecLoop.

</details>


### [304] [Understanding and Debugging Failures in N-Gram-Based Generative Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17721)
*Richard Takacs,Adrian Bracher,Svitlana Vakulenko*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文研究了生成式检索（GR）的失效模式，提出了分类体系、实证揭示了现有ngram类模型在标识符生成与排序上的缺陷，并提供了一款分析工具以提升检索过程的可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式检索（GR）作为一种新兴的信息检索范式，虽然具备独特性优势，但也引入了独特的失效机制；当前缺乏对这些失效模式的系统性理解与诊断方法。

Method: 1. 文献综述：构建了生成式检索（GR）的失效模式分类法（Taxonomy）；2. 实验分析：针对ngram类GR方法（SEAL及MINDER）进行实证研究；3. 工具开发：构建了一个可视化Web工具，用于分析生成ngram对最终排序的影响。

Result: 研究发现ngram类GR模型存在以下关键问题：1. 文档ID（docids）存在歧义性；2. 标识符的多样性低下；3. 特定标识符对检索结果产生不成比例的影响（偏见或过度权重）。

Conclusion: 通过定义生成式检索（GR）的失效模式分类法、揭示ngram类模型（如SEAL, MINDER）存在的固有缺陷，并提供辅助分析工具，本文为GR系统的改进提供了诊断框架，强调了在生成式检索范式中理解和缓解失效模式的重要性。

Abstract: Generative Retrieval (GR) is an emerging Information Retrieval (IR) paradigm that is motivated by increasingly capable language models. In GR, a model directly generates identifiers for relevant documents. While these systems offer unique advantages, they also introduce distinct failure mechanisms. We explore these failure modes in three contributions: (1) We present a taxonomy of GR failure modes based on GR literature. (2) We empirically investigate failure in a subset of GR: ngram-based methods, more specifically, SEAL and MINDER. Our analysis reveals common issues, such as ambiguous docids, low identifier diversity, and the disproportionate impact of specific identifiers. (3) We introduce a new web-based tool that helps the IR community analyze generated ngrams and their respective contribution to the final ranking, providing an intuitive interface to identify where such GR methods go wrong.

</details>


### [305] [Non-negative Elastic Net Decoding for Information Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.17910)
*Koki Okajima,Yasutoshi Ida,Tsukasa Yoshida,Yasuaki Nakamura*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文针对密集检索中结果冗余的问题，提出了一种名为 NNN 解码的新方法，通过将文档集作为整体进行联合优化，实现了检索多样性和准确性的显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的密集检索（基于内积）由于独立评估每个文档，忽略了语料库整体上下文，导致检索结果缺乏多样性且存在冗余。

Method: 提出“非负弹性网络（NNN）解码”方法，将检索视为联合解码问题，通过选择能够稀疏、非负线性重构查询嵌入的文档集来进行检索；并引入了针对 NNN 解码的端到端嵌入训练流程。

Result: 理论上证明了 NNN 解码涵盖了所有传统密集检索能解决的查询，且在处理含冗余文档的语料库时具有更强性能。实验表明，在冻结参数和端到端训练场景下，NNN 解码均在多个基准测试中超越了标准密集检索。

Conclusion: NNN 解码通过联合考虑语料库上下文，克服了传统密集检索中检索结果冗余的问题，为利用密集嵌入提供了一种优于内积评分的新范式。

Abstract: Dense retrieval has become the dominant paradigm in information retrieval, in which each document is scored against a query by the inner product of their vector embeddings, and the top-$k$ documents by score are retrieved for this query. However, since each document's score depends solely on the embedding of the query and itself, the retrieval process is oblivious to the content of the entire corpus. Therefore, dense retrieval cannot avoid selecting semantically similar documents from the corpus, which may result in a non-diverse, redundant set of retrieved documents. To this end, we approach retrieval as a joint decoding problem, in which documents are selected as a set with regard to the context of the rest of the corpus. To achieve this, we propose Non-Negative elastic Net (NNN) decoding, which selects documents whose embeddings jointly reconstruct the query embedding as a sparse non-negative linear combination.
  Our main theoretical result establishes a strict separation between dense retrieval and NNN decoding. For any corpus, every query correctly handled by dense retrieval is also handled by NNN decoding, while on corpora containing correlated documents, NNN decoding additionally handles queries that dense retrieval cannot. Experimental results indicate that applying NNN decoding to frozen embeddings trained for inner-product scoring yields consistent improvements across several benchmarks. Moreover, we introduce an end-to-end training procedure which optimizes the embeddings for NNN decoding, producing significant performance gains surpassing in all metrics and benchmarks compared to dense retrieval. Our work establishes a new paradigm for leveraging dense embeddings in information retrieval, beyond the standard practice of inner-product scoring.

</details>


### [306] [IUU+DB: Tracking Illegal, Unreported, and Unregulated Fishing, Seafood Fraud, and Labor Abuse through LLM-driven Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.18181)
*Henry Bodwell,Hong Yang,John C. Simeone,Kelvin Gorospe,Bella Sullivan,Lana Huang,Jessica Gephart,Sandy Aylesworth,Molly Masterton,Naren Ramakrishnan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出了“IUU+”概念以涵盖更广泛的渔业相关违法行为，并开发了基于LLM的IUU+DB系统，通过自动化处理非结构化文档，构建全球非法捕捞事件数据库，从而改善对渔业犯罪的监测、分析与执法效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的IUU（非法、不报告和不管制）捕捞定义过于狭窄，难以涵盖环境与供应链相关的犯罪行为。此外，由于缺乏量化数据，难以准确评估IUU+活动的频率、地理分布、涉及物种及模式，导致打击和监管困难。

Method: 提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的系统——IUU+DB。该系统通过 ingestion（摄取）异构文档，执行文本分类以识别相关事件，并进行关键信息提取（如行为主体、地点、物种、船只、违规行为及执法结果），同时支持数据去重与趋势分析。

Result: 案例研究和验证结果表明，IUU+DB能够成功整合分散的证据，识别地理和行为热点，为学术研究、行业风险评估以及政府的政策执行和针对性执法提供支持。

Conclusion: IUU+DB系统通过利用大语言模型从非结构化文献中提取和整合信息，能够有效地处理碎片化的非法捕捞数据，为学术界、政府及行业在打击IUU+活动、保护海洋生态及供应链风险管理方面提供强有力的数据支持和决策依据。

Abstract: Illegal, unreported, and unregulated fishing (IUU) traditionally refers to fishing activities that violate applicable laws or occur in areas that lack applicable laws. We propose the term IUU+ to capture a broader suite of fisheries sector environmental and associated supply chain trade-related crimes and behaviors. Although IUU+ activity is widely recognized as a serious threat to marine ecosystems, markets, and livelihoods, a quantitative understanding of these incidents, e.g., their frequency, geography, species, actors, and patterns in the type of illicit activity, remains difficult to obtain. We propose IUU+DB, a large language model driven system for building a global incident database of IUU+ activity. The system ingests heterogeneous documents, classifies whether they describe relevant incidents, extracts key data elements such as actors, locations, species, vessels, violations, and enforcement outcomes, and supports deduplication and trend analysis. Case studies and validation results show that IUU+DB can help organize fragmented evidence, surface geographic and behavioral hotspots, support fisheries-domain specific research in academia and non-government organizations, assist source and species risk assessments for industry, and provide support for policy implementation and targeted enforcement efforts to government agencies.

</details>
